TL;DR: 本文研究了开放多体系统中自持续振荡的两种动力学组织机制：有限振幅背景吸引子的选择与其中性相位的控制。作者基于分岔理论，分析了驱动-耗散集体自旋系统中不同的微观耗散结构如何决定背景吸引子类型及对U(1)对称性破缺的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开放量子多体系统中自持续振荡的微观起源机制，特别是耗散结构如何决定系统的振荡行为与对称性破缺响应。

Method: 采用热力学极限下的平均场动力学分析与分岔理论。对比不同类型的耗散算符：线性U(1)协变跳跃算符、非线性协变耗散、以及线性U(1)破缺跳跃算符，分析其对固定点、极限环及Hopf分岔的影响。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 线性U(1)协变跳跃只能产生极向固定点背景；(2) 非线性协变耗散可通过超临界Hopf分岔稳定有限纬度自持续振荡流形；(3) 相干U(1)破缺下精确共振导致可逆双零退化而非标准Hopf；(4) 有限失谐将奇异点展开为真实有限频率Hopf边界，可超临界或亚临界；(5) 单个线性耗散U(1)破缺跳跃无法产生标准Hopf不稳定性。

Conclusion: 建立了普适设计原则：非线性协变耗散负责选择自持续振荡背景，而对称性破缺通道的结构决定局部响应是双零型、真正Hopf型还是非Hopf型。

Abstract: Self-sustained oscillations in open many-body systems can be organized by two distinct dynamical ingredients: the selection of a finite-amplitude background attractor and the subsequent control of its neutral phase. We develop this bifurcation-based picture for driven-dissipative collective spins and show how the microscopic dissipative structure determines the available background and its response to explicit U(1) breaking. In the thermodynamic-limit mean-field dynamics, a single linear U(1)-covariant jump produces only polar fixed-point backgrounds, whereas nonlinear covariant dissipation provides amplitude-dependent saturation and stabilizes a finite-latitude self-sustained-oscillator manifold through a supercritical Hopf bifurcation. Under coherent U(1) breaking, exact resonance leads to a reversible double-zero degeneracy with vanishing critical frequency rather than a standard Hopf onset. Finite detuning unfolds this singularity into a genuine finite-frequency Hopf boundary, which exists only on the self- sustained-oscillator side and can be either supercritical or subcritical. By contrast, a single linear dissipative U(1)-breaking jump cannot generate a standard Hopf instability: when its phase-pinning invariant vanishes the azimuthal direction remains neutral, whereas otherwise the phase-locked fixed points have a purely real Jacobian spectrum. These results establish a general design principle: nonlinear covariant dissipation selects the self-sustained background, while the structure of the symmetry-breaking channel determines whether the resulting local response is double-zero, genuinely Hopf, or non-Hopf.

</details>


### [103] [An interpretation-independent formulation of the measurement problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03409)
*Antoine Soulas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文不试图解决测量问题，而是基于理论无关的方法论（类似贝尔定理），将测量问题重新表述为一种与解释无关的经验统计特性：量子物质无法用柯尔莫哥洛夫概率论描述，因此量子系统的本体论无法独立于认识论而构建——不存在对量子系统的"上帝视角"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量问题缺乏精确且被广泛接受的定义，原因在于它在不同量子力学诠释下呈现出不同面貌。作者受贝尔定理等理论无关结果的启发，希望以诠释无关的方式找到测量问题的共同根源，仅从经验统计出发进行推导。

Method: 作者指出量子物质不能被柯尔莫哥洛夫概率理论所描述，并论证柯尔莫哥洛夫概率公理是"认识论不确定性的公设"。由此将测量问题重新表述为：无法为量子物质构建独立于认识论的本体论（即不存在对量子系统的上帝视角）。

Result: 这种重新表述本身没有传统意义上的"解"，因为它是宇宙统计特征的一种固有属性。但它带来附带收益：(i) 对各种诠释提供新视角；(ii) 从根本层面而非纯数学技术层面解释为何量子力学与广义相对论如此不相容；(iii) 为量子引力研究提供指引。

Conclusion: 重新表述后的测量问题虽然不可解，但作为理论框架具有启发价值，能帮助厘清诠释多样性、理解量子力学与广义相对论冲突的根源，并指引量子引力的探索方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we do not try to solve the measurement problem, but rather to properly formulate it. One of the reasons why it still lacks a precise, agreed definition is that the problem may take very different aspects depending on the interpretation of quantum mechanics embraced. Inspired by the methodology of theory-independent results like Bell's theorem, we propose to identify the common root of the puzzle in an interpretation-independent way i.e. as a property of the empirical statistics only, before deriving its philosophical consequences. The key observation is that quantum matter can not be described by a Kolmogorovian probabilistic theory. Arguing that the Kolmogorov axioms of probability theory are the postulates of epistemic uncertainty leads us to reformulate the measurement problem as the impossibility to build an ontology independent of epistemology for quantum matter. Said differently, there exists no God's-eye view on quantum systems. Although it is meaningless to solve the measurement problem as defined in this way, insofar as it is a feature of the universe's statistics, such a formulation may on the other hand bring side benefits. In particular, we argue that it allows to: (i) shed a new light on the variety of interpretations; (ii) propose a fundamental reason why quantum mechanics and general relativity are so incompatible, not relying on purely mathematical or technical arguments; (iii) guide the quest for quantum gravity.

</details>


### [104] [In defence of the Ehrenfest mean-field molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03419)
*Vladimir U. Nazarov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了电子-原子核耦合运动问题，证明了在原子核经典极限下，所谓的"平均场"Ehrenfest图像是精确理论而非近似，并与精确分解（Exact Factorization）方法完全等价，从而恢复了平均场Ehrenfest分子动力学作为简单、有效且形式上精确的方案在含时密度泛函理论框架中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为平均场Ehrenfest分子动力学是对电子-核耦合运动的近似处理，而Abedi等人提出的精确分解方法（Exact Factorization）被认为是更严谨的形式理论。作者希望重新审视Ehrenfest图像的理论地位，澄清其与精确分解方法的关系。

Method: 作者通过严格的数学证明，在原子核经典极限条件下，建立了平均场Ehrenfest图像与精确分解方法之间的完全等价性，从而证明Ehrenfest图像在该极限下是精确理论。

Result: 证明了在原子核的经典极限下，平均场Ehrenfest图像与更为复杂的精确分解方法完全等价，即Ehrenfest图像在经典极限下不是近似而是精确理论。

Conclusion: 该结论为平均场Ehrenfest分子动力学"平反"，使其可作为一种简单、有效且形式上精确的方案，适用于含时密度泛函理论框架下的电子-核耦合动力学模拟。

Abstract: Re-visiting the problem of the coupled electronic--nuclear motion, we prove that the so called `mean-field' Ehrenfest picture is an exact theory, rather than an approximation, in the classical limit for nuclei. By establishing its full equivalence to the much more involved Exact Factorization approach to the problem (Abedi {\it et al.}, Europhys. Lett. {\bf 106}, 33001 (2014)), our conclusion rehabilitates the mean-field Ehrenfest molecular dynamics as a simple, effective, while formally exact scheme, amenable to the use within the framework of the time-dependent density functional theory.

</details>


### [105] [Tunable topological enhancement of covariant quantum Fisher information via non-Bloch skin effect in non-Hermitian SSH lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03421)
*Qi-Cheng Wu,Yan-Hui Zhou,Tong Liu,Dong-Xu Chen,Chui-Ping Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将协变量子Fisher信息（CQFI）形式从单模系统推广到多模非厄米Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）晶格，揭示了由非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE）驱动的可调拓扑增强机制。在开放边界条件下，NHSE将传统布里渊区形变为半径为r=exp(κ)的广义布里渊区，使增强因子E(N)=F_OBC/F_PBC在N~30时可超过30，联合Cramér-Rao界可降低多达15个数量级，为量子计量学提供了空间域的新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量子Fisher信息已被确立为伪厄米传感器的终极精度基准，但现有分析局限于单模系统。为了将该形式推广到更实际的物理场景，需要探索多模非厄米晶格系统中CQFI的行为及其潜在增强机制，从而为量子计量学开辟新的空间域途径。

Method: 将CQFI形式从单模系统扩展到多模非厄米SSH晶格；利用非厄米趋肤效应在开放边界条件下将传统布里渊区形变为广义布里渊区（半径r=exp(κ)，κ为非Bloch衰减率）；系统分析总CQFI随系统尺寸N的线性标度行为，并计算其前缀因子的κ依赖性；对增强因子E(N)=F_OBC/F_PBC进行量化，并评估其对局部无序的鲁棒性及多参数估计能力。

Result: 总CQFI随系统尺寸N线性增长，但前缀因子关键依赖于非Bloch衰减率κ；增强因子E(N)在N≈30时超过30，显著优于周期边界传感器性能；该增强对中等程度的局部无序具有鲁棒性；在多参数估计中，联合Cramér-Rao界可降低多达15个数量级。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了空间域量子计量的新机制，与已有的时间域策略形成互补。该机制可通过拓扑电路、光子晶格和超导电路等现有实验平台实现，为基于非厄米拓扑系统的高灵敏度传感提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The covariant quantum Fisher information (CQFI) has recently been established as the ultimate precision benchmark for pseudo-Hermitian sensors [Phys. Rev. Lett. 136, 080802 (2026)], yet existing analyses are limited to single-mode systems. Here we extend the CQFI formalism to multi-mode non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger lattices and reveal tunable topological enhancement enabled by the non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE). Under open boundary conditions, the NHSE deforms the conventional Brillouin zone into a generalized Brillouin zone of radius r = exp(\k{appa}), where \k{appa} denotes the non-Bloch decay rate. While the total CQFI scales linearly with system size N, its prefactor depends critically on \k{appa}, yielding an enhancement factor E(N) = F_OBC / F_PBC that exceeds 30 for N ~ 30, substantially outperforming periodic-boundary sensors. The enhancement is robust against moderate local disorder and supports multi-parameter estimation, with the joint Cramér-Rao bound reduced by up to 15 orders of magnitude. These findings establish a spatial-domain mechanism for quantum metrology that complements time-domain strategies and is experimentally accessible using topoelectrical circuits, photonic lattices, and superconducting circuits with current technology.

</details>


### [106] [Convolution absorbing boundaries for explicit-circuit quantum simulation of the wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03440)
*Hoang Anh Nguyen,Ali Tura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Schrodingerisation方法首次将卷积型吸收边界条件（复频移完美匹配层）纳入显式量子电路模拟波方程，克服了传统哈密顿模拟只能处理封闭域的局限。作者发现该非酉动力学的记忆形式存在不可约的不定厄米部分，导致标准后选择恢复成本随时间指数增长；并提出一种经典预计算的Lyapunov对称化方法，将变换后的生成元转为耗散型，用时间无关的条件化因子（数百量级）替代时间相关的惩罚，在后期交叉点上将后选择成本降低4至26个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟波方程的显式电路仅适用于封闭域，外向波在计算区域边缘反射返回，无法真实模拟开放或无界区域的物理现象。为提升量子模拟的实用性和物理真实性，需要引入吸收边界条件（ABC）来处理出射波。

Method: 采用复频移完美匹配层（C-PML）结合指数核记忆变量构建卷积型吸收边界；利用Schrodingerisation将非酉线性动力学嵌入酉演化的高阶维空间；扩展Bell基项演化电路以处理投影子值算子串，并使用二阶Trotter化构造显式门级电路；提出Lyapunov对称化方法（经典预计算）将生成元变换为耗散形式。

Result: (1) 在17至21个量子比特上端到端运行，结果与精确参考值的误差在0.1%到1%之间；(2) 记忆形式吸收生成元的不可约不定厄米部分，其最大特征值随吸收强度/网格间距的平方根增长，且无法通过记忆场对角重缩放消除，导致标准后选择代价随模拟时间指数增长；(3) Lyapunov对称化将时间相关惩罚替换为数百量级的时间无关条件因子，在网格多达4000个未知量上测量并随网格细化饱和；交叉后对称化恢复成本降低4至26个数量级，是最长模拟时域下唯一可行的恢复方案。

Conclusion: 显式量子电路可实现含卷积型吸收边界的波方程模拟，但标准Schrodingerisation后选择的指数代价构成结构性障碍；基于Lyapunov的经典预计算对称化提供了一种实用的补救方案，使大规模长时间模拟成为可能。

Abstract: Explicit quantum circuits for the wave equation, built by Hamiltonian simulation, are restricted to closed domains, in which outgoing waves reflect off the edge of the computational region and return. We lift that restriction with absorbing boundaries of the convolution type - a complex-frequency-shifted perfectly matched layer realised through exponential-kernel memory variables - and make the resulting non-unitary dynamics quantum-implementable by Schrodingerisation. We obtain three results. First, explicit gate-level circuits for the absorbing evolution, obtained by extending a Bell- basis term-evolution circuit to projector-valued operator strings and Trotterising to second order; these run end to end on seventeen to twenty-one qubits and agree with exact references to within a tenth of a percent to a percent. Second, a structural obstruction: the memory form of the absorbing generator carries an irreducibly indefinite Hermitian part, whose largest eigenvalue grows as the square root of the absorption strength divided by the grid spacing and survives any diagonal rescaling of the memory fields. The known recovery threshold for Schrodingerisation then makes the post-selection cost grow exponentially in the simulated time. Third, a remedy: a Lyapunov symmetrizer, precomputed classically, renders the transformed generator dissipative and replaces that time-dependent penalty with a time-independent conditioning factor of several hundred, measured on grids of up to four thousand unknowns and saturating under mesh refinement. The crossover is early: past it the symmetrized recovery is cheaper by four to twenty-six orders of magnitude in post-selection cost, and at the longest horizons tested it is the only recovery that works.

</details>


### [107] [Weakly Driven and Finite Detuning Boundary Time Crystals Enabled by Low-Dissipation Dynamical Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03491)
*Xiang Guo,Xiaojun Zhang,Zhihai Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了驱动-耗散系统中的边界时间晶体（BTCs），发现通过将两个原子系综耦合到公共马尔可夫库，共享耗散可将无耗散和低耗散模式组织为动态可访问的低耗散通道，从而在弱驱动和有限失谐条件下实现BTCs，且有限失谐会从初始态依赖的振荡轨迹族中选出唯一的稳定极限环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统边界时间晶体通常需要强驱动和精确的原予-驱动器共振条件，这对其物理实现施加了严格限制，因此需要在更宽松的参数条件下实现鲁棒的BTCs。

Method: 考虑两个原子系综耦合到一个公共马尔可夫库，利用共享耗散将无耗散与低耗散模式组织成动态可访问的低耗散通道，以实现弱驱动和有限失谐条件下的时间晶体行为。

Result: 共享耗散机制使得在弱驱动和有限失谐条件下可以产生BTCs；有限失谐能够从初始态依赖的振荡轨迹族中选出唯一稳定的极限环。

Conclusion: 低耗散动力学通道为在放宽驱动和共振条件的情况下实现鲁棒边界时间晶体提供了一种新路径。

Abstract: Spontaneous breaking of continuous time-translation symmetry in driven-dissipative systems gives rise to boundary time crystals (BTCs), characterized by persistent oscillations sustained by coherent driving and collective dissipation. Conventional BTCs, however, typically require strong driving and exact atom-drive resonance, imposing stringent constraints on their realization. Here we consider two atomic ensembles coupled to a common Markovian reservoir and show that shared dissipation organizes dissipation-free and low-dissipation modes into dynamically accessible low-dissipation channels, enabling BTCs under weak driving and finite detuning. Finite detuning further selects a unique stable limit cycle from an initial-state-dependent family of oscillatory trajectories. Our results establish low-dissipation dynamical channels as a route to robust BTCs under relaxed driving and resonance conditions.

</details>


### [108] [Topology-Dependent Enhancement of Entanglement Extraction in Repeater Graph States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03496)
*Poramat Chianvichai,Poramet Pathumsoot,Naphan Benchasattabuse,Michal Hajdušek,Rodney Van Meter,Sujin Suwanna*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子中继器图态（RGS）架构中从完整二分图态中提取贝尔对的最大数量问题，通过模拟发现贝尔对产量取决于连接拓扑，在低至中等边密度时产量最大，且随着网络跳数增加需要更高的内部量子比特连接性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子中继器依赖物理量子存储器，存在退相干和实际实现困难。RGS架构虽为无存储器的替代方案且对光子损耗具有天然鲁棒性，但其实现面临高效图态生成器和复杂量子比特测量的挑战，尤其是RGS瓶颈问题——只能从完整二分图态中提取单个贝尔对，限制了其性能。

Method: 针对RGS结构，研究通过量子比特连接从图中提取最大数量贝尔对的策略，并通过仿真模拟观察贝尔对提取产量与图连接拓扑（如边密度、网络跳数）之间的关系。

Result: 模拟结果表明：贝尔对最大提取数量取决于连接拓扑，在低至中等边密度下贝尔对产量趋于最大；随着网络跳数增加，RGS需要更高的内量子比特连接性以维持足够的可提取贝尔对产量。

Conclusion: RGS架构的资源需求预期将转向使用具有更高内部量子比特连接性的RGS，以解决瓶颈问题并支持多跳的远距离量子通信。

Abstract: Quantum repeaters are essential for establishing long-distance quantum communication to overcome the exponential decay of entanglement due to photon loss. Traditional repeater architectures rely on physical quantum memory, which introduces decoherence and poses significant practical implementation challenges. The repeater graph state (RGS) architecture offers a promising memory-less alternative that is inherently resilient to photon losses. A key challenge in implementing RGS lies in the requirement for highly efficient graph state generators and complex qubit measurement. In this work, we aim to investigate the strategies for extracting the maximum number of Bell pairs from the RGS structure via its qubit connection to resolve the well-known bottleneck problem of RGS in which only a single Bell pair can be extracted from a complete bipartite graph state. From simulations, we observe that the maximum number of extracted Bell pairs depends on its connection topology, where the Bell-pair yield tends to be maximal at low to moderate edge densities. As the number of network hops increases, the RGS must be equipped with higher inner-qubit connectivity to maintain a sufficient yield of extractable Bell pairs. Thus, the expected resource requirement shifts toward the use of RGSs with higher inner-qubit connectivity.

</details>


### [109] [Heralded one-, two- and three-photon states from waveguided parametric down-conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03542)
*Borrero Landazabal Daniel,Laiho Kaisa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在电信波长范围内通过周期性极化KTiOPO₄波导中的参量下转换制备交叉偏振孪生光束，并宣告出最多三光子的光子数态。作者利用单次点击概率提取态的平均光子数并采样矩生成函数值，同时测量了宣告n光子态的高阶归一化阶乘矩g^(m)（至m=n+1阶），通过违反g^(m+1)≥g^(m)≥1的条件来检验光的高阶非经典性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对流（coincidences）和单次计数（singles counting）可在严重实验缺陷存在时仍适用，是一种资源节约型的量子光学分析工具。本文旨在利用该工具在电信波长范围内表征所制备的光子数态，并证明其不仅能提取平均光子数，还能作为高阶非经典性检验的便捷手段。

Method: 利用周期性极化KTiOPO₄波导中的参量下转换产生交叉偏振孪生光束，宣告（herald）出光子数态（最高三光子）。一方面利用单次点击（single-click）概率提取态的平均光子数和矩生成函数的采样值；另一方面测量归一化阶乘矩g^(m)（阶数至m=n+1），通过违反不等式g^(m+1)≥g^(m)≥1来检验高阶非经典性。

Result: 成功制备并宣告了最高三光子的光子数态；实验表明单次点击概率可有效提取平均光子数并采样矩生成函数；测量了归一化阶乘矩直至m=n+1阶，并观察到对不等式g^(m+1)≥g^(m)≥1的违反，从而验证了光场的高阶非经典性。

Conclusion: 相对流和单次计数方法为量子光学态表征提供了资源节约且对实验缺陷鲁棒的途径；测量高阶归一化阶乘矩并检验其不等式违反是一种高效的高阶非经典性判定方法，适用于电信波段的实际量子光源。

Abstract: The manifold coincidences and singles counting provides a resource-saving quantum-optics analysis tool, since it is appropriate even in the presence of heavy experimental imperfections. Here, we prepare cross-polarized twin beams in the telecommunication wavelength range via parametric down-conversion in a periodically-poled KTiOPO$_4$ waveguide and herald photon-number states up to three photons. First, we show that the single-click probability is a versatile tool not only for extracting the state's mean photon number but also for sampling values of the moment generating function being the core behind any quantum optical state. Second, we measure values of the normalized factorial moments of photon number, $g^{(m)}_{\text{h}}$, up to the order $m = n+1$ for the heralded $n$-photon state. These normalized photon correlations provide an expedient method for examining the higher-order non-classicality of light by violating the condition $g^{(m+1)}_{\text{h}} \ge g^{(m)}_{\text{h}} \ge 1$.

</details>


### [110] [Pulse-Controlled Topologically Protected Quantum Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03543)
*Jin Yang,Biao Xiong,Jibing Liu,Houguo Yu,Dehua Liu,Feng Mei,Chuanjia Shan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于脉冲控制超导量子比特链的拓扑保护远距离充电协议，通过动态调制脉冲介导耦合实现从充电器到电池的拓扑保护能量传输，该过程无能量回流、对脉冲控制缺陷鲁棒、存储能量完全可提取，并可通过优化脉冲形状加速充电。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池作为新兴的能量存储设备，其远距离充电至关重要，因为它能最小化充电器与电池之间的干扰。但现有的远距离充电方案往往缺乏鲁棒性，容易受到控制误差和能量回流的影响，因此需要一种拓扑保护且抗干扰的远距离充电协议。

Method: 基于脉冲控制的超导量子比特链实现拓扑保护充电协议。具体而言：1) 通过动态调制脉冲介导的耦合强度，构造拓扑机制实现能量从充电器到电池的定向传输；2) 利用拓扑结构的保护特性消除能量回流并抵御脉冲控制的不完善；3) 通过优化脉冲形状加速充电过程并揭示其背后的物理机制。

Result: 充电过程无能量回流，且对脉冲控制中的缺陷具有鲁棒性；目标时刻储存在电池中的能量可以完全提取；该协议对较大系统规模仍然有效；通过优化脉冲形状可进一步加速充电。

Conclusion: 该脉冲控制的拓扑量子电池协议为实现远距离拓扑充电提供了通用框架，并建立了设计最优控制策略以增强量子电池性能的理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum batteries have emerged as a promising new generation of energy-storage devices for powering quantum technologies. Long-distance charging is particularly attractive because it minimizes interference between the charger and the battery, thereby attracting considerable interest. Here, we propose a topologically protected long-distance charging protocol for quantum batteries based on a pulse-controlled superconducting qubit chain. By dynamically modulating the pulse-mediated couplings, we realize topologically protected energy transfer from the charger to the battery. We show that the charging process is free of energy backflow and remains robust against imperfections in pulse control. Moreover, the energy stored in the battery at the target time is fully extractable, and the protocol remains effective for relatively large system sizes. To further accelerate charging, we optimize the pulse shape and elucidate the underlying physical mechanism. Our pulse-controlled topological quantum battery protocol provides a versatile framework for implementing long-distance topological charging and establishes a theoretical foundation for designing optimal-control strategies to enhance quantum battery performance.

</details>


### [111] [Enhancing noise robustness in device-independent conference key agreement with asymmetric parity-CHSH inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03551)
*Makoto Ishihara,Wojciech Roga,Jonatan Bohr Brask,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为非对称奇偶性CHSH不等式的多体贝尔不等式族，用于增强设备无关会议密钥协商（DI-CKA）的鲁棒性，并推导了条件冯·诺依曼熵的紧解析下界，表明结合噪声预处理可显著提升对检测效率低下及噪声的容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关会议密钥协商需要观测到无漏洞贝尔不等式违例，但实际实现极其困难，因为需要极高的检测效率。作者致力于通过新的不等式族来增强该协议的鲁棒性，使其对设备缺陷（如检测效率低、噪声）更加容忍。

Method: 1）引入一类新的多体贝尔不等式——非对称奇偶性CHSH不等式；2）推导基于该不等式协议中某一方结果的条件冯·诺依曼熵的紧解析下界，并纳入噪声预处理；3）利用该下界分析对检测效率低下以及局部和全局退极化噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 证明了非对称奇偶性CHSH不等式与噪声预处理的结合能够显著提高对检测不完善和噪声的鲁棒性，使设备无关会议密钥协商协议的实现门槛降低。

Conclusion: 通过引入非对称奇偶性CHSH不等式族并推导紧熵下界，显著改善了设备无关会议密钥协商对实验缺陷的容忍能力，为该协议的实际可行性迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Conference key agreement allows multiple remote parties to establish a shared secret key with information-theoretical security. In device-independent conference key agreement, security can be guaranteed with minimal assumptions on the devices used, provided that a violation of a Bell inequality is observed. However, implementations are extremely challenging because high detection efficiency is required to observe loophole-free Bell violations. Here, we enhance the robustness of device-independent conference key agreement by introducing a new family of multipartite Bell inequalities called the asymmetric parity-Clauser-Horne-Shimony-Holt (CHSH) inequalities. We derive a tight analytical lower bound on the conditional von Neumann entropy of the outcomes of one of the parties in a protocol based on this inequality, including noisy preprocessing. Using this bound, we analyze robustness to detection inefficiencies as well as local and global depolarizing noise. We show that the combination of the asymmetric parity-CHSH inequality and noisy preprocessing can significantly improve the robustness to imperfections.

</details>


### [112] [Optimizing Atom Transport, Gate-Count and Depth with Parity Twine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03583)
*Javad Kazemi,Michael Fellner,Riccardo J. Valencia-Tortora,Michael Schuler,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对中性原子量子处理器提出了奇偶校验架构（Parity Architecture）的高效实现，将Parity Twine Networks（PTNs）适配于不同的原子布局、原生纠缠门及原子穿梭能力，实现了量子电路门数、深度和原子传输的硬件感知优化。以量子傅里叶变换（QFT）为例，展示了在双量子比特门数、原子传输和电路深度上的显著削减，30量子比特QFT的估计电路保真度比对比编译策略高三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子处理器具有独特的布局灵活性和原子穿梭能力，但现有编译策略未能充分利用这些硬件特性。Parity Architecture虽然在减少门数和深度方面有理论优势，但缺乏针对不同中性原子硬件平台的高效实现方案，因此需要开发一种通用的硬件感知优化框架。

Method: 作者将Parity Twine Networks（PTNs）泛化到中性原子平台，提出了基于三种不同原生纠缠门（CZ、CZSWAP和iSWAP）的PTN构建方案，以适配静态和移动两类中性原子平台的硬件能力。该框架通过将任意交互图编码到公共基元中，同时优化门数、电路深度和原子输运过程。

Result: 以量子傅里叶变换（QFT）为基准测试，方法在双量子比特门数、原子传输量和电路深度上均有显著降低。30量子比特QFT的估计电路保真度比现有编译策略高出三个数量级。该方法还扩展到近期提出的乐观QFT（optimistic QFT）并讨论了在其他量子算法上的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 论文证明Parity Twine Networks可以有效地适配各种中性原子硬件配置，提供一种通用且硬件感知的量子电路编译优化框架，能够显著提升实际电路保真度，并具有扩展到其他量子算法的潜力。

Abstract: We present an efficient implementation of the Parity Architecture for neutral-atom quantum processors. We adapt Parity Twine Networks (PTNs) to different atom layouts, native entangling gates, and atom-shuttling capabilities. This provides a general framework for hardware-aware optimization of gate count, circuit depth, and atom transport for quantum circuits encoding arbitrary interaction graphs in a common basis. Specifically, we develop PTN constructions based on different native entangling-gate realizations, namely CZ, CZSWAP, and iSWAP, providing flexibility to accommodate different hardware capabilities on both static and mobile neutral-atom platforms. Using the quantum Fourier transform (QFT) as a representative example, we demonstrate substantial reductions in two-qubit gate count, atom transport, and circuit depth. These resource savings translate into an estimated circuit fidelity three orders of magnitude higher than competing compilation strategies for a 30-qubit QFT. We further extend the construction to the recently introduced optimistic QFT and discuss the broader applicability of PTNs to other quantum algorithms on neutral-atom platforms.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum Quasi-Monte Carlo: a window for pre-asymptotic quantum advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03625)
*Paolo Recchia,Zhan Yu,Kelvin Koor,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子准蒙特卡洛算法，将低差异序列与量子振幅估计相结合，在不改变渐近复杂度的情况下，通过预渐近优势窗口在有限查询次数场景下实现精度与查询效率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典蒙特卡洛方法计算开销大（精度ε需O(1/ε²)次函数求值），量子振幅估计可二次加速，但准蒙特卡洛方法尚未在量子框架中被探索。作者希望结合两者的优势，在实用（有限查询）场景中获得加速。

Method: 将低差异点集相干地制备为量子叠加态，结合量子振幅估计进行数值积分。总误差分解为有限网产生的离散化误差和量子估计误差两部分；通过调节电路分辨率和振幅估计参数（Q>q），在预渐近区域用更少的函数查询达到相同精度。

Result: 该方法在渐近意义上不优于经典准蒙特卡洛（误差仍受离散化项限制），但存在一个可调控的预渐近优势窗口：以2^q个低差异点才能达到的目标精度，可用2^Q（Q>q）的高分辨率网叠加制备，以显著更少的查询次数达成。

Conclusion: 量子准蒙特卡洛在渐近层面没有增益，但通过调节电路分辨率与振幅估计参数，可在有限的、实际的查询预算下获得实用优势，特别适合金融衍生品定价等需要高精度且查询量有限的场景。

Abstract: Numerical integration with Monte Carlo methods is a central computational task in many scientific and industrial applications, including financial derivative pricing and risk management. Classical Monte Carlo algorithms are computationally demanding: achieving an accuracy $ε$ typically requires a number of function evaluations scaling as $O(1/ε^2)$. Quantum-accelerated Monte Carlo methods based on quantum amplitude estimation can in principle quadratically improve this dependence. However, \textit{quasi}-Monte Carlo methods have not been explored in the quantum context. In this work, we introduce a quantum quasi-Monte Carlo algorithm that combines low-discrepancy nets with quantum amplitude estimation. The proposed method prepares the quasi-random point set coherently in superposition. The method does not yield an asymptotic improvement over classical quasi-Monte Carlo, since the total error separates into a discretization error, determined by the finite net, and a quantum estimation error. Instead, we explore a pre-asymptotic advantage window: for a target accuracy that would classically require $2^q$ low discrepancy points, one can prepare a higher-resolution net of size $2^Q$, with $Q>q$, in superposition and reach the same accuracy using significantly fewer function queries. This window can be controlled by tuning the circuit resolution and amplitude-estimation parameters, making the approach relevant for practical regimes where the number of queries is finite rather than asymptotically large.

</details>


### [114] [Entanglement dynamics of accelerated atoms with environment-induced interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03638)
*Xuerui Zhou,Chenhao Ma,Zixu Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个均匀加速原子在带反射边界的涨落无质量标量场中，环境诱导的原子间相互作用与原子-平板相互作用对纠缠动力学的竞争效应。研究发现演化中产生的最大并发度随加速度非单调变化，表明即使在两类环境诱导作用都存在时，反Unruh现象仍可在某些情况下出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在存在反射边界（平板）的环境中，加速原子系统的纠缠动力学如何受环境诱导的原子间相互作用与原子-平板相互作用的共同影响，并验证反Unruh现象在这种更复杂场景下是否依然存在。

Method: 采用开放量子系统框架，将两个垂直于边界对齐的均匀加速原子与带反射边界的涨落无质量标量场耦合，通过量子主方程求解纠缠动力学，分析环境诱导相互作用对演化中并发度的影响。

Result: 纠缠行为由两类环境诱导相互作用（原子间与原子-平板）的竞争决定，可通过特定临界值表征。演化中产生的最大并发度随加速度呈非单调变化，表明反Unruh现象在同时考虑两类环境诱导相互作用的情况下仍可在某些情形下存在。

Conclusion: 环境诱导的原子间与原子-平板相互作用共同决定纠缠演化行为，且反Unruh效应在包含平板边界的环境中依然可能发生，为理解加速系统与环境相互作用下的量子纠缠特性提供了新的认识。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of environment-induced interactions on the entanglement dynamics of two uniformly accelerated atoms coupled to a fluctuating massless scalar field with a reflecting boundary. The two atoms are aligned vertically to the boundary. The entanglement behaviors are influenced by the competition between the environment-induced interatomic and the environment-induced atom-plate interactions, which can be characterized by certain critical values. The maximum of concurrence generated during evolution decreases non-monotonically with the acceleration, which implies the anti-Unruh phenomenon can exist for some situations even when both environment-induced interatomic and the environment-induced atom-plate interactions are considered.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum Hamiltonian Evolution for Coherent Quantum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03640)
*Ignacio B. Acedo,Javier Gonzalez-Conde,Pablo Rodriguez-Grasa,Barry C. Sanders,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出相干量子学习（CQL）框架，将量子学习模型的参数本身视为量子自由度，在编码损失函数的哈密顿量下演化，使概率幅通过干涉集中在低损失构型附近，无需梯度计算或经典反馈即可完成训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子机器学习仍然依赖经典优化回路：参数由使用测量梯度估计的经典外层循环更新，量子相干性在训练动力学中不起作用，与经典处理无异。作者旨在消除这一经典瓶颈，让参数训练过程本身实现全量子化。

Method: 将参数寄存器初始化为叠加态，利用块编码和哈密顿量模拟构造编码损失函数的哈密顿量，通过幺正演化使概率幅在低损失参数处相干集中（干涉效应），无需梯度计算或经典反馈；构造适用于任意参数化量子电路，并通过顺序哈密顿量演化支持批处理训练。

Result: 在二元分类和干涉相位估计任务上的数值实验证实，演化后的分布峰值落在最优参数处，其性能与基于梯度的训练方法相当；构造原理上与容错量子实现兼容。

Conclusion: CQL展示了量子相干性可以直接驱动训练过程本身，开创了无需经典优化的全量子学习范式，为未来容错量子计算时代的量子机器学习提供了新的理论基础和实现路径。

Abstract: We introduce Coherent Quantum Learning (CQL), a training framework for quantum learning models in which the model parameters are quantum degrees of freedom evolved under a Hamiltonian that encodes the loss function. Current quantum machine learning retains classical optimization: parameters are updated by a classical outer loop using gradient estimates from measurements, and quantum coherence has no role in the training dynamics, just as in any classical treatment of the same problem. In the quantum case, a parameter register initialized in superposition evolves unitarily, and probability amplitude concentrates near low-loss configurations through interference, without gradient computation or classical feedback. We give an explicit construction using block encodings and Hamiltonian simulation, applicable to arbitrary parameterized circuits. Numerical experiments on binary classification and interferometric phase estimation confirm that the evolved distribution peaks at the optimal parameters, matching gradient-based performance. The construction is compatible in principle with fault-tolerant implementations and extends to batched training via sequential Hamiltonian evolution.

</details>


### [116] [Q-Edge: Symmetry-Reduced Quantum Simulation of Structured Extreme Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03706)
*Hongrui Zhang,Paolo Recchia,Ying Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Edge（量子极端依赖引擎），利用对称性将高维极端事件模拟的高维依赖空间无损压缩到轨道空间（对称类集合），并直接在轨道空间运行量子生成模型，实现结构化极端系统的可扩展模拟。30维问题中约160万角度态压缩至256个轨道态，量子表示从21量子位降至8量子位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多元极值模拟主要受限于依赖结构的组合复杂性而非极端观测的稀缺性。随着维度增加，依赖空间呈指数增长，传统方法难以扩展，亟需一种能减少状态空间同时保留结构化极端依赖的计算方法。

Method: 提出对称性无损轨道空间表示原理，将指数级的依赖空间替换为紧凑的对称类集合，保留结构化极端依赖。基于此开发Q-Edge框架，其在轨道空间直接运行量子生成模型，关键在于将对称性编码到数据表示中而非量子电路中，从而使无约束的量子生成模型利用显著缩减的状态空间。

Result: 在30维问题中，约160万角度状态被压缩为256个轨道状态，所需量子表示从约21个量子位降至8个，验证了轨道空间表示在量子模拟中的显著可扩展性优势。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了可扩展量子模拟结构化极端依赖的一般计算原理，表明将对称性融入数据表示层可同时实现无损压缩与量子资源节省，为高维极值系统的数字孪生开辟了新的路径。

Abstract: High-dimensional simulation of multivariate extremes is fundamentally limited by the combinatorial complexity of dependence, often more than by the scarcity of extreme observations. We show that symmetry admits a lossless orbit-space representation that preserves structured extreme dependence while replacing an exponentially large dependence space with a compact set of symmetry classes. Based on this principle, we develop Q-Edge (Quantum Extreme Dependence Engine), a symmetry-reduced quantum framework that operates directly in orbit space, enabling scalable simulation and digital twins of structured extreme systems. By transferring symmetry into the data representation rather than the quantum circuit, Q-Edge allows unconstrained quantum generative models to exploit dramatically reduced state spaces. For a 30-dimensional problem, approximately 1.6 million angular states collapse to 256 orbit states, reducing the required quantum representation from about 21 qubits to 8. Our results establish a general computational principle for scalable quantum simulation of structured extreme dependence.

</details>


### [117] [Analog quantum simulation of bosonic and anyonic models with flux-driven transmons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03737)
*Isak Lyngfelt,Jorge Fernández-Pendás,Göran Johansson,Laura Garciá-Álvarez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用电容耦合的磁通可调transmon阵列来模拟Bose-Hubbard和anyon-Hubbard模型的通用协议，通过交替调制transmon频率来共振驱动多体跃迁，并在数值模拟中验证了该方案能够重现目标模型的特征动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体相互作用产生的现象难以用经典计算模拟，而量子模拟器提供了一种替代方案。作者希望利用超导量子硬件直接实现目标系统的动力学，特别是针对Bose-Hubbard和任意子-Hubbard模型，因为此前尚未有利用transmon阵列模拟任意子-Hubbard模型的方案。

Method: 作者提出使用电容耦合的磁通可调transmon晶格，通过交替模式调制transmon频率，从而共振驱动多个多体跃迁。通过这种驱动方式可以调节每个位点多达三个玻色子的在位相互作用和密度依赖的跳跃振幅。通过在调制中加入相位使跃迁振幅变为复数，从而实现任意子-Hubbard模型的模拟。作者使用实验上现实的参数对所驱动的transmon阵列进行了数值模拟。

Result: 数值模拟结果表明，该协议在广泛的相互作用强度和统计相位范围内能够重现目标模型的特征动力学，包括依赖于相互作用的局域化现象以及任意子量子行走中依赖于统计相位的不对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提出了利用transmon阵列模拟任意子-Hubbard模型的协议，为在超导量子硬件上实现玻色子与任意子多体物理的量子模拟提供了可行的通用方案。

Abstract: When quantum particles interact, many-body phenomena that are hard to simulate classically emerge. Quantum analog simulation offers an alternative in which the target system's dynamics is directly realized in controllable quantum hardware. Here, we give a general protocol for simulating the Bose-Hubbard and anyon-Hubbard models using lattices of capacitively coupled flux-tunable transmons. By modulating the transmon frequencies in an alternating pattern, we resonantly drive multiple many-body transitions and can tune the on-site interaction and density-dependent hopping amplitudes for up to three bosons per site, with no additional restriction on the total particle number. By adding phases to the modulation, which renders the transition amplitudes complex-valued, we propose the first simulation protocol for the anyon-Hubbard model with transmons. Numerical simulations of the driven transmon arrays with experimentally realistic parameters reproduce the characteristic dynamics of the target models across a range of interaction strengths and statistical phases, including the interaction-dependent localization and the statistics-dependent asymmetry of the anyonic quantum walk.

</details>


### [118] [Thermodynamic Irreversibility from Inaccessible Endogenous Quantum Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03748)
*Borhan Ahmadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨孤立有限系统中，微观幺正动力学保留全部精细信息，但受限记录下熵仍会上涨的"鲁棒热力学窗口"现象。作者推导了精确熵率，区分熵扩散与回归电流，揭示隐藏信息无法组织恢复宏观低熵态的原因，并提出可工程调控的动力学以重构低熵宏态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观动力学是完全可逆且信息守恒的，但宏观系统却表现出不可逆的熵增。核心疑问在于：为何被当前记录隐藏的微观信息通常无法重建低熵宏态？作者试图从精确动力学分解入手，阐明信息隐藏与不可逆性之间的本质关系。

Method: 对固定投影记录，将每个有限步的演化精确分解为"仅由记录预测的演化"与"由未解析微观结构携带的精确修正"两部分。推导精确熵率，将电流分解为熵扩散项与回归导向项，并将回归电流按能隙幅值展开。通过相干求和分析具有相同能隙的跃迁，考察相互作用混合动力学与自由/受控规整动力学中的频率分布差异。同时引入分布级独立检验区分隐藏动力学活动与有限步回归，并提供经典保测对应物以区分量子特有部分。

Result: 记录贡献仅在时间的二阶及以后开始，因此所有记录的瞬时变化均来自宏态之间的隐藏电流。在相互作用混合动力学中，电流功率分散在众多频率上；而自由及精心设计的受控系统则能逐步集中电流功率，工程化动力学可重构低熵宏态。经典对应物表明部分构造并非量子独有的。

Conclusion: 在给定记录与观测窗口内，不可逆性并非微观信息的丢失，而是这些信息未能组织成能够恢复宏观秩序的电流。

Abstract: Microscopic unitary dynamics preserves all fine information, yet an isolated finite system can show a robust thermodynamic window in which the entropy associated with a restricted record rises. We ask why information hidden from the current record usually fails to rebuild a low-entropy macrostate. For a fixed projective record, every finite step separates exactly into the evolution predicted from the record alone and an exact correction carried by unresolved microscopic structure. The record-only contribution starts only at second order in time, so all instantaneous change of the record comes from hidden currents between macrostates. We derive the exact entropy rate, separate entropy-spreading from return-oriented currents, and resolve those currents into energy-gap amplitudes. Transitions with the same gap add coherently, revealing how the Hamiltonian and the microscopic state organize hidden information for return. In interacting mixing dynamics the current power is spread over many frequencies; free and deliberately commensurate controls progressively concentrate it, and the engineered dynamics reconstructs a low-entropy macrostate. An independent distribution-level test separates hidden dynamical activity from finite-step return, and an exact classical measure-preserving counterpart shows which parts of the construction are not uniquely quantum. Within the specified record and observation window, irreversibility is therefore not loss of microscopic information, but the failure of that information to organize currents that restore macroscopic order.

</details>


### [119] [Quantisation of Abstract Data Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03778)
*Mingsheng Ying,Zhicheng Zhang,Kean Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在泛代数框架中引入抽象量子数据类型的概念，为量子编程中的数据抽象提供代数基础。通过对经典数据类型进行量子化，证明其等式规范可以可靠地提升到量子环境，并表明比特预言机和相位预言机是此一般构造的特例，同时应用于量子数组和量子纠错码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编程语言需要形式化的数据类型抽象机制，而现有研究缺乏一个统一的代数框架来描述经典数据类型如何系统性地量子化，以及量子化后数据类型之间如何保持结构关系。

Method: 基于泛代数（universal algebra）框架，形式化定义了经典数据类型的量子化（quantisation）过程，证明等式规范可声音地提升至量子设置，将比特预言机与相位预言机统一为该构造的特例，并研究量子化对嵌入、同构和乘积等结构关系的保持条件。

Result: 提出了抽象量子数据类型的代数基础，统一了两种标准量子化方法（比特预言机和相位预言机），并通过量子数组与量子纠错码的实例验证了框架的表达力，同时刻画了量子化保持经典结构关系的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子编程中的数据抽象提供了系统的代数理论基础，能够统一解释多种量子数据类型构造，并为未来量子程序设计语言的类型系统设计提供数学支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a notion of abstract quantum data type within the framework of universal algebra. This notion provides an algebraic foundation for describing data abstraction in quantum programming. We formally define a quantisation of classical data types and show that their equational specifications can be soundly lifted to the quantum setting. Two standard quantisation methods for classical functions, namely the bit oracle and the phase oracle, arise as special cases of this general construction. We illustrate the framework with applications to quantum arrays and quantum error-correcting codes, showing how they can be understood through the lens of data-type quantisation. We further establish conditions under which quantisation preserves structural relationships and constructions of classical data types, including embeddings, isomorphisms, and products.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum Query Complexity of Finding a Tarski Fixed Point on a High-Dimensional Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03802)
*Tongyang Li,Weiran Ma,Ziyi Yang,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在量子查询模型中寻找塔斯基不动点（单调函数在完全格上的不动点）的复杂度下界。作者证明了在[n]^k上寻找单调函数不动点需要Ω(k log n)的量子查询下界，该下界在n=2和k=1两个极端情形下与已知经典下界匹配。为此，作者提出了树-过滤对手方法（Tree-Filtration Adversary Method）作为构建对手矩阵的新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knaster-Tarski不动点定理在经济学、博弈论和编程语言等领域有广泛应用。虽然经典查询模型下塔斯基不动点的查询复杂度已被广泛研究，但量子设定下的理解知之甚少。填补这一空白，建立量子查询模型下的不动点查找下界是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者使用非负谱对手方法（nonnegative spectral adversary method）证明量子查询下界。为了构造所需的对手矩阵，他们开发了树-过滤对手方法（Tree-Filtration Adversary Method），该方法不仅用于导出下界结果，还提供了非负谱对手方法更透明的组合学解释。关键是利用硬实例的树状组织结构，类比经典决策树下界的思想。

Result: 证明了在[n]^k上寻找单调函数不动点的Ω(k log n)量子查询下界。在n=2和k=1两个极端情形下匹配经典下界Ω(k)和Ω(log n)；当n,k≥2时，该下界在n<k情形优于已知最佳经典下界，在n≥k情形与经典下界相差不超过log n/log k因子。

Conclusion: 本文奠定了量子模型下塔斯基不动点查找问题的复杂度下界基础，提出的树-过滤对手方法为后续在具有树状硬实例结构的问题上建立量子复杂度下界提供了有前景的新途径。

Abstract: The Knaster-Tarski fixed-point theorem states that every monotone function over a complete lattice has a fixed point. Beyond its fundamental role in order theory, the theorem and its algorithmic variants have found broad applications in areas such as economics, game theory, and programming languages. While the query complexity of finding a Tarski fixed point has been extensively studied in classical models, comparatively little is known in the quantum setting.
  We prove an $Ω(k\log n)$ quantum query lower bound for finding a fixed point of a monotone function on $[n]^k$, using the nonnegative spectral adversary method. In the two extremal regimes $n = 2$ and $k = 1$, our quantum lower bound matches the previous classical lower bounds $Ω(k)$ and $Ω(\log n)$, respectively. For $n, k\geq 2$, our bound improves the best previous classical lower bound when $n < k$ and is within a factor of $\log n / \log k$ compared to the known classical lower bound when $n \geq k$. To construct the adversary matrix, we develop the Tree--Filtration Adversary Method. Besides yielding our lower bound, the method offers a more transparent combinatorial interpretation of the nonnegative spectral adversary method. When the hard instances of a problem admit a tree-like organization and suggest an intuition analogous to classical decision-tree lower bounds, our method provide a promising approach to establishing quantum complexity lower bounds.

</details>


### [121] [Energetic Costs of Subspace Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03825)
*Jakub Czartowski,Felix C. Binder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从热力学角度研究量子纠错作为"熵泵"的能耗问题，指出副边寄存器（syndrome register）的清除不可避免地贡献于纠错的能量代价。作者基于Knill-Laflamme条件构建了有效副边态（syndrome state），其von Neumann熵设定了维持可复用副边寄存器所需理想功的下界，并系统分析了稳定子码在独立局部Pauli噪声下的副边熵成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错将噪声引起的不确定性从受保护量子系统转移到存储于辅助存储器的副边信息中，相当于一台熵泵。然而，重复操作需要清除该存储器，这一清除过程不可避免地产生能量开销。此前对量子纠错热力学代价的理解不够系统，本文旨在刻画这一贡献及其与码结构、噪声和副边信息表示方式的依赖关系。

Method: 1) 从Knill-Laflamme条件出发构建有效副边态，利用其von Neumann熵给出可复用副边寄存器所需理想功的下界；2) 分析投影测量副边读出引入的额外熵，通过测量低效性（measurement inefficiency）量化该间隙；3) 将分析特化到稳定子码在独立局部Pauli噪声下的情形，区分"抽象误差标签"和"校验子比特"两个经典表示层级的副边熵；4) 在五量子比特码、Steane码、广义Shor码和旋转表面码上说明两种成本。

Result: 在抽象误差标签层面，单量子比特误差的简并性可降低处理后恢复标签的前导阶熵；在校验子比特层面，低权重校验子可降低低噪声区单次测量结果产生的边际熵。保留并单独擦除这些结果产生的额外负担被刻画为比特级低效性。四种示例码展示了副边存储器热力学成本的分层结构。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了副边存储器能量代价的层级体系，识别出控制子空间量子纠错理想热力学负担的码结构、噪声结构和测量结构，为理解量子纠错的热力学成本提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Quantum error correction acts as an entropy pump, transferring noise-induced uncertainty from a protected quantum system into syndrome information stored in an auxiliary memory. Repeated operation requires this memory to be cleared which unavoidably contributes to the energetic cost of error correction. Here, we characterise this contribution for subspace quantum error-correcting codes and identify how it depends on the joint structure of the code, the noise, and the representation of the retained syndrome information. Starting from the Knill-Laflamme conditions, we construct an effective syndrome state whose von Neumann entropy sets a lower bound on the ideal work required to maintain a reusable syndrome register. Projective syndrome readout generally generates additional entropy, and we quantify the resulting gap through measurement inefficiency. We then specialise to stabiliser codes under independent local Pauli noise and analyse two classical levels of syndrome representation. At the level of abstract error labels, degeneracies among single-qubit errors reduce the leading-order entropy of processed recovery labels. At the parity-check level, lower-weight checks reduce the marginal entropy generated by individual measurement outcomes in the low-noise regime. We identify the additional burden associated with retaining and separately erasing these outcomes as a bit-level inefficiency, and illustrate both costs for the five-qubit, Steane, generalised Shor, and rotated surface codes. Our results establish a hierarchy of syndrome-memory energetic costs and identify the code, noise, and measurement structures that control the ideal thermodynamic burden of subspace quantum error correction.

</details>


### [122] [Exact Compatibility Geometry of Three-Qubit Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03864)
*Wei Song,Xiao-Lan Zong,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在三量子比特系统中提出了一种置换对称的支撑关系，连接两两共形度（pairwise concurrences）与三体纠缠量（three-tangle），其极值仅由对称的 W 和 GHZ 局部幺正类达到。通过引入四维相容性体概念，将提出的全局关系与传统的 CKW 关系统一视为同一可达集的不同支撑方向，并给出了三量子比特纯态四维完全相容性的充要半代数刻画及其推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠是多体量子信息中的核心资源，理解多体系统不同层次的纠缠（两体与三体纠缠）之间的兼容性关系是量子纠缠理论的基础问题。CKW 不等式虽刻画了两体与三体纠缠间的不等式关系，但其不对称性与多体系统的置换对称性相悖，且无法完整描述多体纠缠分量的兼容约束。因此需要建立更普适、对称的关系框架来完整刻画三量子比特系统中的纠缠兼容性。

Method: 论文方法包括：(1) 构造三量子比特系统中三体纠缠量（three-tangle）与三对两两共形度之间置换对称的支撑关系；(2) 引入四维相容性体的几何概念，将新关系与 CKW 关系统一为同一可达集在不同方向上的支撑函数；(3) 利用半代数几何工具给出该四维相容性体的充要半代数刻画（即多项式等式与不等式系统）；(4) 将该刻画应用于纠缠分量间兼容性的逆向描述，并推广至任意由 concurrence 生成的纠缠度量。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 新提出的置换对称关系的极值在对称 W 类与 GHZ 类纯态上达到；(2) CKW 关系与所提出的新关系可统一为同一四维可达集在不同方向上的支撑，揭示了二者互为补充的几何本质；(3) 给出了三量子比特纯态完整四维兼容性的必要且充分的半代数描述；(4) 基于该刻画，给定其余纠缠分量时，缺失的两体纠缠分量仅能落在精确的无参数区间内；(5) 该理论框架对任意 concurrence 生成的纠缠度量均普适成立。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了三量子比特系统中两体与三体纠缠分量间更完整、具备置换对称性的兼容性理论，通过四维几何统一了 CKW 关系与新型全局支撑关系，并以半代数方法获得了纠缠分量间兼容性的精确刻画。这一框架深化了对多体纠缠层次间协调约束的理解，且由于可推广至更广的 concurrence 生成度量而具有一般方法论意义。

Abstract: We present a permutation-symmetric supporting relation between the pairwise concurrences and the three-tangle in three-qubit system, whose extrema are attained only by the symmetric $W$ and GHZ local-unitary classes. Furthermore, by introducing the concept of a four-dimensional compatibility body, we show that our proposed global relation and the previous CKW relation can be uniformly viewed as different supporting directions of the same achievable set. Subsequently, we provide a necessary-and-sufficient semi-algebraic characterization of the complete four-dimensional compatibility of a three-qubit pure state. Based on this, we can conversely describe the compatibility between entangled components: given the remaining entangled components, the missing two-body entangled component can only take values within a precise parameter-free interval. We demonstrate that the above construction also applies to arbitrary concurrence-generated entanglement measures.

</details>


### [123] [Ultra-Precise Quantum Projective Designs in Constant Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03925)
*Qingyue Zhang,Junjie Chen,Zhou You,You Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稀疏对易（commuting）电路系综，在n个量子比特上通过稀疏Clifford相位层与单比特Clifford门组合，就能以相对误差逼近2阶和3阶Haar矩（形成ε-近似射影2-和3-designs），且所需的交互对数度在该电路族内渐近最优。该电路可实现无辅助量子位的O(log(n/ε))深度（全连接架构），或深度为7的常数深度自适应实现（需O(n log(n/ε))辅助量子位）。其分析突破了现有浅层设计范式，利用对易相位电路的内在矩结构，三阶情形需要新的分块分解与组合分析，为高阶浅层设计提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的Haar随机性在实际中代价高昂且通常并非必需，而现有浅层量子设计（shallow design）在资源开销或逼近方式上存在局限。本文旨在构造一种资源极省、能严格相对误差逼近低阶Haar矩的稀疏电路系综，以支撑随机化表征、量子计量、量子算法和多体物理等应用。

Method: 构造一个由稀疏Clifford相位层加独立单比特Clifford门组成的对易电路系综；通过分析对易相位电路的矩结构（moment structure）来证明其在相对误差意义下逼近2阶和3阶射影设计。三阶分析引入了新的块分解与组合计数方法。电路实现上给出两种方案：无辅助位的O(log(n/ε))深度的全连接实现，以及深度为7的常数深度自适应实现（使用O(n log(n/ε))辅助量子位）。

Result: 证明该系综在相对误差下构成ε-近似射影2-和3-designs；所需对数交互度在该电路族内渐近最优；可实现O(log(n/ε))深度（全连接、无辅助位）或深度7的常数深度实现（带辅助位）。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，精确的Haar类统计可从稀疏对易动力学中涌现，且所需量子资源极低，打破了现有浅层设计范式，并为高阶浅层设计的构造提供了新思路。

Abstract: Random quantum objects are powerful resources for quantum information processing, yet exact Haar randomness is costly and typically unnecessary. We introduce an explicit sparse commuting circuit ensemble on $n$ qubits that reproduces low-order Haar moments in the stringent relative-error sense. The circuit consists of a sparse Clifford phase layer followed by independent single-qubit Clifford gates. Acting on a simple product state, the resulting ensemble forms $ε$-approximate projective $2$- and $3$-designs in relative error, with the required logarithmic interaction degree being asymptotically optimal within this circuit family. It admits an ancilla-free implementation of quantum depth $O(\log(n/ε))$ on an all-to-all architecture, as well as an adaptive constant-depth implementation---in fact, depth seven---using $O(n\log(n/ε))$ ancilla qubits. Departing from existing shallow-design paradigms, our analysis exploits the intrinsic moment structure of commuting phase circuits; at third order, this requires a new block decomposition and combinatorial analysis that also suggests a route toward higher-order shallow designs. Our results show that precise Haar-like statistics can emerge from sparse commuting dynamics with remarkably low quantum resources, with applications to randomized characterization, quantum metrology, quantum algorithms, and many-body physics.

</details>


### [124] [Approximate maximum-likelihood decoding via truncated free energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03928)
*Yuanqi Liu,Weilei Zeng,Junjie Wu,Lingling Lao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出近似最大似然译码（AMLD），一个黑盒框架，通过回收随机内层解码器丢弃的候选样本，构建逐类截断自由能估计器来实现近似最大似然译码。AMLD在SA-based Ising解码器基准上将MWD-MLD阈值差距最高关闭83%，并在[[144,12,12]]双环码上相对MWD降低13%的逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然译码（MLD）能达到稳定子码的最小逻辑错误率，但其精确评估是#P-hard问题。实际管线采用最小权重译码（MWD）近似MLD，仅保留每个症状的最低权重恢复并丢弃陪集简并性。这种近似导致性能下降，需要一种既能利用陪集简并性又保持计算可行的译码方法。

Method: AMLD是一个黑盒框架，核心思想是回收随机内层解码器（如SA）丢弃的候选样本，将其用于逐类截断自由能估计。对候选池中每个逻辑类，该估计器可证明地被精确自由能下界和经验最小权重上界所限定。AMLD返回使估计自由能最小的逻辑类，仅需线性经典开销。

Result: 在SA-based Ising解码器基准中，AMLD在环面码和彩色码的bit-flip及去极化噪声下关闭了MWD-MLD阈值差距的83%。最大阈值改进（17.28%到18.62%）出现在6.6.6彩色码去极化噪声下。在[[144,12,12]]双环码上，p=0.05时AMLD相对MWD降低13%的逻辑错误率，且无需匹配枚举或码特定的张量网络收缩。

Conclusion: AMLD提供了一种通用、高效的近似MLD方案，通过利用随机解码器候选池中已有的信息来恢复部分陪集简并，在多种码型和噪声模型下显著缩小MWD与MLD之间的性能差距，且开销仅为线性，具有良好的实用潜力。

Abstract: Maximum-likelihood decoding (MLD) achieves the minimum logical error rate of stabilizer codes under known i.i.d. Pauli noise, but its exact evaluation is \#P-hard. Practical pipelines therefore approximate MLD by minimum-weight decoding (MWD), retaining only the lowest-weight recovery per syndrome and discarding the coset degeneracy. The minimum-weight search is in turn implemented by stochastic solvers. We introduce approximate maximum-likelihood decoding (AMLD), a black-box framework that recycles the candidate samples discarded by stochastic inner decoders into a per-class truncated free-energy estimator. For every logical class represented in the candidate pool, the estimator is provably bounded below by the exact free energy and above by the empirical minimum weight. AMLD returns the logical class minimizing the estimated free energy with linear classical overhead. In SA-based Ising-decoder benchmarks, AMLD closes up to $83\%$ of the MWD--MLD threshold gap across the toric and color codes under bit-flip and depolarizing noise. The largest threshold improvement, from $17.28\%$ to $18.62\%$, occurs on the $6.6.6$ color code under depolarizing noise. We further demonstrate AMLD on the $[[144,12,12]]$ bivariate-bicycle code, whose bit-flip decoding problem has a hypergraph structure. This application requires neither matching-based enumeration nor code-specific tensor-network contraction. At $p=0.05$, AMLD reduces the logical error rate by $13\%$ relative to MWD evaluated on the same BP-OSD candidate pool.

</details>


### [125] [On the geometry and typicality of quantum magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03944)
*Zhenhuan Liu,Z-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于n量子比特系统，满足Tr(ρ²)≤1/(d-a*)条件的所有量子态都位于稳定子多胞体内部（即无魔法性），并结合高维多胞体的几何性质，建立了稳定子多胞体的Hilbert-Schmidt内半径和体积半径的定量估计，揭示了随机诱导态中魔法性的典型性，证明了尖锐的相变现象，并将稳定子多胞体的面数上下界从拟多项式改进为指数级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子魔法（magic）是衡量量子态偏离Clifford稳定子态（可经典模拟）程度的重要资源。理解稳定子集（stabilizer polytope）的几何结构对量子计算中经典模拟边界、资源理论以及量子纠错具有重要意义。然而，高维情况下稳定子多胞体的几何性质（如半径、面数等）此前缺乏精确估计，面数的拟多项式下界也远非紧致，需要更精确地表征其复杂度。

Method: 本文首先证明了任意满足Tr(ρ²)≤1/(d-a*)（其中a*=0.458327...）的态必定位于稳定子多胞体内部，从而给出其Hilbert-Schmidt内半径的下界。随后，结合高维多胞体的一般几何性质（如体积半径与面数的关系），推导出稳定子多胞体的体积半径定量估计。利用这些几何估计，作者分析了从d×k维Haar随机纯态中追踪掉k维子系统所得到的随机诱导态的魔法性概率，并证明其存在尖锐的相变，相变维度k*介于Ω(d²/log²d)与O(d²)之间。最后通过多胞体对偶理论，获得了稳定子多胞体面数介于exp[Ω(d²/log²d)]与exp[O(d²log²d)]之间的结论。

Result: (1) 建立了稳定子多胞体Hilbert-Schmidt内半径和体积半径的定量估计；(2) 证明了随机诱导态魔法性概率存在尖锐相变，相变维度位于Ω(d²/log²d)与O(d²)之间；(3) 将稳定子多胞体面数下界从拟多项式改进为exp[Ω(d²/log²d)]，上界为exp[O(d²log²d)]，表明任何精确描述无魔法区域所需的线性不等式数量（相对于量子比特数）是双重指数级的；(4) 综合表明稳定子多胞体展现出具有特定顶点数的高维多胞体所能允许的近最大几何复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了稳定子多胞体在量子比特数增长时具有极高的几何和组合复杂度：其面数呈指数级增长，意味着精确刻画无魔法态区域在计算上极为困难。稳定子多胞体的这些几何特征（接近高维多胞体理论中的最优复杂度）表明，量子魔法资源理论在高维情形下的精确描述本质上是"复杂"的，这对理解量子计算超越经典模拟的能力边界具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We prove that, for an $n$-qubit system of dimension $d=2^n$, every state satisfying $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^2)\le 1/(d-a_\ast)$, with $a_\ast=0.458327\cdots$, lies inside the stabilizer polytope and is therefore magic-free. Combining this result with general geometric properties of high-dimensional polytopes, we establish quantitative estimates for the Hilbert--Schmidt inradius and volume radius of the stabilizer polytope, and use them to characterize the typicality of magic in random induced states obtained by tracing out a $k$-dimensional subsystem from a $d\times k$-dimensional Haar-random pure state. We prove a sharp phase transition in the probability of such states having magic, whose transition dimension $k_\star$ is bounded between $Ω(d^2/\log^2d)$ and $\mathcal{O}(d^2)$. We further prove that the number of facets of the stabilizer polytope lies between $\exp[Ω(d^2/\log^2 d)]$ and $\exp[\mathcal{O}(d^2\log^2 d)]$, substantially improving upon the previous quasipolynomial lower bound and implying that any exact description of the magic-free region requires a doubly exponential number of linear inequalities in the number of qubits. Overall, our results show that the stabilizer polytope exhibits near-maximal geometric complexity allowed for a high-dimensional polytope with a certain number of vertices.

</details>


### [126] [Robust multi-hypothesis quantum-state discrimination under unknown common unitary perturbations via least favorable priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04020)
*Daichi Fujiki,Fuyuhiko Tanaka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究所有候选量子态受同一未知幺正扰动影响时的鲁棒K元量子态判别问题，将问题建模为关于扰动的极小极大决策问题，并提出使用与最小有利先验（LFP）相关联的Bayes最优集体测量。通过半定规划在有限离散化网格上计算LFP，数值实验表明该方法能显著平坦化成功概率曲线并提升最坏情形下的判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际量子态判别任务中，所有候选态可能共同受到一个未知的幺正扰动影响（如参考系校准误差、系统漂移等）。该扰动不是待识别的标签，而是作为干扰因子降低固定测量的性能。现有的最优测量设计通常假设系统无扰动或扰动已知，缺乏对这类共性扰动的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 将问题形式化为在可能扰动空间上的极小极大（minimax）决策问题；提出采用与最小有利先验（LFP）相关联的Bayes最优集体测量。首先论证在扰动空间的有限离散化下，LFP可通过半定规划（SDP）求解，且对应值等于有限网格上的极小极大成功概率；随后将该方法应用于二进制非正交qubit模型和三态非正交qutrit模型进行数值验证。

Result: 数值结果表明：基于LFP的测量与参考点最优测量和均匀先验Bayes测量相比，能大幅平坦化成功概率分布剖面并显著改善最坏情形成功概率。LFP将权重集中在主动限制鲁棒判别性能的扰动参数区域，既提供了建设性的测量设计方案，也提供了对困难扰动参数范围的诊断性描述。

Conclusion: 基于LFP的Bayes最优测量能够有效对抗未知公共幺正扰动带来的性能退化，其权重分布揭示了限制鲁棒判别性能的关键参数区域，为鲁棒量子态判别提供了兼具理论保证与实际可行性的新方案。

Abstract: We study robust K-ary quantum-state discrimination when all candidate states are affected by the same unknown common unitary perturbation. The unknown perturbation does not represent the label to be identified, but acts as a nuisance factor that changes the performance of a fixed measurement. We formulate the problem as a minimax decision problem over the possible perturbations and propose using the Bayes-optimal collective measurement associated with a least favorable prior (LFP) on the nuisance-parameter space. For a finite discretization of this space, we show that the LFP can be computed by a semidefinite program and that the corresponding value coincides with the finite-grid minimax success probability. As numerical demonstrations, we consider a binary nonorthogonal qubit model and a nonorthogonal three-state qutrit model with an unknown common unitary perturbation. The LFP-based measurement substantially flattens the success-probability profile and improves the worst-case success probability compared with a reference-point optimal measurement and a uniform-prior Bayes measurement. The resulting LFP concentrates its weight on regions of the nuisance-parameter space that actively limit the robust discrimination performance, thereby providing both a constructive measurement design and a diagnostic description of the difficult nuisance-parameter regimes.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum-Enhanced Phase Estimation with Photon-Added Even and Odd Coherent States in an SU(1,1) Interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04064)
*Abdelmajid El Maaroufi,Mouad Ait Maskour,Bouchra Maroufi,Mohammed Daoud,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在SU(1,1)干涉仪中使用m光子增加偶/奇相干态作为非经典输入资源的相位估计问题。通过强度探测和误差传播法评估相位灵敏度，并以量子Fisher信息推导量子Cramér-Rao界作为极限精度。结果表明光子增加显著提升相位灵敏度、增大量子Fisher信息、降低量子Cramér-Rao界，性能超越标准量子极限并逐渐接近海森堡标度。偶相干态相对奇相干态有微弱优势，但随m增大差异减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过光子增加操作增强SU(1,1)干涉仪的相位估计精度，寻求超越标准量子极限并接近海森堡极限的量子计量方案，同时厘清光子增加与相干态初始奇偶性对计量性能的协同或竞争作用。

Method: 采用强度探测结合误差传播方法评估相位灵敏度，利用量子Fisher信息计算量子Cramér-Rao界确定最终精度极限。将m光子增加偶/奇相干态注入SU(1,1)干涉仪作为非经典输入资源，并与未加光子态的对应相干态进行系统性对比。

Result: 光子增加显著增强了相位灵敏度并提高量子Fisher信息，降低量子Cramér-Rao界；灵敏度超过标准量子极限，且随光子增加数m增大逐步趋近海森堡标度。m光子增加偶相干态比奇对应态有适度优势，但m增大时该优势逐渐消失。

Conclusion: 光子增加操作是提升SU(1,1)干涉仪计量性能的有效手段，且随增加光子数增大，初始相干态奇偶性对相位估计性能的影响被削弱，光子增加本身成为主导性能的关键因素。

Abstract: We investigate phase estimation in an SU(1,1) interferometer employing $m$-photon-added even and odd coherent states as nonclassical input resources. The phase sensitivity is evaluated through intensity detection and the error propagation method, while the ultimate precision limit is determined from the quantum Cramér-Rao bound with the quantum Fisher information serving as the relevant metrological quantity. Our results demonstrate that photon addition significantly enhances the phase sensitivity, increases the quantum Fisher information, and reduces the quantum Cramér-Rao bound, leading to a clear improvement over the corresponding even and odd coherent states. Furthermore, the achievable sensitivity exceeds the standard quantum limit and gradually approaches the Heisenberg scaling with increasing photon-addition number. We also find that the $m$-photon-added even coherent states exhibit a modest advantage over their odd counterparts. As $m$ increases, however, this distinction becomes progressively weaker, suggesting that photon addition diminishes the role of the initial parity of the coherent state in determining the interferometric performance.

</details>


### [128] [Small-quench Loschmidt dynamics near quantum critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04065)
*Kohei Kobayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子临界点附近小幅度突然淬火后的短时Loschmidt动力学，证明Loschmidt率函数的初始二次增长由淬火算符的方差密度决定，该方差密度在局域淬火算符情形下等于初始基态中等时连通两点关联函数的空间求和，从而将早期Loschmidt响应与静态临界关联联系起来。通过假设临界点的幂律关联，按淬火算符的标度维度对短时系数的有限尺寸标度进行分类，并在横场Ising链中加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子临界点附近的Loschmidt动力学，特别是早期时间演化如何反映静态临界关联，以及淬火算符的选择如何影响Loschmidt率函数的有限尺寸标度行为。

Method: 理论分析推导Loschmidt率函数在短时域的二次增长系数与初始基态等时两点关联函数的关系；利用共形场论和标度维度的思想对淬火算符进行分类；使用横场Ising链作为具体模型验证理论预测，并引入后淬火哈密顿量的四阶累积量讨论超出二次区的首阶修正。

Result: 推导出Loschmidt率函数初始二次增长的系数等于淬火算符方差密度；对于局域算符等于等时连通两点关联的空间和。在一维情形下，该系数随系统尺寸呈现有限、对数增强或代数增强三种标度行为，取决于淬火算符的标度维度。横场与纵场淬火耦合不同临界算符，展示了算符依赖性。

Conclusion: 短时Loschmidt率函数的响应系数编码了静态临界关联信息，为探测临界系统提供了新的动力学探针。淬火算符的标度维度决定了有限尺寸效应，且四阶累积量提供了对二次区以外修正的描述框架。

Abstract: We study the short-time Loschmidt dynamics after a small sudden quench near a quantum critical point. We show that the initial quadratic growth of the Loschmidt rate function is governed by the variance of the quench operator per system size. For a local quench operator, this variance density is exactly equal to the spatial sum of the equal-time connected two-point correlation function in the initial ground state. This relation connects the early-time Loschmidt response to static critical correlations. Assuming power-law correlations at criticality, we classify the finite-size scaling of the short-time coefficient by the scaling dimension of the quench operator. In one dimension, the coefficient is finite, logarithmically enhanced, or algebraically enhanced with system size. We illustrate this operator dependence in the transverse-field Ising chain, where transverse-field and longitudinal-field quenches couple to different critical operators. We also discuss the first correction beyond the quadratic regime using the fourth cumulant of the post-quench Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [129] [Algebraic Operator Decomposition: A Partitioned Architecture for Noise-Resilient Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04076)
*Wladimir Silva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种算子分解架构（AOD），通过将全局算子数学上分解为可独立执行的局部算子，以经典重建和采样开销为代价降低最大量子电路深度。该架构利用幺半群（Monoid）的数学性质，结合MapReduce编程模型，在量子执行前进行代数预分解，应用于内积、级数展开、迹和卷积等线性算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中，深量子电路容易受到噪声和误差积累的影响。现有的量子纠错和误差缓解方法关注执行中的误差处理，但缺乏在执行前从代数层面降低电路深度的系统性方法。作者希望通过算子的代数预分解来降低电路最大深度，从而补充现有纠错和缓解技术的不足。

Method: 作者提出算子分解架构（AOD），核心思想是将一个全局算子映射为一系列可独立执行的局部算子。方法包括：(1) 利用计算机科学中幺半群（Monoid）的设计模式——要求数据类型、满足结合律的组合函数以及安全的单位元素，确保分解后的算子可安全重组；(2) 定义MapReduce编程模型，以加法(+)作为归约器，利用稳定的交换幺半群特性，避免"负概率税"或相位冲突；(3) 将方法推广到加法阿贝尔群上的线性算子向量空间，涵盖内积、级数展开、迹和卷积等算子类型。

Result: 论文给出了该范式的数学基础与仿真结果，展示了AOD在降低最大量子电路深度方面的有效性，同时通过经典重建和采样开销来换取量子资源的节省。

Conclusion: AOD提供了一种在量子执行前通过代数分解降低电路深度的互补性策略，可与量子纠错和误差缓解方法协同使用。基于交换幺半群和MapReduce模型的框架保证了分解的正确性和稳定性，为复杂量子线路的深度优化提供了新的数学途径。

Abstract: We present an operator-decomposition architecture that mathematically maps a global operator into independently executable local operators, reducing the maximum quantum circuit depth at the cost of classical reconstruction and sampling overhead. By framing complex Quantum Circuits around an operator in a vector space that can be algebraically pre-decomposed, AOD complements quantum error correction and error-mitigation approaches by performing algebraic decomposition before quantum execution.
  Our approach leans in the computer science definition of a Monoid: a design pattern and mathematical concept consisting of a data type, a combining function that is associative, and a safe identity (neutral) element that does not change other values when combined. Simulation wise we define a MapReduce programming model where the addition (+) is the reducer, thus leveraging a naturally stable commutative monoid which carries zero "negative-probability tax" or phase conflicts.
  Furthermore, we define a Vector Space of Linear Operators over Additive Abelian Groups that benefit from this paradigm, including: Inner Products, Series expansions, Traces and Convolutions. Finally, we present the mathematical foundations and simulation results for this paradigm.

</details>


### [130] [Frequency-Multiplexed Parallel Gates for Quantum LDPC Codes in a Two-Dimensional Ion Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04081)
*G. -X. Tang,L. -M. Duan,Y. -K. Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在二维离子晶体上利用频分复用实现并行非局域纠缠门，用于量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）码的综合征测量，避免了物理移动离子的慢速操作。通过对256离子/512离子晶体中的双变量自行车码进行数值优化，在现实噪声参数下可实现10^-12的逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码具有高编码率，但其综合征测量需要非局域纠缠门。现有方案中物理移动离子会随着离子数增加而变慢，成为性能瓶颈。因此需要一种无需物理移动即可实现并行非局域纠缠门的方法。

Method: 在二维离子晶体上采用频分复用（frequency-multiplexing）实现并行非局域纠缠门。通过绝热条件抑制门保真度损失和串扰误差，并增强对阱频慢漂移的鲁棒性（阱频漂移是离子阱的主要误差源）。对量子比特映射和频带分配进行优化以降低激光功率需求并最大化并行性。

Result: 以[[248,10,18]]双变量自行车码、512离子二维晶体为数值示例，优化比特映射与频带分配后，中等激光功率即可实现并行操作；在现实噪声参数下可达到10^-12的逻辑错误率。

Conclusion: 频分复用方案为qLDPC码在离子阱平台的实现提供了可行路径，兼顾了高编码率与并行非局域门的高保真度，同时对抗阱频漂移等主导误差，展示了离子晶体规模化量子纠错的潜力。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes admit high encoding rates but require nonlocal entangling gates for syndrome measurement. Instead of physically moving the qubits which slows down with the increasing qubit number, here we propose to achieve parallel nonlocal entangling gates on a two-dimensional (2D) ion crystal using frequency-multiplexing. Adiabatic conditions ensure the suppression of gate infidelity and crosstalk error, as well as their robustness against slow drift in the trap frequency which is a leading error source in ion trap. We consider a numerical example of a $[[248,10,18]]$ bivariate bicycle code on a 2D crystal of 512 ions. By optimizing the mapping of the qubits and the assignment of the frequency bands for multiplexing, we show that a moderate laser power is sufficient for parallelism, and that a logical error rate of $10^{-12}$ can be achieved under realistic noise parameters.

</details>


### [131] [Non-local Magic: closed-form solution and equivalence with magic of purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04119)
*Michele Viscardi,Lorenzo Leone,Alioscia Hamma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对二分量子态的非局域魔术（non-local magic）给出了解析刻画，证明对数稳定子保真度的非局域魔术可由Schmidt谱唯一确定，并引入"纯化魔术"概念建立混合态资源理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域魔术衡量二分量子态中无法通过局域幺正变换消除的非稳定子性质，但直接计算通常需要困难的局域幺正优化，缺乏高效的解析刻画。

Method: (1) 证明对数稳定子保真度下非局域魔术的优化可解析求解，仅依赖于Schmidt谱；(2) 定义"纯化魔术"（magic of purification），即混合态所有纯化中纯态魔术的最小值；(3) 证明该量诱导的资源理论中自由态为归一化稳定子码投影子；(4) 将对数稳定子保真度的纯化魔术用Uhlmann保真度表达为距离形式；(5) 证明非局域魔术等于约化密度算符在幺正轨道上纯化魔术的最小值。

Result: 获得了非局域魔术的高效解析刻画，建立了非局域魔术与混合态资源理论（纯化魔术）之间的严格联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为非局域魔术提供了精确解析公式及混合态资源理论解释，弥补了此前仅靠数值优化的局限。

Abstract: Non-local magic quantifies the non-stabilizerness of a bipartite quantum state that cannot be removed by local unitary transformations. Despite its natural definition, its evaluation generally requires a difficult optimization over local unitaries. Here, we show that for the log-stabilizer fidelity this optimization admits an exact closed-form analytic solution depending only on the Schmidt spectrum. We then introduce the magic of purification, defined as the minimum pure-state magic over all purifications of a mixed state, and show that it naturally induces a resource theory whose free states are normalized stabilizer-code projectors. For the log-stabilizer fidelity, the magic of purification admits a distance-based formulation in terms of the Uhlmann fidelity. Remarkably, we prove that non-local magic coincides with the minimum magic of purification along the unitary orbit of the reduced density operator. Our results provide both an efficient analytical characterization and a mixed-state resource-theoretic interpretation of non-local magic.

</details>


### [132] [Characterizing Large Scale Quantum Systems with Error Per Circuit Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04132)
*Travis Hurant,Arian Vezvaee,Swarnadeep Majumder,Aniket Dalvi,Jude Alnas,Kenneth R. Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出EPCL（Error Per Circuit Layer），一种基于重叠度的电路级量子基准测试方法，通过在两个寄存器上施加相同随机电路并测量输出态重叠的衰减来估计有效层极化度，无需经典模拟理想输出分布，兼容任意门集（包括非Clifford门），并在IBM量子硬件上验证了8和16量子位的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路级量子基准测试方法存在局限性：有的要求结构化门集和高昂的编译开销，有的需要经典模拟参考输出，有的依赖无法捕捉全寄存器行为的子系统分解。需要一种能够捕捉跨相互作用操作噪声累积效应、同时避免这些限制的通用基准测试方法。

Method: EPCL通过对两个不相交的量子寄存器施加相同的随机电路，测量其输出态之间的重叠（overlap）作为电路深度的函数，以此估计有效层极化度。在集合平均的退极化噪声模型下推导期望的重叠衰减，采用单指数拟合提取衰减参数。

Result: 数值模拟表明：在弱局部随机噪声下EPCL能恢复预测的极化度，在更强随机噪声下仍可用单指数衰减良好描述。固定纠缠层中的相干误差需要Pauli twirling或随机编译来产生预期衰减。IBM量子硬件上的8和16量子位实验展示了清晰的EPCL衰减。

Conclusion: EPCL可作为测量聚合寄存器性能的有效方法，无需经典模拟理想电路输出，也不受限于结构化门集，为通用电路级量子基准测试提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum benchmarks provide compact measures of performance that are important for evaluating and comparing quantum systems. Circuit-level benchmarks are particularly valuable because they capture the accumulated effects of noise across interacting operations, but existing approaches may require structured gate sets and costly compilation, classical simulation of reference outputs, or subsystem decompositions that do not capture full-register behavior. We introduce Error Per Circuit Layer (EPCL), an overlap-based circuit-level benchmark that estimates an effective layer polarization by applying identical random circuits to two disjoint quantum registers and measuring the overlap between their output states as a function of circuit depth. EPCL avoids classical simulation of ideal output distributions and recovery to a known reference state, and is compatible with arbitrary gate sets, including non-Clifford gates.
  We derive the expected overlap decay under an ensemble-averaged depolarizing model and identify the assumptions under which the fitted decay parameter represents an effective layer polarization. Numerical simulations show that EPCL recovers the predicted polarization under weak local stochastic noise and remains well described by a single-exponential decay at stronger stochastic noise levels. The simulations further show that coherent errors associated with fixed entangling layers may require Pauli twirling or randomized compiling to produce the expected decay, while inter-register correlations contribute an additional covariance term to the measured overlap. Finally, experiments on IBM quantum hardware demonstrate clear EPCL decay in 8- and 16-qubit implementations. These results support EPCL as a method for measuring aggregate register performance without requiring classical simulation of ideal circuit outputs or restriction to structured gate sets.

</details>


### [133] [Efficient Conversion of Optical to Mechanical States Close to the Single-Quantum Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04150)
*Alexander Rolf Korsch,Liu Chen,Pedro V. Pinho,Boris Müllendorff,Jan N. Kirchhof,Yong Yu,Thiago P. Mayer Alegre,Simon Gröblacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在毫开尔文温度下运行的光子晶体光机系统中，实现了接近单量子极限的低噪声光子-声子态转移。利用弱相干光脉冲（少光子水平），达到了创纪录的0.76光子-声子转换效率、7.3微秒机械存储寿命及超过4.5 MHz的可调转换带宽，并通过HBT测量验证了转换声子态的相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前光机诱导透明（OMIT）转换方案受限于室温工作导致的热机械噪声干扰，仅能处理具有大相干态光子数的经典输入信号，无法实现接近单量子级别的光子-声子相干态转换。

Method: 将光机晶体器件冷却至毫开尔文温度以抑制热机械噪声，使用弱相干光脉冲（少光子级别）作为输入信号，通过OMIT机制实现光子到GHz机械模式的相干映射与存储。

Result: 实现了η=0.76的创纪录光子-声子转换效率、T1=7.3微秒的机械存储寿命、超过4.5 MHz的可调转换带宽以及n_th=9.0的低附加热噪声。HBT测量证实了所检索信号的相干性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了光机晶体作为GHz机械模式高效光学接口的地位，为实现确定性单量子级别的机械态制备和声子态工程铺平了道路。

Abstract: Coherent interfaces between optical photons and mechanical excitations provide a promising route towards phonon-state engineering and hybrid quantum information processing. Cavity optomechanical systems enable such interfaces via optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT), allowing coherent mapping between traveling optical fields and localized mechanical modes. However, previous implementations of OMIT conversion protocols were limited to classical input signals with large coherent state photon numbers due to room temperature operation and corresponding thermal mechanical noise. Here, we demonstrate efficient low-noise photon-phonon state transfer close to the single-quantum regime in an optomechanical crystal operated at Millikelvin temperatures. Using weak coherent optical input pulses at the few-photon level, we achieve a record-level photon-phonon conversion efficiency of $η=0.76$, a mechanical storage lifetime of $T_\mathrm{1}=7.3~μs$, and a tunable conversion bandwidth exceeding 4.5 MHz. Hanbury Brown-Twiss measurements of the retrieved signal demonstrate the coherent nature of the converted phononic state, evidencing low added thermal noise in the conversion process ($n_\mathrm{th}=9.0$). These results establish optomechanical crystals as efficient optical interfaces to GHz mechanical modes and provide a pathway toward deterministic single-quantum-level mechanical state preparation.

</details>


### [134] [Spurious quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04157)
*Shashaank Khanna,Matthew F. Pusey,Roger Colbeck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了因果结构中"伪量子关联"的存在性：某些关联在一种因果结构中看起来是非经典的量子关联，但在另一种因果结构中可以有自然的经典解释（无需微调）。作者进一步区分了哪些因果结构的非经典量子关联可在替代因果结构中被经典解释，哪些则不行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell定理表明量子关联无法在Bell因果结构下用经典方式解释，但若允许超光速因果影响等替代因果结构，经典解释是可能的，只是需要微调。本文旨在探讨这些替代解释的普遍性，特别是是否存在"伪量子关联"——在一种结构中看似量子但在他处可自然经典解释，从而更深入地理解量子非经典性与因果结构之间的关系。

Method: 通过对比分析不同因果结构对同一组关联的容纳能力，分类考察：1）一类因果结构中非经典的量子关联是否在另一因果结构中存在无微调的经典解释；2）这些解释是否违反相对论自然约束；3）寻找哪些因果结构具有非经典量子关联，在任何替代因果结构中都无法无微调地经典化解释，类似于Bell因果结构中的情形。

Result: 发现存在因果结构，其中的非经典量子关联可以在另一因果结构中获得无微调的经典解释，且该替代实现不违反相对论自然约束（即存在"伪量子关联"）。另一方面，也找到了其他因果结构，其非经典量子关联在任何替代因果结构中都不存在无微调的经典解释，与Bell因果结构的情形类似。

Conclusion: 因果结构视角下的量子非经典性并非绝对：某些量子关联的"非经典性"是相对于特定因果结构而言的，可以被另一种因果结构自然经典化。然而，存在某些因果结构的非经典量子关联具有鲁棒性，不可在任何替代因果结构中无微调地获得经典解释，表明量子关联的非经典本质在更深的层面仍然成立。

Abstract: In his seminal paper, Bell [Physics Physique Fizika 1, 195 (1964)] considers the correlations that result from space-like separated measurements on a pair of entangled particles. He uses relativity theory to motivate the Bell causal structure, then shows the existence of quantum correlations that cannot be explained classically within this causal structure. Classical explanations of such quantum correlations are possible in alternative causal structures, for instance, those that allow superluminal causal influences, but, as shown in [New Journal of Physics 17 033002 (2015)], all such alternative explanations require fine tuning (causation without correlation). Here we discuss the existence of spurious quantum correlations --- correlations that look quantum in one causal structure, but have a natural classical explanation in another. More precisely, there are causal structures that admit non-classical quantum correlations, but for which the same correlations have a classical explanation in another causal structure without fine tuning. The realisation in the other causal structure can also be achieved without breaking any natural constraints on the causal structure that follow from relativity theory. However, similarly to non-classical quantum correlations in the Bell causal structure, we find other causal structures with non-classical quantum correlations that do not have a classical causal explanation in any alternative causal structure without fine tuning.

</details>


### [135] [Vanilla Exact Synthesis of CNOT Circuits is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04160)
*Chenjian Li,Ji Guan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了基础CNOT综合（vanilla exact CNOT synthesis）的判定问题是NP完全的，从而其优化版本是NP难的。证明通过将网格图上的哈密顿路径问题多项式归约到该问题，分两步实现：先用一元编码将网格图等距嵌入超立方体，再将超立方体哈密顿路径编码为CNOT综合问题。关键技巧是引入记录器量子比特，将中间顶点访问约束融入最终变换，使任何CNOT电路都必须实现预期的路径结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的CNOT综合相关问题虽已被证明计算困难，但其证明都依赖额外结构（如受限比特连接、编码输入或不受限中间变量）。最基本的设置——恒等输入、固定数目标签量子比特、无辅助比特、全连接CNOT——的复杂性此前悬而未决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: (1) 从网格图哈密顿路径问题出发，通过一元编码映射将网格图等距嵌入超立方体中的哈密顿圈问题；(2) 将该超立方体哈密顿路径编码为vanilla精确CNOT综合；(3) 核心难点是CNOT综合只指定最终奇偶矩阵，无法直接强制中间的顶点访问顺序，因此引入额外的记录器（recorder）量子比特来将中间路径访问编码进最终变换矩阵，迫使任何实现电路反映目标路径结构。

Result: 证明了vanilla精确CNOT综合的判定版本为NP完全、优化版本为NP难；并据此直接推导出多个相关问题的困难性，包括GL(n,2)上的最短词问题、Cayley图距离计算、顺序XOR程序最小化以及相位多项式电路的精确综合。

Conclusion: 该结果闭合了CNOT综合复杂度分析中基础情形的一个重要缺口，表明即使在最简单的设置下，精确CNOT综合也是计算困难的，并衍生出对多个代数与量子电路相关问题的复杂度下界。

Abstract: Exact CNOT synthesis asks for a minimum-size CNOT circuit implementing an invertible linear transformation. Although several related synthesis models have been shown to be computationally hard, their hardness proofs rely on additional structure such as restricted qubit connectivity, encoded inputs, or unrestricted intermediate variables. The complexity of the most basic setting---identity input, a fixed number of labelled qubits, no ancillas, and all-to-all CNOT connectivity---had remained unresolved. In this work, we prove that the decision version of this vanilla exact CNOT synthesis problem is NP-complete, and consequently that its optimization version is NP-hard. Our proof gives a polynomial-time reduction from the Hamiltonian-path problem on grid graphs in two steps. First, we isometrically embed the grid graph into a hypercube via a unary encoding map. We then encode this hypercube Hamiltonian path problem into vanilla exact CNOT synthesis. The main challenge is that CNOT synthesis specifies only the final parity matrix and cannot directly enforce the intermediate vertex visits required by a Hamiltonian path. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce extra recorder qubits that encode the required intermediate vertex visits into the final transformation, forcing any CNOT circuit implementation to realize the intended path structure. Beyond CNOT synthesis, our result directly implies hardness for several related problems, including the shortest word problem over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, distance computation on Cayley graphs over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, minimization of sequential XOR programs, and exact synthesis of phase polynomial circuits.

</details>


### [136] [Parameterised graph theory for tensor networks: entanglement rerouting, structural simplification, and agnostic tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04165)
*Matthias C. Caro,Natalie McHugh,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用参数化图理论系统研究了张量网络态（TNS）的表示复杂度与学习复杂度。作者证明cutwidth和tree-cutwidth分别决定了将TNS表示为MPS或TTN所需的bond dimension开销，并基于"纠缠路由"技术给出了证明。此外，他们推导了可实现TNS层析的样本与计算复杂度上界，引入了新图参数"learning complexity"，并将解纠缠MPS学习器扩展到TTN及任意已知图的张量网络。最后扩展到agnostic设置，对任意输入态输出保真度在ε加性误差内的纯态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化图理论已成功用于分析张量网络模拟的复杂度，但其对张量网络表示和张量网络层析（tensor-network tomography）的影响尚未被充分理解。具体而言，哪些图参数决定了TNS能否被有效地表示为MPS或TTN，以及哪些参数控制学习该量子态的复杂度，这些问题此前缺乏系统性的理论刻画。

Method: (1) 引入"纠缠路由"（entanglement rerouting）技术——经典网络中信息路由的张量网络类比，以此证明cutwidth和tree-cutwidth分别界定了TNS转MPS/TTN的bond dimension开销；(2) 将解纠缠MPS学习器（源自Cramer等人2010，后被Bakshi等人和Lin等人进一步分析）推广到TTN及任意已知图的张量网络，得到依赖cutwidth、tree-cutwidth和新参数"learning complexity"的样本及计算复杂度上界；(3) 将该框架扩展到agnostic（非可实现）设置，对任意输入态输出最优TNS近似（保真度ε加性误差内）。

Result: (1) 证明了cutwidth和tree-cutwidth对MPS/TTN表示的bond dimension开销提供了紧致的图上界；(2) 推导了可实现TNS层析的图相关样本与计算复杂度上界，其指数由cutwidth、tree-cutwidth和learning complexity决定，learning complexity又可用度和treewidth来界定；(3) 在agnostic设置下获得了显式的图相关复杂度界，对任意量子态可学习到在保真度ε内的最优张量网络近似态。

Conclusion: 本文建立了张量网络表示与学习复杂度与结构图参数之间的精确联系，表明图拓扑（cutwidth、tree-cutwidth、degree和treewidth）从根本上决定了张量网络态的可表示性与可学习性。这一框架为理解TNS的经典有效表示和层析奠定了理论基础，并提供了agnostic设置下具有显式复杂度界的实用学习算法。

Abstract: Parameterised graph theory studies how the complexity of graph-theoretic problems depends on structural parameters of the input graph. This perspective has proved useful in analysing tensor-network simulation (Markov and Shi, 2008). Its implications for tensor-network representations and tomography are less well understood. In particular, which graph parameters determine whether a tensor-network state (TNS) admits a tractable matrix product state (MPS) or tree tensor network (TTN) representation, and which control the complexity of learning the state?
  We address these questions using parameterised graph theory. First, we show that cutwidth and tree-cutwidth bound the bond dimension overhead required to represent a TNS as an MPS or TTN. In the TTN case, tree-cutwidth also bounds the local dimension of the grouped subsystems. The proofs are based on entanglement rerouting, a tensor-network analogue of rerouting information in a classical network. Second, we derive graph-dependent upper bounds on the sample and computational complexity of realisable TNS tomography, with exponents that depend on cutwidth, tree-cutwidth, and a new graph parameter, learning complexity, which we bound in terms of degree and treewidth. We obtain these results by extending the disentangling MPS learner of (Cramer et al., 2010), as analysed further in (Bakshi et al., 2025; Lin et al., 2025), to TTNs and to tensor networks on arbitrary known graphs. Finally, we extend the framework beyond the realisable setting. For an arbitrary input state, our agnostic learner outputs a pure state whose fidelity is within additive error $ε$ of the optimum over tensor-network states on the given graph with a given bond dimension, with explicit graph-dependent bounds on sample and computational complexity.

</details>


### [137] [Measurements on the separated subsystems of an entangled state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04178)
*Gregory D. Scholes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文解决了复合量子系统中纠缠态在分离子系统的测量问题，提出通过自由向量空间中的陪集来获取分离子系统上测量所检测到的态投影，从而无需引入随机坍缩，并从这一视角重新解释了非局域性的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠的复合量子系统可以被物理分离并对子系进行独立测量，但对分离子系统的测量应作用于各自局部希尔伯特空间中的向量。然而除了可分离态外，如何从张量积基中解析出分离子系统的相关态一直不清楚，这限制了人们对纠缠态下分离测量行为的理解。

Method: 作者从张量积空间所定义的自由向量空间出发，考虑其中对应的（陪集形式）态，证明了任意一般纠缠态中可被分离子系统测量所检测到的投影可以通过这种方法获得。

Result: 该方法成功地从张量积表述中解析出分离子系统测量对应的态投影，消除了随机坍缩假设的必要性，并表明非局域性源于每个分离子系统上的测量对可能测量结果进行投影的方式。

Conclusion: 该工作为纠缠态的分离测量提供了更清晰的数学框架，不需要引入波函数坍缩的随机性，为非局域性提供了一种基于投影机制的新视角。

Abstract: The entangled states of composite quantum systems are well studied. The particle-like nature of these systems also means that, while entangled, they can be physically separated and measurements performed on the separated subsystems. Measurements on the separated subsystems A and B should pertain to vectors in the local Hilbert space of each subsystem, but to date it has not been clear how to elucidate the relevant states of the separated subsystems because it is not obvious how to resolve them from states given in the tensor product basis, except for the separable states. Here it is shown that the projections of any general entangled state that are detected by measurements on the separated subsystems can be obtained considering the corresponding (cosets of) states in the free vector space from which the tensor product space is defined. The result eliminates the need to invoke random collapse, and from this perspective nonlocality arises because of the way measurements on each separated subsystem projects possible measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum communication and Bell nonlocality require infinite classical communication to simulate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04182)
*Carlos de Gois,Thyago S. R. Santos,Carlos Vieira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了量子通信与量子关联的经典模拟问题在所有维度下的完整答案：对于三维（qutrit）系统，构建了一个使用357个经典比特的精确模拟协议；而对于四维及以上系统，证明不存在任何有限经典通信量可以精确模拟，揭示了从维度4开始出现质变过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统虽然可以处于连续统状态，但其承载的经典信息量有限，量子关联虽强于经典关联却不能超光速传输信息。这暗示量子通信和量子关联可能可以用有限经典通信量来模拟。此前只解决了最小的非平凡维度（qubit，需要2个经典比特），更高维度的情形长期悬而未决，本文旨在解决所有量子维度的经典模拟问题。
