Method: 论文采用两部分工作：一是构造性方法，为qutrit通信和关联设计了精确模拟协议（使用357个经典比特）；二是不可能性证明，通过数学论证证明在维度4及以上，即使允许无限共享随机性，任何有限经典通信量都无法精确模拟ququart通信及两个纠缠ququart的所有关联。

Result: 揭示了从维度4开始的意外质的过渡：不存在有限经典通信可精确模拟ququart通信或两个纠缠ququart的所有量子关联。同时为qutrit通信构造了使用357个经典比特的精确模拟协议，从而也能模拟两个纠缠qutrit的所有关联。

Conclusion: 量子通信和量子关联的经典模拟问题在不同维度存在截然不同的答案：qutrit是可精确模拟的（需357个经典比特），而ququart及以上维度则完全不可能用有限经典通信精确模拟。这一过渡出现在维度4而非维度3，出乎物理直觉预期。

Abstract: A quantum system of any fixed dimension can be prepared in a continuum of states, yet it cannot be used to transmit an unlimited amount of classical information. Similarly, the correlations observed between measurement outcomes on separate parts of a shared quantum system can be stronger than classical correlations, but they cannot transmit information. These fundamental limitations suggest that the statistics observed from quantum communication and quantum correlations may admit a simulation using a finite amount of classical communication. This expectation is confirmed in the smallest nontrivial quantum dimension, with two classical bits being necessary and sufficient to exactly simulate qubit communication and all correlations between qubits. Despite significant efforts during the previous decades, this remained the only solved case. Here we resolve both problems for every quantum dimension. The solution reveals an unexpected qualitative transition starting at dimension four: no finite amount of classical communication can exactly simulate ququart communication nor all quantum correlations of two entangled ququarts, even with unlimited shared randomness. One might have expected this transition, if it existed, to appear already for qutrits. Instead, we construct an explicit protocol that exactly simulates qutrit communication using $357$ classical bits, and consequently, all correlations of two entangled qutrits.

</details>

### [139] [Finite-duration non-slow-roll effects in inflationary production of ultralight vector dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02970)
*Imtiaz Khan,Niamat Ullah,Chengxun Yuan,G. Mustafa,Farruh Atamurotov,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究超轻矢量暗物质在暴胀期间的产生机制，指出瞬时质量跃迁会导致紫外振荡与有限时间预测不确定性问题。作者用平滑轮廓替代尖锐跃迁，推导了纵模的动量响应核，发现有限持续时间保留红外增强、抑制可分辨跃迁的模，并引入与轮廓相关的遗迹丰度修正。在固定暗物质丰度后，诱导引力波信号仍保留独立的横-无迹源依赖性，仅靠丰度重新标度无法消除轮廓信息。数值模演化和辐射纪元张量积分支持有限宽度处理，表明跃迁持续时间将微观暴胀动力学与超轻矢量丰度及其低频引力波信号联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻矢量暗物质的暴胀产生传统上采用瞬时质量跃迁模型，但该模型存在紫外振铃和有限时间预测不确定性问题，亟需更物理的平滑跃迁处理来获得可靠的预测。

Method: 将瞬时（尖锐）质量跃迁替换为平滑轮廓，推导纵模的动量响应核；通过数值模演化和辐射纪元张量积分来验证有限宽度处理的有效性，并分析跃迁持续时间对暗物质丰度及诱导引力波信号的影响。

Result: 有限持续时间跃迁保留红外增强机制，同时抑制可分辨跃迁的高频模，产生依赖轮廓形状的遗迹丰度修正。固定暗物质丰度后，诱导引力波信号仍携带横-无迹源的独立依赖，因此仅作丰度重新标度无法去除轮廓信息，跃迁持续时间可影响低频引力波信号。

Conclusion: 暴胀期间的超轻矢量质量跃迁持续时间是连接微观暴胀动力学与宏观暗物质丰度及低频引力波可观测信号的桥梁，平滑跃迁框架解决了尖锐跃迁模型的理论缺陷，为解释和理解低频引力波信号提供了新的物理线索。

Abstract: Inflationary production of ultralight vector dark matter can be amplified by a brief non-slow-roll phase, yet instantaneous mass transitions generate ultraviolet ringing and leave finite-time predictions unresolved. We replace the sharp transition with a smooth profile and derive the momentum-response kernel of the longitudinal mode. Finite duration preserves the infrared enhancement, suppresses modes that resolve the transition, and introduces a profile-dependent correction to the relic abundance. After fixing the dark matter abundance, the induced gravitational-wave signal retains an independent transverse-traceless source dependence, so abundance rescaling alone leaves residual profile information. Numerical mode evolution and radiation-era tensor integration support the finite-width treatment. Transition duration therefore links microscopic inflationary dynamics to ultralight vector abundance and its low-frequency gravitational-wave signal.

</details>


### [140] [Resolving Local Structure of Distribution Functions in Collider Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02972)
*Cong Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究对撞机测量中有限宽度（binning/模糊效应）如何稀释底层分布的窄特征，并提出一类在分布空间具有可计算局域性的观测量。通过构建基线归一化的F_dir(X)，可实现零宽度极限下的局域值提取，其中协方差决定主导偏差，可用于筛选能保留意外精细结构的过程与观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对撞机测量中，数据点采样的是有限区域而非单点，导致底层分布中的窄特征在拟合前被模糊化（blurred）。这种效应使得某些精细结构难以被探测到，因此需要一种系统化的方法来判断哪些观测量和过程能保留这些窄特征，以及如何量化测量有限宽度带来的影响。

Method: 论文证明：对于线性依赖于选定目标分布的观测量，在外部输入约束被吸收为半正定有限响应（nonnegative finite response）之后，其分布空间的局域性由硬动力学和运动学决定。提出了基线归一化的观测量F_dir(X)，其在零宽度极限下给出局域值，在非零宽度时给出受控的平均值，并用协方差作为领先偏差的度量。

Result: 该方法为选择能保留意外精细结构的过程和观测量提供了系统性指导。此外，论文发现EIC（电子-离子对撞机）中的光子辐射观测量能够在实际上实现精确的零宽度极限。

Conclusion: 通过构造基线归一化且线性于目标分布的观测量，可以定量评估测量分辨率对底层精细结构的影响，并识别出能够严格达到零宽度极限的实际可观测量（如EIC光子辐射），为在实验设计中保留窄特征提供了实用准则。

Abstract: Collider measurements may blur narrow features in underlying distributions before fitting, since a data point samples a finite region. We show that observables linear in a selected target distribution, after externally constrained inputs are absorbed into a nonnegative finite response, have a calculable distribution-space locality set by hard dynamics and kinematics. The baseline-normalized $F_{\rm dir}(X)$ yields the local value at zero width and a controlled average otherwise, with covariance setting the leading bias. This guides the choice of processes and observables that retain unexpected fine structure. An EIC photon-radiation observable realizes the exact zero-width limit.

</details>


### [141] [Probing Quantum Foundations in Long-Baseline Neutrino Oscillations: Wave Packet Approach from MINOS Data to DUNE Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02975)
*Baktiar Wasir Farooq,Bipin Singh Koranga,Massimo Blasone*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在波包框架下研究中微子味振荡，推导了包含MSW物质效应、退相干和局域化抑制的全三味振荡概率，并利用MINOS和DUNE数据约束波包宽度。此外，通过量子信息论框架计算波粒纠缠互补量，探索纠缠度、可预测性和可见性之间的三一性关系，证明即使振荡概率与平面波结果不可区分，波包结构仍在量子信息量中留下可观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准平面波处理中微子振荡无法刻画质量本征态的有限空间相干性，而真实的探测过程本质上涉及波包的有限宽度。作者希望建立一个理论上自洽、实验上可检验的波包框架，以弥补平面波方法的不足，并探索在中微子振荡这一宏观量子系统中揭示量子基础物理（如波粒二象性和互补性）的新途径。

Method: 采用波包形式主义推导全三味振荡概率，纳入MSW物质效应、退相干和波包宽度控制的局域化抑制；以MINOS消失数据为基准进行参数约束，并外推到DUNE预言。进一步计算经典Fisher信息以量化数据对波包宽度的敏感性，并通过量子信息论框架计算波粒纠缠互补量，构建纠缠度、可预测性和可见性三者的三一性描述。

Result: 发现Fisher信息对波包宽度的敏感度集中在低能区，该区域正是波包与平面波预言发生分化的地方；即使振荡概率在数值上与平面波结果无法区分，三一性量（纠缠、可预测性、可见性）仍能显示波包结构留下的可观测印记。

Conclusion: 波包方法为长基线大气中微子振荡实验提供了理论自洽且具有实验动机的框架，能够作为探测量子基础物理（特别是波粒二象性与互补性）的有效工具，其敏感性不仅体现在振荡概率中，更体现在量子信息测度上。

Abstract: We investigate neutrino flavor oscillations in the wave packet formalism, extending the standard planewave treatment to incorporate the finite spatial coherence of neutrino mass eigenstates.We derive the full three flavor oscillation probability with MSW matter effects, decoherence, and localization suppression governed by the wave packet width, benchmarking against MINOS disappearance data and extending to DUNE predictions under the same parameters. To quantify how sharply the oscillation data constrain the wave packet width, we compute the classical Fisher information FC as a function of neutrino energy for both MINOS and DUNE, and show that its sensitivity is concentrated precisely in the low-energy regime where the wave-packet and plane-wave predictions diverge. Beyond the oscillation probability analysis, we compute wave particle entanglement complementarity quantities within a quantum information theoretic framework and explore the complete triality description involving entanglement, predictability, and visibility as functions of neutrino energy for both experiments such as MINOS and DUNE. We show that even when the oscillation probability is numerically indistinguishable from the planewave result, the wave packet structure leaves observable imprints in these triality measures. Our results demonstrate that the wave packet approach provides a theoretically consistent and experimentally motivated framework for probing quantum foundations in long-baseline atmospheric neutrino oscillation experiments.

</details>


### [142] [Sub-TeV Singlino Dark Matter in light from Sagittarius A$^\ast$ and LUX-ZEPLIN Nuclear-Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02994)
*Utpal Chattopadhyay,Debottam Das,Rahul Puri,Joydeep Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了银河系中心超大质量黑洞（Sgr A*）周围暗物质密度尖峰以及恒星质量黑洞迷你尖峰对NMSSM中Singlino主导的中性ino暗物质可探测性的影响，通过伽马射线观测来探测子TeV质量的暗物质候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Singlino主导的LSP在传统暗物质晕中产生的间接探测信号很弱，在LHC和直接探测实验中也很难以探测；然而LUX-ZEPLIN实验观察到的248 keV核反冲事件可能暗示存在质量大于200 GeV的Singlino暗物质，因此需要探索其他探测途径。

Method: 利用Sgr A*周围的暗物质密度尖峰（spike）和恒星质量黑洞周围的迷你尖峰（mini-spike）对暗物质密度进行增强，将低质量X射线双星XTE J1118+480周围的sBH纳入考虑，并通过伽马射线观测来评估这些子TeV暗物质场景的可探测前景。

Result: 文章尚未给出具体结果，仅描述了研究方向：考察通过观测Sgr A*和XTE J1118+480中sBH周围区域的伽马射线来探测这些暗物质场景的可能性。

Conclusion: 本文探讨了利用黑洞周围密度增强结构来弥补Singlino暗物质弱间接信号的可探测性挑战，为难以通过传统手段探测的暗物质候选体提供了新的观测策略参考。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of a dark matter density spike surrounding the Milky Way's supermassive black hole (SMBH) on the detectability of Singlino-dominated neutralino dark matter within the Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (NMSSM). Similar density enhancements, or mini-spikes, around stellar-mass black holes (sBHs), have also been considered. Such a dark matter (DM) candidate typically produces weak indirect detection signals in conventional dark matter halos. Additionally, a Singlino-like lightest supersymmetric particle (LSP) is very difficult to probe at the LHC or through the direct DM search experiments. On top of that, recent observation of the LUX-ZEPLIN 248 keV Nuclear-Recoil Event may hint towards a DM that can be accommodated by a Singlino with mass more than 200 GeV. Keeping these in consideration, we examine the prospects for detecting these sub-TeV dark matter scenarios through gamma-ray observations of the regions surrounding the SMBH Sgr A$^\ast$ and the sBH in the low-mass X-ray binary XTE J1118+480.

</details>


### [143] [Yukawa coupling deviations from four-quark interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03000)
*Sophie Renner,Benjamin Smith*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由新物理产生的四夸克相互作用如何通过希格斯-夸克耦合的偏差被探测到，特别是在不耦合第一代夸克的情况下。研究表明，来自味物理和顶夸克对产生的约束很重要，但希格斯-粲夸克和希格斯-底夸克耦合仍是LHC上对某些标量流四夸克算子最灵敏的观测量；在FCC-ee上可测试更多算子和味结构。作者还研究了能产生这些相互作用的简单单粒子模型（第二希格斯二重态、标量和矢量双夸克），发现在某些情况下（尤其是新标量最强耦合到第三代夸克时），汤川耦合的偏差可能是四夸克新物理的首个信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四夸克相互作用的新物理在LHC上可能很难被直接探测到，特别是当它不耦合第一代夸克时。作者旨在探索这些相互作用是否可以通过其在希格斯-夸克耦合中产生的可测量偏差来间接探测，从而为这些难以直接发现的物理场景提供替代搜索策略。

Method: 作者系统分析了四夸克算子对希格斯-夸克耦合（主要是希格斯-粲和希格斯-底）产生的修正效应，对比了LHC上的灵敏度，并考虑来自味物理和顶夸克对产生的约束。他们还研究了FCC-ee（未来正负电子对撞机）对更多算子和味结构的测试能力，并构建了单粒子简化模型（包括第二希格斯二重态、标量和矢量双夸克）来解释这些相互作用的生成机制。

Result: 研究发现，虽然味物理和顶夸克对产生提供了重要约束，但希格斯-粲和希格斯-底耦合在LHC上对某些涉及标量流的四夸克算子仍然是最灵敏的观测量。FCC-ee能够扩展可测试的算子和味结构的范围。在简化模型研究中，发现某些情况下（特别是新标量最强耦合到第三代夸克时），汤川耦合偏差可能成为四夸克新物理的最早信号。

Conclusion: 四夸克相互作用的新物理虽然难以直接探测，但在特定条件下可以通过希格斯-夸克耦合的偏差间接观测到。希格斯汤川耦合的精确测量（在LHC和未来的FCC-ee）可以作为探测这类新物理的灵敏途径，尤其是对耦合到第三代夸克的新标量场景。

Abstract: New physics generating four-quark interactions can be difficult to detect at the LHC, especially if it does not couple to first generation quarks. We investigate the possible role of such interactions in generating measurable deviations in Higgs-quark couplings. We find important constraints from flavour physics and top-pair production, but show that Higgs-charm and Higgs-bottom couplings remain the most sensitive LHC observables to some four-quark operators involving scalar currents. At FCC-ee, more operators and flavour structures can be tested. We further investigate simple single-particle models that can generate these interactions, including a second Higgs doublet, and scalar and vector diquarks. We find that in some cases, including new scalars most strongly coupled to the third generation of quarks, deviations in Yukawa couplings could offer the first signs of new physics generating four-quark interactions.

</details>


### [144] [A Task Force on Strong Coupling Determinations from Event Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03002)
*Anthony Badea,Luca Buonocore,Gherardo Vita,Simone Alioli,Zeno Capatti,Yi Chen,David d'Enterria,Thomas Gehrmann,Andre H. Hoang,Alexander Huss,Sebastian Jaskiewicz,Alexander Karlberg,Yen-Jie Lee,Michelangelo L. Mangano,Vicent Mateu,Jiahao Miao,Pier Francesco Monni,Paolo Nason,Luca Rottoli,Iain W. Stewart,Paolo Torrielli,Giulia Zanderighi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文档概述了成立专门工作组（Task Force）以协调社区力量解决强耦合常数α_s从事件形状观测中提取时存在的显著分歧问题，并报告了2025年11月在CERN举行的首次两天会议，总结了科学讨论、优先实验分析和理论社区的具体任务清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合常数α_s是标准模型的基本参数，其精确确定对大型强子对撞机及未来实验（如FCC）至关重要。事件形状和关联函数观测量为提取α_s提供了最干净的环境，但不同确定方法之间在中心值和不确定度上持续存在显著分歧，亟需协调一致的努力来解决这些问题。

Method: 作者提议建立专门工作组以协调社区范围的合作，并在CERN组织了首次两天会议（2025年11月），会议内容包括科学讨论、识别优先开展实验分析的领域，以及为理论社区制定具体任务清单以供后续会议准备。

Result: 报告了首次工作组会议的结果，包括对科学讨论的总结、被识别为优先事项的实验分析列表，以及理论社区需要执行的具体任务清单。会议确认了α_s不同测量之间存在的系统性差异问题，并确立了后续协调行动的方向。

Conclusion: 建立专门工作组是解决α_s事件形状测定分歧的有效途径。通过社区协调、明确优先实验分析和理论任务分配，有望在未来提高α_s测定的精度和一致性，为QCD的精确与发现前沿奠定更可靠的基础。

Abstract: The strong coupling constant $α_s$ is a fundamental parameter of the Standard Model. Its precise determination is essential for accurately predicting, studying, and understanding processes at the Large Hadron Collider and future experiments such as the Future Circular Collider. Event shape and correlator observables measured at electron-positron colliders provide one of the cleanest environments for extracting $α_s$, thanks to their sensitivity to $α_s$ and the availability of high-precision data from the Large Electron-Positron Collider. More broadly, such observables provide an ideal setting to develop and test our understanding of the perturbative and non-perturbative elements of Quantum Chromodynamics, which will underpin the field's precision and discovery frontiers for decades to come. Despite these advances, significant discrepancies persist between different determinations of $α_s$ from event shapes, both in the extracted central values and estimated uncertainties. This document motivates the establishment of a dedicated Task Force to coordinate a community-wide effort addressing these open questions. We report on the first two-day meeting held at CERN in November 2025, summarizing the scientific discussion and documenting the experimental analyses identified as priorities during the meeting, as well as the concrete list of tasks to be carried out by the theory community in preparation for future meetings.

</details>


### [145] [Strong Constraints for Line Signals from Dark Matter Annihilation in Sub-halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03016)
*Asier Salces Pérez,Thong T. Q. Nguyen,Pedro De la Torre Luque,Tim Linden*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 作者利用近15年的Fermi-LAT Pass 8数据，对一个近期提出的邻近暗物质子结构候选体进行了单色伽马射线发射的专门搜索。在10-300 GeV能量范围内未发现统计显著的线状信号，最大超出出现在E_γ≈28 GeV处，局部显著性为2.5σ。作者据此推导了χχ→γγ湮灭截面的95%置信水平上限，结果显示在假定NFW子结构模型下，约束强于现有的银心线状信号限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质子结构候选体为测试暗物质性质提供了除银河系中心之外的独立探测目标。线状伽马射线信号（如χχ→γγ）是暗物质湮灭的"冒烟枪"特征，对找寻暗物质信号至关重要，因此需要在这种新提出的子结构候选体上开展专门搜索以检验其暗物质解释的可能性。

Method: 使用近15年的Fermi-LAT Pass 8数据，在10-300 GeV能量范围采用滑动窗口方法搜索伽马射线谱线信号；对最大超出位置计算局域显著性；在一定信号模型假设下推导湮灭截面的95%置信水平上限，并将线状约束转换到Higgs-portal和Wino等标准WIMP模型参数空间。

Result: 未发现任何统计显著的线状信号；最大超出在E_γ≈28 GeV处，局部显著性仅2.5σ；在NFW子结构模型假设下，获得的湮灭截面约束在大部分所探索的质量区间内强于已有银心线状信号限制，并对热冻结化学平衡兼容的Higgs-portal、Wino暗物质以及银河系中心过剩暗物质解释的相关参数区域施加了新的限制。

Conclusion: 邻近暗物质子结构候选体未展现出可确认的暗物质线状信号证据，但该目标仍为暗物质搜索提供了有效的探测途径，所获得的约束可显著压缩若干WIMP模型（如Higgs-portal和Wino模型）以及银河系中心过剩暗物质解释中此前尚未被排除的参数空间。

Abstract: We present a dedicated search for monochromatic gamma-ray emission from a recently proposed nearby dark matter subhalo candidate. Using nearly 15 years of Fermi-LAT Pass 8 data, we perform a sliding-window search for gamma-ray lines between 10 and 300 GeV. We do not find any statistically significant evidence for a line signal. The largest excess occurs at $E_γ\simeq 28 \, {\rm GeV}$ with a local significance of $2.5σ$. We therefore derive 95% confidence level upper limits on the annihilation cross section for $χχ\rightarrow γγ$. Under the assumed NFW subhalo model, the resulting constraints are stronger than existing Galactic Center line limits over much of the explored mass range. We further translate these line constraints into bounds on well-motivated WIMP models, such as Higgs-portal and Wino dark matter scenarios, restricting previously open parameter regions consistent with thermal freeze-out as well as models associated with dark matter interpretations of the Galactic Center Excess.

</details>


### [146] [Energy-Energy Correlators in $Z$-Tagged Jets in $pp$ Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03072)
*João Barata,Zhong-Bo Kang,Xoán Mayo López,Jani Penttala,Yiyu Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了在质子-质子碰撞中Z标记喷注内部共线能量-能量关联函数（EEC）跨禁闭相变的预测，结合横动量依赖（TMD）因子化框架与NLL'精度的EEC喷注函数，预测了8和13 TeV下不同横动量区间的分布，并给出风味分解与普适性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是为非微扰夸克碎裂提供一个干净的探针，并直接检验从e+e-对撞中提取的特征性禁闭标度在强子环境（pp碰撞）中是否保持普适性，从而深入理解色禁闭相变与QCD的非微扰行为。

Method: 将TMD因子化用于背对背Z+jet产生的框架与一个包含微扰演化和味道依赖非微扰碎裂的微分EEC喷注函数相结合；EEC喷注函数在NLL'精度下求值，夸克与胶子的非微扰标度分别由现有e+e-和包含喷注数据约束。

Result: EEC分布作为z_χ的函数在小z_χ区域呈现有限平台，相应的角分布展现出与禁闭相变相关的特征性拐折；风味分解显示随着喷注横动量增加，禁闭相变逐渐由夸克起始喷注主导。

Conclusion: 前向Z标记喷注提供了对非微扰夸克碎裂的干净探针，并可直接检验从e+e-碰撞中提取的特征性禁闭标度在强子环境中是否具有普适性。

Abstract: We present predictions for the collinear energy-energy correlator (EEC) measured inside $Z$-tagged jets in proton-proton collisions across the confinement transition. Our framework combines transverse-momentum-dependent factorization for back-to-back $Z+$jet production with an exclusive differential EEC jet function incorporating perturbative evolution and flavor-dependent non-perturbative fragmentation. The EEC jet function is evaluated at $\mathrm{NLL}^{\prime}$ accuracy, with separate quark and gluon non-perturbative scales constrained by existing $e^+e^-$ and inclusive-jet data. We provide predictions for forward $Z+$jet production at $\sqrt{s}=8~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $13~\mathrm{TeV}$ in the jet transverse-momentum intervals $(20, 30)$, $(30, 50)$ and $(50, 100) \, \mathrm{GeV}$. The EEC distributions as functions of $z_χ$ exhibit finite plateaus at small $z_χ$, while the corresponding angular distributions display the characteristic turnover associated with the confinement transition. A flavor decomposition shows that this transition becomes increasingly dominated by quark-initiated jets as the jet transverse momentum increases. Forward $Z$-tagged jets therefore provide a clean probe of non-perturbative quark fragmentation and offer a direct test of whether the characteristic confinement scale extracted from $e^+e^-$ collisions remains universal in a hadronic environment.

</details>


### [147] [Quark Mixing Model with Three Higgs Doublets and $S_3$ Modular Symmetry at $τ=i$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03204)
*Carlos Cerón,Myriam Mondragón*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个包含三个电弱双重态希格斯粒子并辅以模S₃对称性的模型中的夸克扇区。通过合理分配夸克和希格斯场在S₃下的表示及模权重，构造了具有现象学可行纹理的质量矩阵。结合标量势极小化条件得出的希格斯VEV约束以及固定点τ=i处的Yukawa耦合评估，大幅减少了自由参数。提出了两个方案：一个只有3个有效参数但过度约束，另一个有4个有效参数可精确拟合VCKM矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用模对称性（特别是模S₃）来构建夸克质量矩阵的具体纹理，以期在减少自由参数的同时解释费米子质量和味混合现象，从而为超越标准模型的新物理提供可行框架。

Method: 构造一个含三个希格斯双重态的模型，赋予其模S₃对称性；为夸克场和希格斯场指定合适的S₃表示和模权重；从标量势极小化推导希格斯VEV约束条件，在固定点τ=i处评估Yukawa耦合，从而减少自由参数并检验两种参数设置的可行性。

Result: 实现了具有现象学可行纹理的夸克质量矩阵。在固定点τ=i处结合标量势极小化条件后，自由参数显著减少。提出了两种设置：3个有效参数的方案过于约束，无法满足所有实验数据；4个有效参数的方案则能精确拟合VCKM矩阵。

Conclusion: 模S₃对称性与三重希格斯双重态模型的结合为夸克味物理提供了一种有前景的框架，其中4个有效参数的设置能够精确再现夸克混合矩阵，展现了模对称性在压缩标准模型参数空间方面的潜力。

Abstract: We study the quark sector of a model with three Higgs electroweak doublets, complemented with a modular $S_3$ symmetry. We show that a proper assignment of the quark and Higgs fields in $S_3$ and their modular weights allows to write a mass matrix with a phenomenologically viable texture. Moreover, by considering the conditions on the Higgs VEVs derived from the minimization of the scalar potential together with evaluation of the Yukawa couplings at the fixed point $τ=i$, leads to a significant reduction of the free parameters. We present two setups, one with three effective parameters, which is overly constrained, and one with four effective parameters, where the $V_{CKM}$ matrix can be fitted precisely.

</details>


### [148] [Statistical Symmetry Breaking and Emergent Colored Noise in a Stochastic Scalar-Doublet Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03312)
*Pei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个相对论性随机场论，其中复标量二重态与复白噪声源耦合。该作用在统计层面上保持洛伦兹对称性和U(1)×SU(2)对称性，但欧拉-拉格朗日方程在单个随机实现中表现出对称性破缺。标量二重态在场空间中发生噪声驱动的随机游走，产生有限且随时间变化的系综平均模量。与费米子通过汤川耦合后，标量场解分离为尾巴分量（表现为有效质量项）和光锥分量（表现为诱导费米子波函数空间相关随机相位的色噪声源）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索相对论性随机场论中统计对称性与个体实现对称性破缺之间的关系，并研究随机动力学如何产生有效的质量项和色噪声，为理解基础物理中对称性破缺与随机性的内在联系提供理论框架。

Method: 构建一个复标量二重态耦合复白噪声源的相对论性随机场论，在无规范场的简化扇区内求解析解；通过系综平均分析标量场的随机游走性质；引入汤川耦合研究标量场对费米子动力学的色噪声效应；在解析近似下推导色噪声的统计特性与相关长度。

Result: 证明了标量二重态在场空间中发生噪声驱动的随机游走，系综平均模量有限且随时间演化；在与费米子汤川耦合时，标量场解自然分离为两个分量——尾巴分量贡献有效质量类项，光锥分量充当诱导费米子波函数中空间相关随机相位的色噪声源；该色噪声的统计性质和相关长度在所述近似下可解析推导。

Conclusion: 该工作为相对论性随机标量二重态场论中的统计对称性破缺和涌现色噪声动力学提供了探索性研究，揭示了随机噪声在统计层面保持对称性、而在个体实现中破坏对称性的机制，并展示了随机标量场如何通过汤川耦合对费米子产生有效质量贡献和色噪声效应。

Abstract: We investigate a relativistic stochastic field theory in which a complex scalar doublet is coupled to a complex white-noise source. The action preserves Lorentz and $\mathrm{U(1)}\times\mathrm{SU(2)}$ symmetries at the statistical level, whereas the corresponding Euler-Lagrange equations exhibit symmetry breaking along individual stochastic realizations. Within a gauge-field-free sector introduced to obtain analytical solutions, we show that the scalar doublet undergoes a noise-driven random walk in field space, leading to a finite, time-dependent ensemble average of its magnitude. As an illustrative application, we further investigate the coupling of the stochastic scalar field to fermions through a Yukawa interaction. The scalar-field solution naturally separates into a tail component, which contributes as an effective mass-like term, and a light-cone component, which acts as a colored-noise source that induces a spatially correlated stochastic phase in the fermion wave function. The statistical properties and correlation length of this emergent colored noise are derived analytically within the adopted approximations. The present work provides an exploratory study of statistical symmetry breaking and emergent colored-noise dynamics in a relativistic stochastic scalar-doublet field theory.

</details>


### [149] [Leading-Neutron Electroproduction at HERA and the EIC: Sullivan Process, Target Fragmentation, and Pion PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03368)
*Wen-Chen Chang,Chia-Yu Hsieh,Satyajit Puhan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Pythia事件生成器对HERA实验中领头中子电产生过程中的目标碎裂（target fragmentation）贡献进行建模，证明其与单π交换（OPE）贡献的结合能够在整个测量的x_L范围内复现HERA数据特征，无需额外归一化因子，从而展示了将更广泛的领头中子数据纳入未来全局分析以扩展对更小x_π敏感性的潜力，并为未来EIC实验提供了领头中子产生的前景预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对HERA领头中子数据的分析主要集中在大x_L区域以抑制DIS目标碎裂贡献，从而限制了可利用的数据范围和可探测的π动量分数x_π区间。作者希望通过更好地建模目标碎裂贡献，使更广泛的数据范围能够被纳入全局拟合，从而扩展对π子部分子分布函数（PDFs）的约束能力。

Method: 使用Pythia事件生成器对DIS目标碎裂贡献进行建模，并将其与Sullivan单π交换框架下的OPE贡献相结合，与HERA H1和ZEUS实验的领头中子电产生数据在整个x_L范围内进行比较。同时研究了π-核子顶点形状因子的模型依赖性，并对未来EIC实验中不同束流能量和运动学区域的领头中子产生进行了投影研究。

Result: OPE贡献与Pythia建模的目标碎裂贡献之组合能够复现HERA领头中子数据的主要特征（无需额外的ad hoc归一化因子）。HERA数据对Pythia中不同目标碎裂处理方式敏感，且对π-核子顶点形状因子存在模型依赖性。EIC投影识别出了对π结构灵敏度增强同时抑制DIS目标碎裂贡献的束流能量配置和运动学区域。

Conclusion: 通过Pythia对目标碎裂贡献的建模，扩大了可用的领头中子数据范围，为未来全局分析提供了对小x_π的更敏感约束。同时，EIC的特定束流配置有望为π子PDFs提供进一步增强的测量精度。

Abstract: Leading-neutron electroproduction measurements from the H1 and ZEUS experiments at HERA have been used to constrain pion parton distribution functions (PDFs) at small momentum fractions within the Sullivan one-pion-exchange (OPE) framework, complementing large-$x_π$ constraints from pion-induced Drell--Yan measurements. Previous analyses have focused primarily on the region of large neutron longitudinal momentum fraction, $x_L$, where contributions from deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) target fragmentation are suppressed. In this work, we use the \textsc{Pythia} event generator to model the target-fragmentation contribution and show that its combination with the OPE contribution reproduces the main features of the HERA leading-neutron data over the full measured $x_L$ range without introducing additional ad hoc normalization factors. This result demonstrates the potential of incorporating a broader range of leading-neutron data into future global analyses, thereby extending sensitivity to smaller pion momentum fractions $x_π$. We investigate the model dependence associated with the pion--nucleon vertex form factor and show that the HERA data are sensitive to different target-fragmentation treatments implemented in \textsc{Pythia}. Finally, we present projections for leading-neutron production at the future U.S. Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), identifying beam-energy configurations and kinematic regions that provide enhanced sensitivity to pion structure while suppressing DIS target-fragmentation contributions.

</details>


### [150] [Meson mass spectrum in isospin QCD medium from the $N_f=2+1$ quark-meson model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03385)
*Yu-Han Gao,Xu-Guang Huang,Daiki Suenaga*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在N_f=2+1夸克-介子模型中，引入Kobayashi-Maskawa-'t Hooft型耦合以考虑U(1)_A轴反常效应，在平均场近似和夸克单圈水平下研究了有限温度与有限等旋化学势下QCD的相结构、介子质量谱和声速。研究发现：低温下π凝聚（超流相）的发生为二级相变，高温下可能展示一级相变特征；超流相中π_+质量为精确零（Nambu-Goldstone玻色子），而中性π介子质量在凝聚相中随μ_I严格线性增长；等温声速平方在相变附近呈现特征结构，且增强的U(1)_A反常效应会使冷介质中凝聚产生的声速峰变得不那么显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统地理解有限温度和等旋化学势下QCD的相结构、介子质量谱及声速行为，特别是U(1)_A轴反常对π凝聚（超流相变）的影响，为未来格点QCD模拟提供对称性方面的有用信息和理论预言。

Method: 采用N_f=2+1夸克-介子模型，加入Kimura-Maskawa-'t Hooft型耦合以体现U(1)_A轴反常的动态效应；在平均场近似和夸克单圈（one-loop）水平下进行分析，研究π凝聚的onset条件及相变性质，计算标量和赝标量单态-八重态介子质量谱及等温声速。

Result: 低温下π凝聚表现为二级相变，高温时可能呈现一级相变行为；超流相中π_+为精确无质量的Nambu-Goldstone玻色子，中性π介子质量在凝聚相中随μ_I严格线性增长；等温声速平方在相变附近呈现特征结构，增强的U(1)_A反常会削弱冷介质中声速峰。

Conclusion: 本文揭示的相结构、介子质量谱和声速特征可为未来格点QCD模拟提供对称性方面的参考，有助于更深入理解等旋化学势下QCD中轴反常、π凝聚与声速之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We study phase structures, meson mass spectra, and sound velocities at finite temperature and density in QCD with isospin chemical potential ($μ_I$). We employ the quark-meson model with $N_f=2+1$, incorporating the Kobayashi-Maskawa-'t Hooft type coupling to capture dynamical effects of the $U(1)_A$ axial anomaly. Within the mean-field approximation at quark one-loop, we analyze the onset of pion condensation, which manifests as a second-order phase transition at low temperatures and may exhibit a first-order behavior at higher temperatures within this approximation. We examine the corresponding mass spectra of scalar and pseudoscalar singlet-octet mesons, in which the $π_+$ mass is exactly massless in the superfluid phase due to its Nambu-Goldstone boson nature. The neutral pion mass is, meanwhile, found to exhibit a strictly linear growth with $μ_I$ in the pion condensed phase. We also investigate the isothermal squared sound velocity and identify characteristic structures associated with the phase transitions. Furthermore, we highlight how enhanced $U(1)_A$ anomaly effects facilitate the pion condensate to generate a less-pronounced sound velocity peak in cold medium. Our findings are expected to provide future lattice simulations with useful information on meson mass spectra from symmetry aspects.

</details>


### [151] [Exploring millicharged particles in laboratory and astrophysical strong-field regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03399)
*Cheng-Rui Jiang,Tong Li,Kai Ma,Haolong Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强电磁场环境中探测和约束轻质量毫电荷粒子（MCPs）的可能性，涵盖实验室激光实验和磁星等天体物理环境两种途径。作者提出了通过相对论电子束与高强度激光脉冲相互作用的非线性康普顿散射产生MCP对的理论框架，同时重新审视了磁星极隙中通过Schwinger机制产生MCP对的过程，发现两者在约束能力上具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型之外的轻暗物质粒子尤其是毫电荷粒子在强场环境中的探测具有重要意义。作者旨在探索新的搜索通道和约束手段，将实验室激光实验与天体物理观测相结合，以覆盖对MCPs的探测盲区。

Method: 采用Furry图像和Dirac方程的Volkov解来描述外部经典激光场背景下的电子和MCPs；计算非线性康普顿散射产生MCP对的截面，并考虑带中微子丢失的不可约标准模型本底；基于Ruderman-Sutherland模型重新评估磁星极隙中Schwinger机制产生MCP对导致的能量损失和电场加速效应。

Result: 论文给出了非线性康普顿散射产生MCP对的截面计算及标准模型本底分析结果，同时基于已确认磁星的数据给出了Schwinger机制产生MCP对的能量损失约束。核心发现是高磁化磁星的约束与实验室激光实验的搜索潜力具有互补性。

Conclusion: 高度磁化磁星所提供的天体物理约束与实验室激光实验方案的搜索能力在参数空间上覆盖互补区域，两者结合可更全面地限制轻毫电荷粒子的参数空间。

Abstract: The probe of light dark particles beyond the Standard Model (SM) under a strong-field environment has drawn significant attention. In this work, we investigate the potential to search for and constrain light millicharged particles (MCPs) via strong electromagnetic fields in both laboratory laser experiments and astrophysical environments such as magnetars. We propose the MCP pair production from nonlinear Compton scattering through the interaction of a relativistic electron beam with a high-intensity laser pulse. The Furry picture and Volkov solution of Dirac equation in a background electromagnetic field are used to describe the electrons and MCPs under an external classical laser field. We calculate the cross sections of nonlinear Compton scattering to MCP pairs and take into account the irreducible SM background with missing neutrinos. We also revisit the MCP pair production via the Schwinger mechanism from magnetars with ultra-strong magnetic field and parallel electric field in polar gap. The energy loss due to the Schwinger pair production of MCPs and electric field acceleration is evaluated based on Ruderman-Sutherland model for confirmed magnetars. We find that the constraints from highly magnetized magnetars and the search potential in laboratory laser experiments are complementary.

</details>


### [152] [CGC-py: A Monte Carlo Event Generator for Gluon Saturation Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03434)
*Haowu Duan,Cong Yi,Si-Wei Dai,Shu-Yi Wei,Wenbin Zhao,Liang Zheng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了CGC-py，一个用于深非弹性散射的蒙特卡洛事件生成器，将完整的Color Glass Condensate（CGC）截面与Parton-Branching横向动量依赖的后向初态簇射耦合，由Pythia 8处理末态辐射和强子化。通过解析闭合检验和与H1数据对比验证了生成器，并研究了核修正因子和双强子背对背宽度随能量的增长机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要构建一个能够一致地生成单强子和双强子观测量的事件生成器，以在电子离子对撞机（EIC）运动学范围内研究胶子饱和效应，并区分CGC小-x演化与DGLAP共线动力学对观测量增长的贡献。

Method: 将完整的γ*p(A)→qq̄+X CGC截面（保留靶弹性与非弹性贡献，不取背对背关联极限）耦合至Parton-Branching横向动量依赖的后向初态簇射；采用Pythia 8处理末态辐射和强子化；通过解析闭合检验验证单含夸克谱，并对ep碰撞的带电强子谱与H1数据对比；采用Pythia 6基线和x_g重标度研究区分CGC与DGLAP贡献。

Result: 生成器与H1数据表现出很好的一致性；核修正因子R_eAu^h呈现预期饱和图形：低p_T^*处抑制，高p_T^*处趋近于1；发现小-x CGC演化和共线DGLAP动力学对双强子离侧宽度随能量的增长贡献相当，真正的饱和驱动展宽仅在最高能量下出现；eAu碰撞相对ep表现出增强的离侧宽度和抑制的背对背产额，但在EIC运动学范围内核效应仍较为温和。

Conclusion: CGC-py能够在统一的框架下为各种观测量提供自洽的事件生成能力，但EIC标准运动学下胶子饱和信号较为有限，建议在最向前可及的运动学区域进行测量，并采用互补观测量以最大化对胶子饱和的敏感性。

Abstract: We develop CGC-py, a Monte Carlo event generator for deep-inelastic scattering. It couples the full Color Glass Condensate (CGC) cross section for $γ^*p(A)\to q\bar q+X$ to a Parton-Branching transverse-momentum-dependent backward initial-state shower, while \textsc{Pythia}~8 handles final-state radiation and hadronization. CGC-py retains the complete target-elastic and target-inelastic contributions without taking the back-to-back correlation limit, allowing single- and di-hadron observables to be generated consistently from the same event sample. We validate the generator through an analytic closure test of the single-inclusive quark spectrum and a comparison of charged-hadron spectra in $ep$ collisions with H1 data, finding excellent agreement. The predicted nuclear modification factor $R_{e\mathrm{Au}}^h$ shows the expected saturation pattern: suppression at low $p_T^*$ followed by a rise toward unity at higher $p_T^*$. A comparison with a \textsc{Pythia}~6 baseline, together with an $x_g$-rescaling study, indicates that small-$x$ CGC evolution and collinear DGLAP dynamics contribute comparably to the growth of the dihadron away-side width with energy. Genuine saturation-driven broadening emerges only at the highest energies considered. Within CGC-py, $e\mathrm{Au}$ collisions exhibit an enhanced away-side width and a suppressed back-to-back yield relative to $ep$ collisions. These nuclear effects remain modest over EIC kinematics, motivating measurements at the most forward accessible kinematics and the use of complementary observables to maximize sensitivity to gluon saturation.

</details>


### [153] [A Bayesian Pixel Based Approach for Model Independent TMD Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03646)
*Marco Zaccheddu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于非参数像素化的贝叶斯推断框架，用于横向动量依赖（TMD）部分子分布的成像。该方法将Collins-Soper-Sterman形式论中的TMD演化集成到可微分框架中，并利用生成式AI（混合归一化流驱动的Metropolis-Hastings算法）实现高效后验采样。通过多尺度closure测试验证，并利用奇异值分解表征TMD中的零模（无观测约束的功能分量），证明多尺度数据可打破这些简并性，实现三维部分子成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMD部分子分布在描述三维核子结构（横向动量与纵向动量）中至关重要，但现有推断方法在非参数化、高维后验采样及处理TMD演化复杂度方面存在瓶颈。同时，观测对TMD的限制具有内在简并性（存在"零模"），需要系统性的贝叶斯框架来表征并解决这一问题。

Method: 采用非参数像素化表示TMD，将Collins-Soper-Sterman（CSS）形式论中的TMD演化嵌入可微分框架；提出混合归一化流辅助的Metropolis-Hastings算法以提高高维后验采样效率；通过多尺度closure测试（逐步增加复杂度）验证框架可靠性；运用奇异值分解（SVD）系统表征观测无法约束的TMD零模子空间。

Result: 通过多尺度closure测试验证了框架的正确性与收敛性；SVD分析揭示并表征了TMD中存在不可由观测约束的零模功能分量；证明了引入多尺度数据能有效打破观测简并性，使得这些零模被约束，从而实现完整的三维部分子成像。

Conclusion: 该框架为TMD部分子的贝叶斯推断与三维核子成像提供了新的非参数工具，弥合了生成式AI、贝叶斯推断与高能物理之间的鸿沟；多尺度实验数据是打破TMD观测简并性、实现完整三维结构成像的关键。

Abstract: We introduce a nonparametric pixel-based framework for the Bayesian inference and imaging of transverse momentum dependent (TMD) parton distributions. The methodology integrates TMD evolution within the Collins-Soper-Sterman formalism in a differentiable framework, and leverages generative AI through a hybrid normalizing flow-driven Metropolis-Hastings algorithm for efficient posterior sampling. The framework is validated through multi-scale closure tests of increasing complexity. Using singular value decomposition, we characterize the existence of null TMDs, functional components that remain unconstrained by observables, and demonstrate how multi-scale data break these degeneracies, enabling 3D partonic imaging.

</details>


### [154] [Light Leptoquarks in a Dark Sector: Scalar Dark Matter, Neutrino Mass, and Collider Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03648)
*Priyotosh Bandyopadhyay,Kirtiman Ghosh,Anirban Karan,Snehashis Parashar,Debabrata Sahoo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个暗轻子夸克（LQ）模型，其中暗LQ和暗矢量夸克参与单圈辐射产生马约拉纳中微子质量，并以一个Z2奇性的单态标量作为暗物质候选者。由于LQ的Z2奇性禁阻其常规衰变通道，使其可绕过标准LHC约束，从而允许亚TeV暗LQ与TeV级暗矢量夸克共存。亚TeV暗LQ通过共湮灭帮助实现观测到的单态暗物质丰度，文章分析了相应暗物质现象学并评估了在未来缪子对撞机上探测该模型的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的轻子夸克模型受限于标准LHC搜索约束，难以存在亚TeV质量尺度的LQ；同时需要寻找既能解释暗物质和中微子质量，又能在未来对撞机上有可探测信号的统一框架。该工作旨在规避这些约束，探索新的物理可能性与探测前景。

Method: 构建包含暗LQ、暗矢量夸克和Z2-奇性单态标量的模型，通过单圈图辐射产生马约拉纳中微子质量；利用Z2对称性使LQ失去常规衰变模式以规避LHC约束；通过共湮灭机制计算暗物质 relic density；并使用未来缪子对撞机的模拟来分析其探测灵敏度。

Result: 该框架允许亚TeV暗LQ与TeV级暗矢量夸克共存的独特参数空间；亚TeV暗LQ通过共湮灭有效实现观测到的单态暗物质热残余丰度；展示了未来缪子对撞机对这一模型的探测前景。

Conclusion: 该模型为暗物质、中微子质量和对撞机现象学提供了一体化的自洽解释，特别是亚TeV暗LQ因绕过LHC约束而存活，并有望在未来缪子对撞机实验中得到检验。

Abstract: We investigate the dark matter (DM) phenomenology and subsequent collider signatures of a dark leptoquark (LQ) model containing dark vector-like quarks (VLQs) and a scalar singlet. The dark LQs and VLQs participate in the radiative generation of Majorana neutrino masses at one loop. Since the coloured LQs and VLQs cannot serve as viable DM candidates, a $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd singlet scalar is introduced as the DM candidate. The $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd nature of the LQs forbids their conventional decays into a quark and a lepton, allowing them to evade the standard LHC constraints that apply to visible LQ signatures. This framework therefore offers the distinctive possibility of sub-TeV dark LQs coexisting with TeV-scale dark VLQs. These sub-TeV dark LQs help in achieving the observed relic abundance of the singlet through co-annihilation. We analyse the resulting DM phenomenology and assess the prospects for probing this scenario at a future muon collider.

</details>


### [155] [Probing the Geometry of Viable Froggatt-Nielsen-like Flavor Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03812)
*Davide Meloni*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在可控的玩具景观中研究可行的Froggatt-Nielsen类味道纹理的几何结构。每个点由上下类型Yukawa矩阵中的18个整数指数表示，通过质量比和CKM可观测量的截断施加可行性约束。主成分分析和本征维度估计均表明原始指数空间缺乏强线性压缩，有效维度高达10-12，提示原始指数可能不是揭示隐藏味道几何的自然坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Froggatt-Nielsen类味道模型的参数空间几何结构，检验是否可以通过纹理层面的数据分析揭示隐藏的味道理论几何，为理解标准模型中味道结构的起源提供线索。

Method: 构建四种基准系综（从仅质量约束到包含全部CKM可观测量），每个点用18个整数Yukawa指数表示；采用主成分分析（PCA）检查线性压缩程度，并利用TwoNN和Levina-Bickel等最近邻本征维数估计器诊断内蕴维度。

Result: 所有基准中需15个主成分才能解释90%的方差，表明无强线性压缩；TwoNN和Levina-Bickel估计给出10-12的高有效维度，与PCA结论定性一致。

Conclusion: 原始Froggatt-Nielsen指数空间可能不是揭示隐藏味道几何的自然坐标；结果不排除在其他坐标系中存在隐藏的味道理论几何，需要寻找更合适的坐标表示。

Abstract: We investigate the geometry of viable Froggatt--Nielsen-like flavor textures in a controlled toy landscape. In this first texture-level analysis, each point is represented by the eighteen integer exponents entering the up- and down-type Yukawa matrices, without imposing the additional charge-factorization constraints of specific Froggatt--Nielsen models. Viability is imposed through cuts on quark mass ratios and selected CKM observables. We construct four benchmark ensembles, ranging from masses-only constraints to the inclusion of the full set of CKM observables considered in this work, and study the resulting point clouds in the raw exponent space. Principal-component analysis shows no evidence for strong linear compression: in all benchmarks, fifteen principal components are required to account for 90% of the variance. Nearest-neighbour intrinsic-dimensionality diagnostics, including TwoNN and Levina--Bickel estimators, support the same qualitative conclusion, yielding high effective dimensions of order 10-12. These results do not rule out hidden flavor-theory geometry in other coordinates; rather, they suggest that raw Froggatt--Nielsen exponents may not provide natural coordinates for revealing such a geometry.

</details>


### [156] [Exploring Extended Higgs Dynamics via Higgsstrahlung at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04039)
*Anisha,Francisco Arco,Stefano Di Noi,Christoph Englert,Margarete Mühlleitner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来正负电子对撞机FCC-ee通过Z玻色子伴随的Higgs产生过程，在双Higgs二重态模型（2HDM）框架下，以次领头阶精度探索超出标准模型（BSM）的Higgs扇区物理，展示了FCC-ee在240 GeV运行模式下对alignment极限附近的BSM相关Higgs相互作用的精确约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC-ee将以空前精度探测Higgs玻色子和电弱尺度的物理，但需要一个具体且参数空间紧凑的理论框架来评估其精度约束对BSM物理的敏感度。2HDM提供了这样一个最小可行的框架，同时其关键特征也普遍存在于更复杂的BSM扩展中。

Method: 作者选取Two-Higgs-Doublet Model（2HDM）作为研究对象，在次领头阶（NLO）精度下，通过Higgs与Z玻色子的伴随产生过程（associated Higgs production with a Z boson），分析FCC-ee在240 GeV运行时的预期精度如何约束2HDM的alignment极限附近的参数空间。核心关注三类驱动截面偏差的物理机制：新标量态的存在、Higgs混合以及Higgs间非平凡耦合。

Result: 研究表明，FCC-ee在240 GeV运行下的预期精度能够对靠近alignment极限的2HDM Higgs扇区BSM相关相互作用进行精确分析。由于2HDM中驱动截面偏离SM预期的关键因素（新标量存在、Higgs混合、Higgs间耦合）同样存在于比2HDM更复杂的模型中，该结果展示了FCC-ee在电弱尺度附近间接发现BSM物理的潜力。

Conclusion: FCC-ee不仅能够以2HDM等具体模型为基准检验标准模型预测，而且其间接探测能力可以推广到更广泛的BSM框架，预示其在电弱尺度揭示超出标准模型新物理方面的广阔前景。

Abstract: The Future Circular Electron-Positron Collider (FCC-ee) will probe aspects of the Higgs boson and the electroweak scale with unprecedented precision via associated Higgs production with a $Z$ boson. We focus on the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM) to frame the FCC-ee's precision constraints within a small, concrete parameter space at next-to-leading order. We demonstrate that the projected precision of the FCC-ee's 240 GeV run will enable a precise analysis of Beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM)-relevant Higgs-sector interactions near the alignment limit. As the key aspects of the 2HDM that drive deviations of the cross section from its Standard Model expectation (presence of new scalar states, Higgs mixing, and non-trivial inter-Higgs couplings) are also typically present in extensions more complex than the 2HDM, this demonstrates the FCC-ee's indirect potential for unveiling BSM physics around the electroweak scale.

</details>


### [157] [Quasicrystalline Inflation: From the Cosmological Dipole to Primordial Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02142)
*Jiro Soda*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"准晶暴胀"概念：六种准周期相位分解为三个声子和三个相子。相干纯相子位移可产生局域功率偶极子而线性无迹四极子为零。通过空间缓变的二次有效场论，推导出非对角关联，其动量转移被限制为六个二十面体波矢的整数组合。这些关联非对角信号提供了原初准周期序（原初衍射）的特征观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宇宙原初物理中是否存在超越标准暴胀预言之外的准周期序信号，利用准晶体的数学结构（声子-相子分解）为原初宇宙学观测提供新的特征性可检验预言。

Method: 将六个准周期相位分解为三个声子和三个相子；分析纯相子位移的物理效应（产生局域功率偶极子、线性无迹四极子为零）；采用空间缓变的二次有效场论推导非对角关联函数，并约束动量转移必须为六个二十面体波矢的整数组合。

Result: 推导出具有受限动量转移（六个二十面体波矢的整数组合）的非对角关联信号，这些信号可作为原初准周期性（原初衍射）的独特观测特征。

Conclusion: 准晶暴胀为宇宙学提供了新的理论框架，其预测的原初衍射信号可为未来宇宙学观测提供可检验的独特特征。

Abstract: We propose quasicrystalline inflation. Six quasiperiodic phases decompose into three phonons and three phasons. It turns out that a coherent pure-phason displacement can generate a local power dipole while the linear traceless quadrupole vanishes. From spatially varying quadratic effective field theory, we derive off-diagonal correlations whose momentum transfer is restricted to integer combinations of the six icosahedral wavevectors. These correlated off-diagonal signals provide a characteristic observational signature of primordial quasiperiodic order, primordial diffraction.

</details>


### [158] [TQ4Q2.0 Fragmentation Functions for Fully Heavy Tetraquark Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04049)
*Francesco Giovanni Celiberto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过TQ4Q2.0共线碎裂函数集研究了全重四夸克态的产生动力学，覆盖标量、轴矢量和张量三种构型，采用NRQCD因子化确定碎裂输入并通过HF-NRevo方案跨重味阈值演化，系统传播碎裂尺度变化带来的微扰不确定性及非微扰效应，特别关注轴矢量扇区对内禀粲夸克贡献的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全重四夸克态的实验观测与理论理解之间存在差距，需要发展一个能够系统描述其产生机制并量化不确定性的理论框架，以连接奇特强子谱学与质子部分子结构。

Method: 基于NRQCD因子化在完整部分子道集合上确定碎裂函数，通过HF-NRevo方案将碎裂输入跨重味阈值进行演化，同时传播碎裂尺度变化引起的微扰不确定性与色复合长距离矩阵元中编码的非微扰效应。

Result: 建立了覆盖标量(0++)、轴矢量(1+-)和张量(2++)三种构型的TQ4Q2.0碎裂函数集，揭示了轴矢量扇区在LHC和FCC能量下对内禀粲贡献的特殊敏感性。

Conclusion: TQ4Q2.0提供了一个具有不确定性意识的框架，可在广阔运动学范围内探索全重四夸克产生，并桥接奇特强子谱学与质子部分子结构之间的关联。

Abstract: We investigate the fragmentation dynamics underlying the production of fully heavy tetraquarks by means of the TQ4Q2.0 collinear FF set, covering scalar ($0^{++}$), axial-vector ($1^{+-}$), and tensor ($2^{++}$) configurations. The fragmentation inputs are determined within NRQCD factorization for the complete set of relevant partonic channels and are subsequently evolved across heavy-flavor thresholds through the HF-NRevo scheme. Perturbative uncertainties associated with fragmentation-scale variations are consistently propagated together with nonperturbative effects encoded in color-composite long-distance matrix elements. Attention is devoted to the axial-vector sector, whose fragmentation pattern makes it especially sensitive to intrinsic-charm contributions at LHC and FCC energies. TQ4Q2.0 therefore provides an uncertainty-aware framework for exploring fully heavy tetraquark production over a broad kinematic range and for connecting exotic-hadron spectroscopy with the partonic structure of the proton.

</details>


### [159] [Inelastic Signatures of Electroweak Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04144)
*Juri Smirnov,Spencer Griffith,John F. Beacom*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了由Majorana和Dirac多重态耦合构成的极小电弱暗物质模型，可解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）合作组近期报道的高能非弹性核反冲事件。在守衡点，电弱对称性破缺产生了中性态劈裂δ和Z玻色子离对角相互作用。对于系列耦合多重态，劈裂和主导非弹性相互作用在固定(mχ,y)下具有普适性，由此确定了质量依赖的δ_LZ(mχ)以及(mχ,y)平面上的普适区域，并与热遗迹轨迹相交选出每个多重态的基准点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ合作组近期报道了高能非弹性核反冲候选事件，需要一个理论框架来解释这类信号。极小电弱暗物质模型（耦合Majorana和Dirac多重态）提供了一个预测性框架，作者旨在展示该框架可以解释LZ事件的类别，并利用该事件约束模型参数空间。

Method: 作者研究由Majorana（M）和Dirac（D）多重态通过Higgs相互作用耦合的极小电弱暗物质模型。在守衡点（custodial point）下，电弱对称性破缺诱导中性态质量劈裂δ和离对角Z相互作用。对于6对耦合多重态（3_M2_D到13_M12_D），在固定质量mχ和耦合y下，劈裂和主导非弹性截面是表示无关的（普适的）。通过要求LZ曝光中预期一个非弹性事件，确定质量依赖的δ_LZ(mχ)（附90%泊松双侧带），定义普适区域，再与表示依赖的热遗迹轨迹相交选取基准点。

Result: LZ观察到的高反冲能量指向数百keV量级的质量劈裂。通过固定Z介导的速率可确定质量依赖的δ_LZ(mχ)，定义(mχ,y)平面上的普适区域。该区域与表示依赖的热遗迹轨迹相交后，为每个多重态选出一个基准点。

Conclusion: 该框架能为每个电弱表示产生基准点，并进一步预测了较低反冲能处由圈诱导的弹性自旋无关信号，为未来暗物质探测实验（如验证LZ事件与检验该模型）提供了可检验的、具有相关性的预言。

Abstract: Minimal electroweak dark matter (DM) extended by a Majorana (M) and a Dirac (D) multiplet coupled through Higgs interactions provides a predictive framework for high-energy inelastic nuclear recoils. We show that this setup can account for the type of event recently reported by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration. At the custodial point, electroweak symmetry breaking induces a neutral-state splitting $δ$ and an off-diagonal $Z$ interaction. For the coupled multiplets $3_M2_D$, $5_M4_D$, $7_M6_D$, $9_M8_D$, $11_M10_D$, and $13_M12_D$, both the splitting and the leading inelastic interaction are universal at fixed $(m_χ,y)$, independent of the electroweak representation. The observed high recoil energy points to splittings of a few hundred keV, while the fixed $Z$-mediated rate allows us to determine a mass-dependent $δ_{\rm LZ}(m_χ)$ by requiring one expected inelastic event in the LZ exposure, with a two-sided $90\%$ Poisson band. This defines a universal region in the $(m_χ,y)$ plane. Intersecting it with the representation-dependent thermal relic trajectories selects a benchmark for each multiplet. The corresponding electroweak representation then predicts a correlated loop-induced elastic spin-independent signal at lower recoil energies.

</details>


### [160] [TeV Higgsino Interpretation of the LZ High-Recoil Event with Intermediate-Scale Electroweak Gauginos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04163)
*Xiaokang Du,Fei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对LUX-ZEPLIN所报告的高反冲事例，提出了一种亚MeV中性态分裂的TeV级Higgsino超对称模型。通过在SU(5)大统一尺度设定非普适的规范费米子边界条件M1^G/M2^G=-3/5，可抵消双希格斯ino和wino的主要贡献，并在单圈演化下保持到Higgsino能标。在wino质量约120 TeV、能隙350 keV、Higgsino质量1.091 TeV且tanβ=10的参数下可得到受控的树级解。文章进一步识别了能实现所需gaugino比值的混合SU(5)表示，并检验了辐射敏感性与谱一致性。由于非弹性Z耦合几乎未被抑制，全密度热Higgsino仍受已发表的太阳俘获界限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN实验报道的高反冲事例暗示可能存在TeV级Higgsino暗物质，其伴随约keV量级的中性态质量劈裂，以解释非弹性散射信号。然而，在MSSM框架下如此小的能隙在无抵消情形下要求电弱gaugino质量高达约10^7 GeV，远超Higgsino质量量级，这种层次差异需要一个理论上自洽且能自然产生小能隙的构造方案。

Method: 作者在SU(5)大统一能标引入非普适的U(1)与SU(2)规范费米子边界条件M1^G/M2^G=-3/5，该比值在单圈均匀演化下可保持到Higgsino能标，从而精确抵消bino和wino对中性态能隙的领先贡献。剩余的有限贡献通过wino质量约120 TeV、能隙350 keV、Higgsino质量1.091 TeV和tanβ=10实现受控的树级解。作者还通过寻找可实现所需gaugino比值的混合SU(5)表示来验证模型构建的一致性，并分析辐射修正的敏感性及谱的可行性。

Result: 研究证明非普适的SU(5)边界条件M1^G/M2^G=-3/5能够精确消除bino和wino对亚MeV能隙的领先贡献，且该条件在单圈演化下保持稳定。在指定参数点可以得到wino质量约120 TeV、能隙350 keV的自洽解。同时文章指出，由于该模型中非弹性Z耦合并未被压低，全密度的热Higgsino方案仍面临已公开发表的太阳俘获实验限制的严峻考验。

Conclusion: 尽管所提出的构造在理论上能够自然产生亚MeV中性态劈裂并解释LUX-ZEPLIN事例，但该模型在暗物质探测方面仍存在观测约束，特别是太阳俘获界限对全密度Higgsino方案的排除，表明此类解释需要更细致的宇宙学条件或额外机制来规避这些限制。

Abstract: The high-recoil event reported by LUX--ZEPLIN motivates a TeV Higgsino with a sub-MeV neutral-state splitting. Away from cancellations, such a small gap in the MSSM points to electroweak-gaugino masses of order $10^7\,\mathrm{GeV}$, far above the Higgsino mass. We show that the non-universal boundary condition $M_1^G/M_2^G=-3/5$ at the $\mathrm{SU}(5)$ GUT scale cancels the leading bino and wino contributions and is preserved by homogeneous one-loop evolution down to the Higgsino scale. The remaining finite-gaugino terms admit a controlled tree-level solution with a wino mass near $120\TeV$ for a $350\keV$ gap, a $1.091\TeV$ Higgsino and $\tanβ=10$. We identify mixed $\mathrm{SU}(5)$ representations that realize the required gaugino ratio and examine the radiative sensitivity and spectrum consistency of this construction. Because the inelastic $Z$ coupling remains essentially unsuppressed, a full-density thermal Higgsino is still subject to the published solar-capture bounds under their astrophysical and transport assumptions.

</details>


### [161] [Confronting the Higgsino Interpretation of the LZ Event with the High-Energy Sideband](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04175)
*Nicholas L. Rodd,Benjamin R. Safdi,Tracy R. Slatyer,Weishuang Linda Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对LZ实验观测到的单个248 keV核反冲事件，检验了其为热产生1.1 TeV higgsino暗物质非弹性散射解释的可行性。作者发现该解释会在LZ的高能边带产生额外事件，而实验未报告该bin中的事件，暗示可能存在张力。此外，非热产生的~500 GeV higgsino可以避开该约束，未来重靶实验可进一步检验该解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验在一段时间内观测到单个高能核反冲事件，其能量谱特征与非弹性散射暗物质（特别是higgsino）的解释相符。然而，需要检查该解释是否与LZ实验中其他能量区间的零观测一致，以验证该暗物质候选的可行性。

Method: 作者计算了满足热产生1.1 TeV higgsino参数（质量劈裂δ约370-490 keV）时预期在LZ高能S1c边带（sideband）产生的核反冲事件率，并与实验报告的零事件进行比较；同时考察非热产生~500 GeV higgsino场景及其对边带约束的规避能力。

Result: 热产生1.1 TeV higgsino暗物质虽能解释LZ观测到的单事件，但会在高能边带中产生更多事件，与LZ零观测存在潜在张力（因该边带接受度未公开，结论需LZ合作组进一步确认）；而非热~500 GeV higgsino可规避该边带约束。

Conclusion: 热higgsino对LZ单事件的解释处于张力之中，未来使用重靶的实验有望判定higgsino解释的可行性。

Abstract: The LZ experiment has reported a single 248 keV nuclear recoil event in their $2.84$ ton-yr exposure on a xenon target. The absence of lower nuclear recoil events is naturally explained by inelastically scattering dark matter (DM), for which the nuclear recoil energy spectra are shifted upwards from zero. Excitingly, the observed rate and spectra are consistent with a thermal $1.1$ TeV higgsino that explains all of DM with a mass splitting $δ\sim 370 - 490$ keV between the two lowest-lying Majorana states, with the range depending on the underlying DM velocity distribution. In this work, however, we show that higgsino DM with these properties would generically produce more higher-energy recoil events in a high-energy sideband along the LZ $S1c$ energy axis, while the LZ experiment reports no events in that bin. This suggests a possible tension which could be further explored by the LZ Collaboration, as the acceptance in that bin is not public. We further show that a non-thermal $\sim$500 GeV higgsino may evade the sideband constraint and that future experiments using heavy targets can potentially test the higgsino interpretation of the event either way.

</details>


### [162] [Seasonal dark matter from the LUX-ZEPLIN high-energy event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04181)
*Christopher McCabe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的一个高能核反冲事件（248±23 keV），将其解释为非弹性暗物质散射（吸热过程）的信号。拟合表明数据倾向于质量劈裂接近暗物质晕逃逸速度决定的运动学极限的参数区域，此时信号由速度分布尾部的暗物质贡献，导致强烈的年度调制（超过50%，部分参数达100%），使信号在一年中部分时间消失。作者量化了验证这种急剧调制所需的事件数，并指出XLZD和PandaX等更大靶质量的实验可直接检验该解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验在扩展搜索窗口中观测到一个高能核反冲事件（~248 keV），传统弹性暗物质散射难以在这种能量产生信号。作者希望探索非弹性暗物质（散射为吸热过程）是否能自然解释该事件，并评估该解释的可检验性。

Method: 将LZ观测到的单次核反冲事件拟合到两个暗物质态的质量和质量劈裂参数空间，分析该事件在吸热散射框架下的物理含义，并计算由此产生的年度调制信号强度及其统计显著性。

Result: 数据倾向于质量劈裂接近暗物质晕逃逸速度对应的运动学极限；此时信号源于暗物质速度分布的尾部，导致强烈的年度调制（>50%，部分参数达100%，即信号在一年部分时间消失）。作者量化了验证这一特征所需的事件数，并指出XLZD和PandaX等更大实验可直接检验。

Conclusion: 非弹性暗物质解释可将LZ的高能事件与强烈年度调制联系起来，该信号特征与无调制信号截然不同，可通过更大靶质量的实验进行直接检验，为识别该事件的物理起源提供了明确预言。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN experiment has reported a single nuclear recoil at $248\pm23({\rm stat})\pm23({\rm sys})\keV$ in an extended search window. Inelastic dark matter, in which the scattering is endothermic, is a natural mechanism for producing a signal at such high energy. We fit the event to the dark matter mass and the mass splitting between two dark matter states, and find that the data favour a splitting pushed close to the kinematic limit set by the escape speed of the halo. The signal is then supplied by dark matter in the tail of the halo speed distribution. This drives a strongly seasonal signal: annual modulation rises above $50\%$ across the favoured region of parameter space and even reaches $100\%$ for some parameters, implying the signal vanishes for part of the year. Such a strong modulation distinguishes this interpretation sharply from a non-modulating signal, and we quantify the number of events needed to establish it. Experiments with larger target masses than LZ's, notably XLZD and PandaX, will be able to test this directly.

</details>


### [163] [Atmospheric neutrino up-scattering explanation of LZ 2026 excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04185)
*Sk Jeesun,Anirban Majumdar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了LZ实验观测到的一次孤立核反冲事件（能量248±23±23 keV）的新物理解释。与主流观点（银河系暗物质及其重伴随粒子）不同，作者提出大气中微子通过上散射产生超越标准模型的重粒子χ，该机制天然地解释了事件发生在高反冲能量的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验观测到在248±23±23 keV能量处的孤立核反冲事件，标准模型背景估算无法解释该事件。现有文献大多考虑星系晕暗物质及其重伴粒子的解释，作者希望探索其他可能的物理机制来解释这一异常事件。

Method: 提出大气中微子与核子发生上散射（νnuc→χnuc）产生超出标准模型的重粒子χ这一机制。该散射的运动学要求会对反冲能量的低端产生截断，从而可以解释高反冲能量处的孤立事件。该模型可通过惰性中微子模型自然实现，但作者保持分析的一般性，不具体指定χ。通过标量媒介子（质量mφ，耦合yχ,q）识别能够产生此类孤立事件的参数空间区域。

Result: 找到了能够解释该过量事件且同时满足其他现有约束的参数空间。例如，mχ~1 GeV且√(yχyq)/mφ=2×10⁻² GeV⁻¹的参数配置可以满足该事件过量，同时不被其他现有约束排除。

Conclusion: 大气中微子上散射机制为LZ实验的异常孤立事件提供了一个可行的新物理解释，补充了现有的暗物质解释路径，并给出了具体的参数空间区域。

Abstract: The recent observation of an isolated nuclear recoil at $248\pm 23\pm 23$ keV energy by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has motivated the community to look for a new physics explanation, as the Standard model background estimation fails to accomodate that. Most of the existing literature hitherto considers a galactic halo dark matter with a heavier partner. In this work, we traverse the alternate route of atmospheric neutrino ($ν$) up-scattering, thus producing a massive beyond standard model (BSM) particle $χ$. The kinematic requirement of such a scattering poses a cut off in the lower recoil energies providing an explanation of the unique isolated event at such a higher recoil energy. Such up-scattering with the nucleons ($\nuc$), $ν\nuc\to χ\nuc$ can be naturally realized in sterile neutrino models, though we keep our analysis generic without specifying $χ$. We identify the region of parameter space that can produce such an isolated event assuming a scalar mediator with mass $m_φ$ and coupling $y_{χ,q}$. For example, with $m_χ\sim1$ GeV, and $\sqrt{y_χy_q}/m_φ=2 \times 10^{-2}$ GeV$^{-1}$ can satisfy such an excess of events while remaining allowed by other existing constraints as well.

</details>


### [164] [Axion Portal Dark Matter and the LUX-ZEPLIN High-Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04186)
*James Unwin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验报告的一个与248 keV核反冲相容的事件，提出了弹性赝标量-赝标量相互作用L₄的暗物质实现模型，作为对非弹性暗物质解释的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了一个可能与248 keV核反冲相容的事件。虽然初步关注集中在非弹性暗物质上，但这一解释敏感依赖于银河系晕不确定的高速尾部。作者转而寻求基于弹性相互作用的替代解释，以提供更稳健的物理解释。

Method: 论文发展了弹性赝标量-赝标量相互作用L₄=(χ̄iγ⁵χ)(N̄iγ⁵N)的实现模型。该模型主要由狄拉克费米子暗物质构成，并引入一个复标量以产生赝标量介质子。为了维持规范不变性，还要求引入一个多TeV量级的矢量型重夸克来完成UV完备化。文中给出的代表性热冻出模型参数为m_χ=400 GeV、m_a=1 GeV。

Result: 该模型打破了等标量/等矢量假设，其响应级反冲谱介于LZ弹性O₄ᵛ模板与接触L₄ᵛ模板之间，表明局部显著性为2.6-3.2σ。

Conclusion: 该弹性赝标量模型为LZ事件的非弹性暗物质解释提供了可行的替代方案，具有2.6-3.2σ的局部显著性，且其对称性结构不同于常规的等标量/等矢量假设。

Abstract: LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) has reported one event compatible with a $248\,\mathrm{keV}$ nuclear recoil. While initial attention has focused on inelastic dark matter, that interpretation depends sensitively on the uncertain high-speed tail of the Galactic halo. We develop instead an elastic realization of the pseudoscalar-pseudoscalar interaction $L_4=(\barχiγ^5χ)(\bar N iγ^5N)$, whose contact templates give one of the largest local significances identified by LZ. The minimal model presented involves primarily Dirac fermion dark matter, and a complex scalar leading to a pseudoscalar mediator. The minimal gauge-invariant UV completion also requires a single multi-TeV vector-like heavy quark. A representative model which realizes thermal freeze-out has $m_χ=400~{\rm GeV}$ and $m_a=1~{\rm GeV}$. The simplest model breaks from the isoscalar/isovector assumption, and the response-level recoil spectrum lies between LZ's elastic $O_4^v$ and contact $L_4^v$ templates, suggesting a local significance of $2.6$-$3.2σ$.

</details>
