### [165] [Tidally-enhanced resonances in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals: A tertiary path to chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03007)
*Kyriakos Destounis,Takuya Katagiri,Sajal Mukherjee,Kostas D. Kokkotas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究极端质量比旋进系统（EMRIs）在外部绝热变化潮汐环境中的受迫动力学。潮汐形变打破Kerr时空的轴对称性，使得测地线动力学变得不可积并产生混沌运动。作者通过Poincaré映射和旋转曲线表征非可积性，识别出两个随潮汐场振幅增强而增宽的平台（标志着从弱混沌到强混沌的转变），并证明动力学对轨道相对于潮汐场的取向敏感。通过作用-角变量的固有时演化分析，发现与主导可通约性相关的角度组合发生相位锁定，能够诱发运动常数的长期变化。最后讨论了辐射反作用效应驱动EMRIs穿越潮汐岛以及其对引力波探测的含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRIs是探测强场引力和复杂相对论动力学的独特实验室。将外部潮汐环境嵌入考虑后，系统实现了受限但仍具天体物理学意义的相对论三体问题（如活动星系核中的场景）。理解潮汐形变导致的可积性破缺、混沌运动及其对引力波推断的影响是重要的开放问题。

Method: 1) 构建包含外部绝热变化潮汐环境的EMRI模型（恒星质量次级天体绕嵌入潮汐场中的超/非旋转超大质量黑洞运动）；2) 利用Poincaré映射和由径向、极向、方位向束缚运动基频之比构建的旋转曲线表征非可积性和混沌签名；3) 通过作用-角变量的固有时演化分析，识别与主导可通约性相关的角度组合的相位锁定行为。核旋（Lense-Thirring）效应也被包含进轨道取向对潮汐场敏感性的分析中。

Result: 识别出两个突出的平台，其宽度随潮汐场振幅增加而增宽，表明从弱混沌到强混沌的转变。混沌动力学对轨道相对于潮汐场的取向敏感。相位分析显示，与主导可通约性关联的角度组合被相位锁定，使得潮汐贡献能引发运动常数的长期（绝热）变化；而非平台上的角度组合则循环演化。这为潮汐共振提供了新的相空间表征。

Conclusion: 潮汐形变将EMRI从可积系统变为具有明显平台结构的混沌系统，这些平台在动力学上对应于相位锁定的潮汐共振，可能通过辐射反作用效应驱动系统穿越"潮汐岛"，对未来引力波探测器在强场区域的推断精度有重要影响。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) provide a unique laboratory for probing strong-field gravity and complex relativistic dynamics. We study a tidally deformed EMRI composed of a stellar-mass secondary orbiting a supermassive (non-)rotating black hole embedded in an external, adiabatically varying tidal environment. These systems provide a restricted, yet astrophysically motivated, realization of the relativistic three-body problem, expected to appear in active galactic nuclei, with a clear hierarchy of masses and radiation-reaction timescales. The external tidal deformation breaks the axisymmetry of the Kerr spacetime, rendering the geodesic dynamics non-integrable and giving rise to chaotic motion. The resulting signature of non-integrability is characterized through Poincaré maps and rotation curves constructed from the ratios of the fundamental frequencies of bound radial, polar, and azimuthal motion. We identify two prominent plateaus whose widths increase with the tidal field amplitude, signaling a transition from weak to strong chaos. We then demonstrate the sensitivity of the chaotic dynamics to the orientation of the orbit relative to the tidal field. We further analyze the proper-time evolution of the action-angle variables, showing that the angle combinations associated with the dominant commensurabilities are phase locked, thereby allowing the associated tidal contributions to induce secular changes in the constants of motion, whereas off-plateau angle combinations circulate. These results clarify the dynamical significance of the prominent plateaus and provide a novel phase-space characterization of tidal resonances in EMRIs. Finally, we discuss the potential role of radiation-reaction effects in driving EMRIs through tidal island crossings and the implications for gravitational-wave inference with future detectors.

</details>


### [166] [Excitation factors and exceptional points of Kerr-de Sitter quasinormal modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03009)
*Dario Rossi,Naritaka Oshita,Leonardo Gualtieri,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了Kerr-de Sitter黑洞拟正规模的激发因子，使用了两种技术：Heun函数表示和超几何函数展开。发现奇异点是Kerr-de Sitter拟正规模谱的普遍特征，在奇异点附近模式表现出"磁滞现象"，且单个模式的激发因子绝对值显著增强，同时相位几乎相反。外推到小宇宙学常数时与Kerr极限结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kerr-de Sitter黑洞拟正规模的激发因子，特别是奇异点附近的行为，以理解宇宙学常数存在时黑洞微扰的谱特性及其与Kerr极限的联系。

Method: 采用两种独立技术计算激发因子：(1) Heun函数表示法；(2) 将解展开为超几何函数。通过比较两种方法验证计算结果，并分析奇异点附近模式的激发因子行为。

Result: 发现奇异点是Kerr-de Sitter拟正规模谱的普遍特征；在奇异点附近观察到此前仅在Kerr黑洞受有质量场扰动时发现的"磁滞现象"；激发因子绝对值显著增强且相位近乎相反；外推到小宇宙学常数极限时与Kerr结果一致。

Conclusion: Kerr-de Sitter拟正规模在奇异点附近展示出与Kerr极限类似但通常模式相关的复杂行为，本文两种计算方法相互验证，并为小宇宙学常数外推提供了可靠性支持。

Abstract: We compute the excitation factors of Kerr-de Sitter black hole quasinormal modes using two different techniques: a Heun function representation and an expansion of the solution in terms of hypergeometric functions. Exceptional points at which the complex frequencies of different overtones coincide are a generic feature of the Kerr-de Sitter quasinormal mode spectrum. Near exceptional points, the modes exhibit a "hysteresis phenomenon" that was previously found for Kerr black holes perturbed by massive fields and the absolute value of the excitation factors of the individual modes is significantly enhanced, while they acquire nearly opposite phases. We extrapolate the excitation factors to small values of the cosmological constant and find good agreement with previous calculations in the Kerr limit.

</details>


### [167] [DANSur_HM: Modularly incorporating higher modes in a deep learning based gravitational-wave surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03025)
*Osvaldo Gramaxo Freitas,Anastasios Theodoropoulos,Nino Villanueva,José A. Font,Antonio Onofre,Alejandro Torres-Forné,José D. Martin-Guerrero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于神经网络训练的快速代理模型DANSur_HM，用于生成双黑洞并合引力波波形，涵盖基波(2,2)模及(3,3)、(2,1)、(4,4)、(3,2)、(4,3)和(5,5)等高阶模式。通过近似数据的预训练和GPU并行化投影技术，模型平均失配阶数达10^-4量级，吞吐量超过6×10^5波形/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值相对论(NR)模拟能最忠实表征双黑洞并合过程中的引力波，但在引力波天文中，参数估计等任务需要每秒大量波形评估且覆盖整个参数空间，完全依赖NR模拟在计算上不可行，因此需要开发高效且精确的代理模型来替代完整NR模拟。

Method: 在前序工作基础上，采用神经网络训练快速代理模型。具体策略包括：(1) 在NR数据上进行微调前，先使用近似(approximant)数据做预训练步骤，以平滑参数空间；(2) 利用GPU并行化能力，在训练期间将NR波形投影到倾角-相位(ι, φ)球面上，从而能够同时拟合所有被探索的模式。

Result: 所开发的代理模型命名为DANSur_HM，平均失配阶数为10^-4量级，最差失配为2.5×10^-3；在NVIDIA V100 GPU上吞吐量超过6×10^5波形/秒。参数估计测试确认了包含高阶模式的有效性。

Conclusion: 该神经网络代理模型在保持高保真度的同时实现了极高的波形生成速度，显著提升了计算效率，且高阶模式的加入对参数估计有实用价值，可用于引力波天文中处理大规模波形评估需求。

Abstract: Numerical relativity (NR) simulations provide the most faithful representation of the gravitational waves (GWs) emitted by binary black hole (BBH) systems during merger. In the context of GW astronomy, tasks such as parameter estimation can require vast numbers of waveform evaluations per second across the entire parameter space. Since performing full NR simulations for each evaluation is not computationally feasible, interpolating methods for existing NR waveforms, known as surrogate models, have been developed with marked success. In this paper, we build on our previous work to introduce methods to train a fast surrogate model based on neural networks in order to generate BBH merger waveforms, including the fundamental (2,2) mode, as well as the (3,3), (2,1), (4,4), (3,2), (4,3) and (5,5) higher-order modes. Applying a pretraining step on approximant data before fine-tuning on NR data allows us to smooth out the parameter space, and making use of the parallelization ability of GPUs to project the NR waveforms in the inclination-phase $(ι, φ)$ sphere during training allows the fitting of all the explored modes simultaneously. The developed surrogate model, \texttt{DANSur\_HM}, achieves average mismatches of the order of $10^{-4}$, with the worst mismatch at $2.5\times10^{-3}$, and achieves throughput above $6\times10^5$ waveforms/second on an NVIDIA V100 GPU. Parameter estimation tests confirm the usefulness of the inclusion of higher modes.

</details>


### [168] [Scalar quasinormal modes of Kerr--AdS$_{\bf 7}$ via accessory parameter expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03037)
*Julián Barragán Amado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有三个独立旋转参数的七维Kerr-AdS黑洞的标量微扰。通过对Klein-Gordon方程（分离为一个径向和两个角向二阶线性ODE，均含五个正则奇点）应用Hill行列式方法，计算了两种参数区间下的辅助参数解析展开式，这些展开式与四维N=2箭形规范理论的Nekrasov-Shatashvili函数的瞬子部分一致，并用于推导慢旋转极限下的角特征值微扰表达式和小黑洞极限下的准正则模频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维旋转黑洞的标量微扰及其谱性质，特别是通过量子场论方法（NS函数）与黑洞谱理论的对应关系，获得角特征值和准正则模的解析展开，加深对高维黑洞动力学和AdS/CFT对应关系的理解。

Method: 将七维Kerr-AdS黑洞的Klein-Gordon方程分离为具有五个正则奇点的二阶线性ODE，应用Hill行列式方法计算辅助参数的解析展开；利用NS函数瞬子部分验证两种参数区间的展开一致性；基于展开式推导角特征值（慢旋转极限）与准正则模频率（小黑洞极限）的微扰表达式。

Result: 获得了两种参数区间下辅助参数的解析展开，且这些展开与四维N=2箭形规范理论NS函数的瞬子部分完全一致；进一步得到了慢旋转极限下角特征值的微扰表达式以及小黑洞极限下准正则模频率的表达式。

Conclusion: 该研究建立并验证了七维多旋转Kerr-AdS黑洞微扰谱与四维N=2超对称规范理论之间的精确对应关系，拓展了黑洞-量子场论对应（即可积系统中的量子/separation of variables对应）到更高维、多旋转参数的情形，为高维黑洞微扰提供了新的解析工具。

Abstract: We study scalar perturbations of a seven-dimensional Kerr--AdS black hole with three independent rotation parameters. In this geometry, the Klein--Gordon equation separates into one radial and two angular second-order linear ordinary differential equations, each with five regular singular points. We apply the Hill determinant method to compute analytic expansions of the accessory parameters in two different parameter regimes. The resulting accessory parameter expansions coincide with those obtained from the instanton part of the Nekrasov--Shatashvili function of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ quiver gauge theories. These expansions are then used to derive perturbative expressions for the angular eigenvalues in the slowly rotating limit and for the quasinormal mode frequencies in the small black hole limit.

</details>


### [169] [Gravitational Waves and Matter Constraints in Gravity from Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03082)
*David S. Pereira*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"引力来自熵"(GfE)理论在一般FLRW（弗里德曼-勒梅特-罗伯逊-沃尔克）背景下的张量传播病态：真空、对称继承的单多重态扇区及代数理想流体本构嵌入中，严格主TT系统仅在满足 Ḣ=K/a² 时有非零实特征值，偏离该轨迹则时间主根变为复数，表明存在高频双曲性障碍；同时秩定理表明单一基础多重态无法提供消除该问题所需的各向同性双矢响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GfE理论作为从熵原理推导引力的替代框架，其动力学传播特性（尤其是双曲性和因果性）尚未被充分检验。理解其在宇宙学背景（如FLRW）上的张量模式传播行为对理论的有效性和预测能力至关重要。

Method: 采用严格主符号（principal symbol）TT系统分析，通过计算FLRW背景中真空及给定扇区的实特征值条件，结合秩定理评估特定多重态能否提供修复病态所需的各向同性双矢响应。

Result: 发现非零实特征值仅在 Ḣ=K/a²（宇宙学常数与空间曲率关系约束）这一特殊轨迹上存在；在其他区域时间主根为复数，说明系统丧失高频率段的双曲性。秩定理进一步排除了单一基础多重态通过各向同性双矢响应修复该问题的可能性。

Conclusion: GfE在一般FLRW背景上面临根本性的张量传播病态，且在最自然的对称性假设下无法通过单一多重特加以修补，暗示该理论在宇宙学尺度上的动力学自洽性存在严重局限。

Abstract: We show that Gravity from Entropy (GfE) exhibits a tensor-propagation pathology on generic FLRW backgrounds. In vacuum, in the symmetry-inheriting single-multiplet sector, and for the algebraic perfect-fluid constitutive embedding, the strict-principal TT system has a nonzero real characteristic only when \(\dot H=K/a^2\). Away from this locus the temporal principal roots become complex, signaling a high-frequency hyperbolicity obstruction. Under the stated isotropy assumptions, a rank theorem further shows that one foundational multiplet cannot provide the isotropic bivector response needed to remove this problem.

</details>


### [170] [Relativistic transformation of temperature for exotic cosmological fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03124)
*Soroor Pouryazdan,Babak Vakili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在协变热力学框架下研究负压有效宇宙学流体的温度洛伦兹变换，推导了重子流体（p=wρ）和（广义）Chaplygin气体（p=-A/ρ^α）的温度变换表达式，揭示了负压对相对论温度增长行为的抑制效应以及Chaplygin气体因压力依赖密度而产生的独特温度-速度单调增长关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在澄清负压效应对宇宙学流体温度相对论变换的影响。传统研究多关注无压或正压流体的温度变换，而对具有负压特征的暗能量或暗流体模型（如Chaplygin气体）在相对论热力学中的温度行为缺乏系统分析，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 采用协变热力学框架，对两类负压流体分别推导温度洛伦兹变换：(1) 状态方程为p=wρ的负压重子流体（常数负w）；(2) 密度依赖压力的广义Chaplygin气体（p=-A/ρ^α）。通过解析推导并结合代表性数值示例，分析变换后温度对相对速度与背景能量密度的依赖关系。

Result: 对于负压重子流体，负的方程态参数会抑制温度在相对论增生下的增长幅度；而Chaplygin气体由于压力随密度变化，展现出不同的定性行为——变换后温度随速度单调增加。文中通过代表性算例展示了相对论温度对相对速度和背景能量密度的依赖特征。

Conclusion: 负压流体的状态方程形式深刻影响其温度的相对论变换行为：常数负压比（重子流体）削弱温度增长，而密度依赖负压（Chaplygin气体）则维持温度的单调递增趋势。该结果对理解宇宙学流体的热力学性质及其相对论效应具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the relativistic transformation of temperature for effective cosmological fluids with negative pressure within a covariant thermodynamic framework. The Lorentz transformation of temperature is derived for barotropic fluids with $p=wρ$ and for the (generalized) Chaplygin gas with $p=-A/ρ^α$. For barotropic fluids with constant negative equation-of-state parameter, we show that negative pressure suppresses the growth of temperature under relativistic boosts. In contrast, the Chaplygin gas exhibits a qualitatively different behavior due to its density-dependent pressure, leading to a monotonic increase of the transformed temperature with velocity. The dependence of the relativistic temperature on the relative velocity and background energy density is illustrated through representative examples.

</details>


### [171] [Non-extensive entropy signatures in compact star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03237)
*B. N. Jayawiguna,M. H. Al Ghifari,A. Rohim,A. Sulaksono*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非广延熵模型（Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto和Rényi形式）如何通过Wald熵形式与推广的f(R)引力理论相联系，并探讨了在MIT袋模型描述的夸克星构型下，这些修正引力框架是否能满足与HESS J1731-347和GW190814相关致密天体的观测约束。结果表明所有研究的非广延熵模型均满足有效能量条件和声速稳定性判据，说明奇异致密天体为探索非广延熵模型提供了可信的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者旨在将黑洞视界的非广延熵模型与广义f(R)引力理论中的Wald熵形式建立联系，并通过夸克星这一致密天体平台来检验这些理论模型的物理可行性。此举可同时验证非广延熵模型在修改引力框架中的适用性和相关致密天体构型的观测一致性。

Method: 采用MIT袋模型描述夸克星的物质状态方程，在f(R)修改引力框架下求解恒星结构方程；通过Wald熵形式将非广延熵模型（Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto、Rényi）与f(R)引力参数关联起来；将所得恒星配置与HESS J1731-347和GW190814的观测质量-半径上下限进行对比，并检验有效能量条件和声速平方来评估物理合理性及稳定性。

Result: 在修改引力框架下，由MIT袋模型描述的夸克星构型成功满足HESS J1731-347和GW190814相关联的致密天体的观测上限与下限约束。所有研究的非广延熵模型（Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto和Rényi）均满足有效能量条件和声速稳定性条件，表明这些恒星配置在物理上自洽且稳定。

Conclusion: 奇异致密天体（夸克星）为探索非广延熵模型（如Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto和Rényi形式）提供了引人注目且可信的物理平台；同时非广延熵模型与f(R)引力之间的对应关系能够反过来为这些致密天体构型提供新的理论视角和约束。

Abstract: The non-extensive entropy models applied to the black-hole horizon are connected to the generalized Einstein--Hilbert action, $f(R)$, via the Wald entropy formalism. We demonstrate that quark star configurations characterized by the MIT bag model under this modified gravity approach conform to the observational lower and upper bounds established for compact objects linked to HESS J1731$-$347 and GW190814. Furthermore, we assess the effective energy conditions and corresponding speed of sound to evaluate the physical plausibility and stability of the stellar configurations. All non-extensive entropy models studied satisfied these conditions. Our findings show that the exotic compact objects offer a compelling and credible framework for exploring aspects of non-extensive entropy models, such as the Barrow, Tsallis-Cirto, and Rényi formalisms, and vice versa.

</details>


### [172] [Constraint closure and gravitational-wave content of shear-free cosmologies in metric f(R) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03260)
*Mudhahir Al Ajmi,Amare Abebe*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了度规f(R)引力中FLRW宇宙学线性无剪切扰动的自洽性条件。研究发现无剪切约束的散度恰为总动量守恒方程，其投影时间导数提供非平凡可积性条件。无剪切性消去了独立的横迹无迹电磁Weyl对，因此不存在普通张量引力波；标量子（scalaron）未被代数消除，但其四变量谐波系统必须处于由无剪切约束及其时间导数生成的一致子空间中。对于de Sitter空外区域中的测地无剪切同余，排除了所有非零固定共动标量谐波，包括健康的R+αR²-2Λ模型。矢量模服从曲率依赖的全局本征值条件，在正密度物质分支上无情非零矢量谐波存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无剪切（shear-free）扰动的传播内容在f(R)引力中的一致性，尤其关注标量、矢量和张量扇区是否分别存在非平凡的物理可传播模式，以及张量引力波在无剪切条件下是否被消除。

Method: 从无剪切约束推导其散度和投影时间导数，分别对应总动量守恒方程和可积性条件。将扰动分为标量、矢量、张量三个扇区并逐一检验。利用约束的微分生成性来确定标量谐波系统的一致性子空间，并通过本征值条件分析矢量谐波的存活可能性。

Result: 无剪切性消除了独立的横迹无迹（TT）电磁Weyl张量对，因此不存在标准的+或×极化张量引力波。标量子未被代数移除，但由于de Sitter外空patch中测地无剪切同余的约束闭包条件，R+αR²-2Λ模型的非零固定共动标量谐波被排除。矢量模服从曲率依赖的全局本征值条件，当f'>0、1+w>0的正密度物质下该条件右端为负，故无矢量谐波存活；R³模型因f'<0分支而规避此障碍。

Conclusion: 张量辐射在无剪切扇区中完全缺失；标量辐射未被运动学排除，但能否存活取决于模型与背景的约束闭包条件，是一个依赖于具体模型的开放问题。

Abstract: We derive the consistency conditions governing the propagating content of linear shear-free perturbations of Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker cosmologies in metric $f(R)$ gravity. The divergence of the shear-free constraint is shown to be the total momentum-conservation equation and therefore does not imply geodesic flow. Its projected time derivative instead supplies a nontrivial integrability condition. Scalar, vector, and tensor sectors must then be tested separately. Shear-freeness removes the independent transverse-traceless electric-magnetic Weyl pair, so no ordinary $+$ or $\times$ tensor gravitational wave survives. The scalaron is not removed algebraically, but its four-variable harmonic system must remain in the time-dependent consistent subspace generated by the shear-free constraint and all of its time derivatives. For a geodesic shear-free congruence in the spatially flat expanding de Sitter patch, this excludes every nonzero fixed comoving scalar harmonic, including the healthy $R+αR^2-2Λ$ model with $α,Λ>0$. Vector modes obey a curvature-dependent global eigenvalue condition whose right-hand side is negative for positive-density matter with $f'>0$ and $1+w>0$; hence no nonzero vector harmonic survives on that branch. The familiar coasting $R^3$ example evades this sign obstruction only because it lies on an $f'<0$ branch. Thus tensor radiation is absent in the imposed shear-free sector, whereas scalar radiation is not excluded kinematically but remains a model- and background-dependent constraint-closure question.

</details>


### [173] [Tidal forces in the quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03278)
*Anuar Idrissov,Hernando Quevedo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子Oppenheimer-Snyder黑洞中径向自由落体大质量粒子所受的潮汐力。该黑洞是圈量子引力对Schwarzschild时空的修正，描述反弹尘埃球的外部几何。研究表明，潮汐张量的径向和角向分量在远处与Schwarzschild情形一致，但在内部符号反转；粒子从静止释放不会到达中心，而是在Cauchy视界内遇到转向点。在反弹作用下，测地偏离矢量始终有限，不会像Schwarzschild情形那样发散，这一正则性源于反弹而非规则核，且只保护类时径向坠入而不影响类光径向测地线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Schwarzschild黑洞的潮汐力在奇点处发散，导致落入其中的粒子被无限拉伸。圈量子引力修正的量子Oppenheimer-Snyder黑洞声称消除了奇点并产生反弹，本文旨在检验该修正几何中潮汐力的行为，探究量子效应如何改变坠入粒子的潮汐体验，以及这种正则性是否源自反弹机制本身。

Method: 采用适应于自由落体观测者的正交标架（orthonormal tetrad），计算潮汐张量的径向与角向分量。求解在两组不同初始条件下的测地偏离方程，分析径向偏离矢量的演化规律。系统扫描整个量子参数范围，考察参数符号与大小对潮汐结构的影响。

Result: 发现：(1) 潮汐张量在远处还原Schwarzschild结果，内部符号反转；(2) 角向零点局限于Cauchy视界与事件视界之间，仅在极值极限下与后者重合；(3) 粒子在Cauchy视界内的转向点停止，该处潮汐分量之比与解参数无关，仅由有效源的横向状态方程决定；(4) 径向偏离矢量在度规函数极小值处达到最大值，始终有限，不像Schwarzschild情形在奇点发散；(5) 正则性源于反弹而非规则核，只保护类时径向坠入，类光径向测地线不受影响；(6) 量子参数符号决定潮汐结构是否存在，其大小决定该结构是否被隐藏，超极值时几何变为无视界，反弹及整个变形历史暴露于远处观测者。

Conclusion: 量子Oppenheimer-Snyder黑洞通过反弹机制消除了潮汐奇点，使径向坠入的大质量粒子在穿越过程中经历有限的潮汐力，且不会到达中心。这种正则性选择性地保护类时坠入，暗示量子引力效应在潮汐层面呈现的结构依赖于量子参数的符号与大小。

Abstract: We investigate the tidal forces experienced by massive particles in radial free fall in the quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder black hole, a loop quantum gravity correction to the Schwarzschild geometry that describes the exterior of a bouncing dust ball. Using an orthonormal tetrad adapted to a freely falling observer, we show that the radial and angular components of the tidal tensor reproduce their Schwarzschild counterparts at large distances but reverse sign in the interior. The angular zero remains confined between the Cauchy and event horizons and coincides with the latter only in the extremal limit. A particle released from rest does not reach the center, it stops at a turning point inside the Cauchy horizon, where the ratio of the tidal components is independent of the parameters of the solution and is fixed by the transverse equation of state of the effective source. Solving the geodesic deviation equations for two sets of initial conditions, we find that the radial deviation vector attains its maximum at the minimum of the metric function, exactly for one set of initial conditions and asymptotically for the other, and remains finite throughout, in contrast with the Schwarzschild case, in which it diverges at the singularity. This regularity originates in the bounce rather than in a regular core, and therefore protects timelike radial infall while leaving radial null geodesics unaffected. Examining the full range of the quantum parameter, we find that its sign determines whether the tidal sector possesses any of this structure, while its magnitude determines only whether that structure is hidden: beyond the extremal value the geometry becomes horizonless, and the entire deformation history, bounce included, is exposed to distant observers.

</details>


### [174] [Introducing SINFONIA: Symplectic, slimplectic and Magnusian (Neural) Flows for Orbital Numerical Integration and Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03329)
*Lidia J. Gomes Da Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出三类结构保持的神经流架构（SINFONIA-J0/J1/J2），将有限时间演化映射学习为显式可微对象并反复复合以完成完整的引力波旋进模拟。实验表明，长期精度由映射误差在能量-角动量平衡确定的单一长期通道上的带符号投影主导。编码该结构后，学习映射在10^2至10^5窗口复合下仍保持精度，链式相位误差比基准slimplectic积分器低数个数量级，且该长期结构可被利用于物理推断（恢复未被建模的动力学摩擦力）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时引力波建模需要同时解析快速轨道运动与慢耗散演化，同时防止小数值误差累积为长期相位漂移。作者试图直接学习有限时间演化映射本身，作为显式、可微且结构保持的对象，并反复复合以通过整个旋进过程。

Method: 构造三种神经流架构：(1) SINFONIA-J0：在Galley加倍相空间上构建辛流与slimplectic流；(2) SINFONIA-J1：Taylor锚定的流；(3) SINFONIA-J2：在相互作用绘景中学习有限时间耗散修正的Magnusian流。应用于2.5PN中子星旋进，通过网络关闭控制隔离学习贡献与内置解析结构的影响。

Result: 三种架构均揭示同一控制机制：长期精度不单独由逐点映射误差决定，而是由其在能量-角动量平衡固定的单一长期通道上的带符号投影决定。编码此结构后，学习映射可在10^2-10^5窗口复合中保持精确到并合，时间步长可达一个完整轨道周期甚至更大，链式相位误差比slimplectic积分器低数个数量级且计算成本更低。当长期通道不设约束时，累积相位可恢复未被建模的动力摩擦力（参数化及作为分离函数的泛函学习均可）。

Conclusion: 这项工作为结构保持的学习演化映射建立了概念验证，证明其可用于引力波源建模中的快速长时积分与物理推断，其关键洞见是长期精度由演化映射在能量-角动量平衡决定的唯一长期通道上的投影控制而非逐点误差。

Abstract: Long-duration gravitational-wave modelling must resolve fast orbital motion together with slow dissipative evolution while preventing small numerical errors from accumulating into secular phase drift. Here we ask whether the finite-time evolution map itself can be learned as an explicit, differentiable, structure-preserving object and then repeatedly composed through a complete inspiral. We construct three neural-flow architectures: a symplectic and slimplectic flow on Galley's doubled phase space, [SINFONIA-J0]; a Taylor-anchored flow, [SINFONIA-J1]; and a Magnusian flow that learns the finite-time dissipative correction in the interaction picture, [SINFONIA-J2]. Applied to a 2.5PN neutron-star inspiral, all three expose the same controlling mechanism: long-time accuracy is governed not by pointwise map error alone, but by its signed projection onto a single secular channel fixed by energy--angular-momentum balance. Encoding this structure allows the learned maps to remain accurate through $10^{2}$--$10^{5}$ window compositions to coalescence at timesteps of a full orbital period and beyond, reaching chained phase errors orders of magnitude below a benchmark slimplectic integrator at lower cost. The same secular structure can also be exploited for physics inference: when the channel is left unconstrained, the accumulated phase retains enough information to recover an un-modelled dynamical-friction-like force, both parametrically and as a learned function of separation. Network-off controls isolate the contribution of learning from the analytic structure already built into each map. These results establish a proof of concept for structure-preserving learned evolution maps as tools for fast long-duration integration and physics inference in gravitational-wave source modelling.

</details>


### [175] [Solutions for neutron stars in General Relativity from a complexity structure scalar boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03393)
*Robert S. Bogadi,Megandhren Govender,Genly Leon,Andronikos Paliathanasis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在球对称各向异性流体的边界处引入了一个涉及复杂度因子（Riemann张量正交分解产生的结构标量）的新条件，由此生成物理合理的模型，并发现复杂度标量在表面处存在非零正的最小值约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者旨在探索复杂度的边界约束条件，而非仅在内部施加约束，以生成物理合理的引力模型，并检验“零复杂度”假设在强引力场中紧凑天体边界处的适用性。

Method: 在球对称各向异性流体的边界（表面）处施加由复杂度因子生成的新条件，不额外引入几何或物质变量的约束，从而构建一个自发生成的天体物理模型。

Result: 生成的模型物理上合理，并表明在表面处复杂度标量存在一个非零且为正的最小值约束。这表明“零复杂度”作为一个全局性约束在边界处并不适用，尤其对于强引力区域中的紧凑天体。

Conclusion: 复杂度因子的边界条件揭示了紧凑天体在强引力背景下不能简单地要求零复杂度，边界处存在固有的最小复杂度下限，这对理解致密星体的内部结构和引力模型的可行性具有启发性意义。

Abstract: A new condition involving the complexity factor, a structure scalar arising from the orthogonal splitting of the Riemann tensor, is generated at the boundary of a spherically symmetric anisotropic fluid. This condition leads to the generation of a model, without invoking any further constraints on geometry or matter variables. The model generated is physically reasonable and suggests that there is a non-zero, positive minimum constraint on the complexity scalar value evaluated at the surface. This suggests that vanishing complexity is a globalized constraint which does not apply at the boundary, specifically for compact objects in the strong-gravity regime.

</details>


### [176] [Dynamical Selection of Horizon-BMS Goldstone Modes in Evaporating Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03477)
*Nihar Ranjan Ghosh,Malay K. Nandy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了蒸发中的Vaidya-Schwarzschild黑洞时空中，与视界附近BMS超平移相关的软自由度的动力学。作者直接从爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量推导出动态超平移参数的有效作用量，揭示了Goldstone扇区与黑洞质量演化之间的双向耦合。关键发现是方位角周期性和极点正则性导出了依赖于质量的"选择规则"，使视界支持的Goldstone模式谱随蒸发而演化——视界收缩时仅保留低阶模式，实现软扇区的"动力学过滤"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞蒸发过程中视界软自由度（与近视界BMS对称性相关）如何随时间演化，并建立宏观视界演化与微观引力软自由度组织之间的直接联系。

Method: 在蒸发Vaidya-Schwarzschild黑洞背景下，将超平移参数视为动态变量，直接从爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量出发推导其有效作用量。结合方位角周期性与极点正则性条件，对角度扇区施加物理约束，导出依赖于黑洞质量的方位角模式选择规则。

Result: 发现Goldstone场与黑洞质量动力学之间存在固有双向耦合：质量函数进入Goldstone模的演化，同时Goldstone构型反馈影响黑洞质量的动态演化。质量依赖的选择规则使得视界在蒸发（收缩）过程中逐步失去高阶模式，仅保留递减的低阶Goldstone模式集，即蒸发诱导了视界软扇区的动力学过滤。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个有效的引力框架，将近视界BMS对称性、动态Goldstone模式与黑洞蒸发联系起来，表明视界质量演化自然地过滤其软自由度谱，暗示了宏观视界演化与视界上引力软自由度的微观组织之间的直接关联。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of horizon soft degrees of freedom associated with near-horizon BMS supertranslations in an evaporating Vaidya-Schwarzschild black hole spacetime. Considering the dynamic nature of the supertranslation parameter, we derive its effective action directly from the Einstein-Hilbert action. The resulting Goldstone sector is intrinsically coupled to the evolving black hole dynamics, with the mass function entering the evolution of the Goldstone modes while the Goldstone configuration contributes to the dynamical evolution of the black hole mass. Azimuthal periodicity and regularity at the poles select the physically admissible angular sector and yields a mass-dependent $selection\ rule$ on the azimuthal modes. Consequently, the spectrum of horizon-supported Goldstone modes evolves with the black hole mass; as the horizon shrinks during evaporation, the horizon is left with a progressively reduced set of lower-order modes. The evaporation thus induces a dynamical filtering of the horizon soft sector, arising intrinsically from the black hole mass dynamics. These results provide an effective gravitational framework linking near-horizon BMS symmetry, dynamical Goldstone modes, and black hole evaporation, suggesting a direct connection between macroscopic horizon evolution with the microscopic organization of the gravitational soft degrees of freedom residing on the horizon.

</details>


### [177] [Long-lived ringing of Bronnikov--Kim brane-world black holes and wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03683)
*Milena Skvortsova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Bronnikov–Kim有效膜世界几何中（包括黑洞和虫洞两侧）有质量标量场扰动形成的长寿命准正规振荡现象。作者发现，在第二Bronnikov–Kim族的黑洞中，虽然阻尼率变得极小，但实频率保持有限（标准准共振模式），并非先前报道的实频率趋于零的行为；而第一、第二族的代表性虫洞则展现出日益长寿命的有质量标量模式，其中第二族虫洞在大质量极限下阻尼率相对无质量情形降低超过四倍。因此，膜世界中稳健的效应是长寿命有质量场振荡，而实频率的消失则是依赖度量或分支的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于膜世界黑洞的研究报道了一种不寻常的极限行为——随着标量场质量增大，真实振荡频率趋于零。作者希望探究这种现象在Bronnikov–Kim有效膜世界几何族（同时涵盖黑洞和虫洞分支）中是否具有普遍性，以厘清长寿命准正规振荡与频率消失现象之间的区别及其适用边界。

Method: 采用两种互补的数值方法：(1) 对黑洞侧使用Leaver连分数方法——需先将第二Bronnikov–Kim族的波动方程约化为有理形式；(2) 对规则虫洞喉部使用Chebyshev伪谱配点法。通过这两套独立的计算框架对第一和第二Bronnikov–Kim族的黑洞与虫洞进行全面比较。

Result: (1) 第二Bronnikov–Kim族黑洞表现出标准的准共振模式：标量分支的阻尼率变得非常小，但实频率保持有限，未重现实频率趋于零的行为。(2) 第一和第二Bronnikov–Kim族的代表性虫洞均发展出日益长寿命的有质量标量模式。(3) 将第二族虫洞扫描扩展到更大场质量后，阻尼相对无质量情形降低超过四倍。

Conclusion: 在膜世界紧致物体几何中，稳健且具普遍性的效应是：有质量标量场在黑洞和虫洞背景上均能形成长寿命准正规振荡。而所谓的"实频率随质量增长趋于零"这一现象并非普遍特征，而是依赖于具体度规或分支（黑洞/虫洞）的个别行为。

Abstract: Massive-field perturbations on black-hole and wormhole backgrounds can form long-lived quasinormal ringing; this behaviour is not confined to brane-world spacetimes, but it is also not universal across compact-object geometries. Recent work on a brane-world black hole reported an unusual limiting behaviour in which the real oscillation frequency tends to zero as the scalar mass grows. We test how general this phenomenon is in the Bronnikov--Kim effective brane-world geometries, treating both the black-hole and wormhole sides of the same families. The comparison uses two complementary numerical treatments: Leaver's continued-fraction method for the black-hole side, after reducing the wave equation for the second Bronnikov--Kim family to rational form, and Chebyshev pseudospectral collocation for regular wormhole throats. The black hole of the second Bronnikov--Kim family shows the standard quasi-resonant pattern: the damping rates of the scalar branches considered here become very small, but the real frequencies remain finite. Representative wormholes from the first and second Bronnikov--Kim families also develop increasingly long-lived massive scalar modes, and extending the second-family wormhole scan to larger field mass reduces the damping by more than a factor of four relative to the massless case. Thus the robust brane-world effect found here is the appearance of long-lived massive-field ringing in both black holes and wormholes, whereas the vanishing of the real frequency is metric- or branch-dependent.

</details>


### [178] [Not All Resonances Are Created Equal: Prioritizing Tidal Resonances in EMRIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03732)
*Béatrice Bonga,Patrick Bourg,Bram ten Brink,H. A.,Peters*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统中由邻近致密天体引起的潮汐共振"踢击"进行了系统性调查研究，在弱潮汐三体层级假设下，覆盖了整个EMRI参数空间，计算了共振轮廓、持续时间以及角动量跳变幅度等关键量，构建了用于波形建模的共振重要性排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI中的潮汐扰动可能由邻近致密天体（如恒星质量黑洞、中子星等）引起，这些扰动可以在共振处对轨道产生"踢击"效应，在LISA频段引力波形中留下潜在可探测的相位偏移。然而此前缺乏对这类潮汐共振的系统性认识，需要全面调查其在不同EMRI参数下的行为与重要性。

Method: 采用弱潮汐、三体层级近似模型，将扰动天体视为在共振时标上静止不动。对于一般的Kerr轨道，系统计算了：(i) 每个(n,k,m)共振在(p,e,x)参数空间中的共振轮廓；(ii) 相应的共振持续时间；(iii) 角动量L_z和Carter常数Q的跳变幅度，其中Δt由平稳模型中的时间平移对称性保证为零。将跳变幅度与持续时间结合，构建了用于波形建模的共振重要性排序。

Result: 完成了对EMRI潮汐共振的系统性分类与量化：所有共振轮廓、持续时间和跳变数据均公开可用。研究发现即使温和的共振也可能通过影响后续共振进入时的相位而产生重要作用，因此不能仅根据跳变幅度大小来忽略任何共振。

Conclusion: 该工作为LISA波段的EMRI引力波建模提供了一个完整的潮汐共振谱图，使得在实际波形建模中可以优先关注最重要的共振，同时提醒模型构建者不可忽视较小共振的累积相位效应。

Abstract: Tidal perturbations from nearby compact objects can drive resonant "kicks" in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), imprinting potentially detectable phase shifts in LISA-band gravitational waveforms. We provide a systematic survey of these tidal resonances across EMRI parameter space in the weak-tide, three-body hierarchy, treating the perturber as stationary on the resonance timescale. For generic Kerr orbits we compute (i) resonance contours in $(p,e,x)$ that locate where each $(n,k,m)$ resonance is encountered, (ii) the associated resonance duration, and (iii) the jump amplitudes in the angular momentum $L_z$ and the Carter constant $Q$ (with $ΔE$ vanishing by time-translation symmetry in the stationary model). Combining jump amplitudes with resonance durations, we construct a practical ranking of the resonances most relevant for waveform modeling, while emphasizing that even modest resonances may still be important through their influence on the phase at which subsequent resonances are entered. All contour and jump data are publicly available.

</details>


### [179] [On the interplay between waveform systematics and lensing signatures in gravitational-wave signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03935)
*Disha Hegde,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究通过对类似GW231123事件的数值相对论注入模拟，检验了波形容貌建模系统误差是否会造成表面上的引力透镜证据。发现透镜自由度能在某些情况下减少波形差异并产生非零的透镜支持，但该行为并非系统性的，且未观察到所有波形与透镜模型一致支持透镜并同时降低波形差异的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW231123引力波事件表现出异常高的总质量和接近极端旋转，且不同波形容貌推断出的源性质差异显著。后续研究倾向于透镜解释并声称可减小波形系统误差，这引出一个关键问题：表观透镜证据是否可能源自波形容貌建模的系统误差？

Method: 采用类似GW231123的数值相对论（NR）注入模拟方法，系统地评估透镜自由度对波形差异和透镜后验支持的影响，并比较不同波形模型与透镜模型在所有注入案例中的表现。

Result: 透镜自由度可以在某些注入案例中减少波形差异并产生不可忽略的透镜支持；但这种行为并非在所有注入中系统出现，且不存在所有波形和透镜模型同时支持透镜并减少波形差异的注入案例。

Conclusion: GW231123事件中观察到的表观透镜证据可能部分源于波形容貌建模系统误差，但该效应不具有系统性；实际透镜解释的证据强度需要更谨慎评估，因为纯波形容貌系统误差难以在所有模型中一致性产生透镜迹象。

Abstract: The exceptional gravitational-wave event GW231123 exhibited unusually high total mass and near-extremal spins, while revealing strong waveform-dependent variations in its inferred source properties. Several follow-up studies favored a lensed interpretation, with reduced waveform systematics, raising the question of whether apparent lensing evidence could arise from waveform modeling systematics. Using GW231123-like numerical-relativity injections, we find that lensing degrees of freedom can reduce waveform discrepancies and yield non-negligible lensing support. However, this behavior is not systematic across injections, and cases where all waveform and lens models favor lensing while reducing waveform discrepancies are not found.

</details>


### [180] [Eight Local Couplings of Gravitational Waves from Unified Field Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03979)
*Hong-Bo Jin,Yue-Liang Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析了引力波探测中记录的局部耦合线性混合，指出真空广义相对论仅允许两个横迹无迹（TT）振幅，而通过E(2)小群分类的应变振幅张量决定电动潮汐张量。研究表明螺旋度为±1的洛伦兹混合产生引力磁（GEM）场，该场不进入潮汐张量，但时间变化的螺旋度±1流可源出GEM波进入混合。通过统一场方程阐明Pvec各分量来源，并利用每个耦合的测量量来隔离极化混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在确定引力波探测器能够记录的最大耦合集合，判断仅以张量臂长结果是否能把广义相对论与其他引力理论区分开来，并促进对独特极化的识别和模型检验。

Method: 利用E(2)小群对零四动量的分类来分类应变幅度p=(p+,p×,px,py,pb,pℓ)，分析洛伦兹混合在螺旋度±1下产生的引力磁场（GEM），并采用统一场方程定义八种耦合Pvec=(p,βg⊥)，通过辐射区波的延迟时间依赖性简化分析。

Result: 确定了八种耦合的完整集合，其中每个耦合的测量量可以隔离对应的极化分量，从而有助于识别不同极化和进行模型测试。GEM场虽然不进入电动潮汐张量，但时间变化的螺旋度±1流可源的GEM波可以作为耦合进入混合体系。

Conclusion: 通过统一场方程明确了Pvec中各分量的来源，每个耦合的测量量能够隔离极化混合中的各极化态，这有利于区分不同的引力和极化的识别以及进行理论模型的检验。

Abstract: A gravitational-wave (GW) detector records a linear mixture of local couplings under the assumption that extra polarizations enter geodesic deviation. Vacuum general relativity (GR) admits two transverse-traceless (TT) amplitudes. It remains to determine the largest set of couplings that can sit in that mixture, and whether a tensor-only arm-length result selects GR uniquely. The little group \(E(2)\) of a null four-momentum classifies the strain amplitudes \(\mathbf{p}=(p_{+},p_{\times},p_{x},p_{y},p_{b},p_{\ell})\), commonly written \(h_{P}\), which determine the electric tidal tensor along a ray. Geodesic deviation, recorded as differential arm length, therefore contains only those \(p_{P}\) that enter that tensor. Lorentz mixing at helicity \(\pm1\) supplies a gravito-magnetic (GEM) field that does not enter the tidal tensor. if GEM field is static, that does not propagate as a wave. A time-varying helicity-\(\pm1\) current sources a GEM wave that enters the mixture as a coupling, to be isolated by its measured quantity. For a radiation-zone wave that depends only on retarded time, \(\betag_{\perp}=\hat{\mathbf{k}}\times\partial_{t}(p_{x},p_{y})\), and the eight couplings are \(\Pvec=(\mathbf{p},\betag_{\perp})\). Here we adopt unified field equations on \(\mathbf{p}\) to clarify the origin of each component of \(\Pvec\); the measured quantity of each coupling then isolates the polarizations in that mixture, which favors identification of distinct polarizations and model tests.

</details>


### [181] [Black holes in alternative theories of gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03983)
*Jose Luis Blázquez-Salcedo,Fech Scen Khoo,Burkhard Kleihaus,Jutta Kunz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了黑洞在超越广义相对论的引力理论中作为检验平台的重要性，重点分析了标量-张量理论中黑洞解与广义相对论中Schwarzschild和Kerr解的差异，以及这些差异的观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论虽然在众多方面得到验证，但在强引力区域（如黑洞附近）可能存在偏差。为检验超出广义相对论的引力理论，黑洞提供了一个重要的天然实验室。标量-张量理论中，标量场与高阶曲率项的耦合会产生不同于广义相对论的黑洞解，研究这些解的特征有助于约束或验证这些替代理论。

Method: 本文聚焦于标量-张量理论中黑洞解的导出与分析，通过对比这些解与广义相对论的Schwarzschild和Kerr解的差异，并研究其不稳定性、阴影（黑洞成像）和引力波谱等可观测特征来表征理论的有效性。

Result: 证明了标量-张量理论中黑洞解与广义相对论的标准解存在显著差异，这些差异体现在解的结构、稳定性行为以及可观测的阴影和引力波信号特征上。这些特征可以作为约束理论耦合参数的观测工具。

Conclusion: 黑洞的强引力场特性使得它们成为检验广义相对论之外引力理论的关键测试场。通过研究替代理论中黑洞解的独特性质——如不稳定性和可观测特征（阴影形状、引力波谱），可以有效地限制标量-张量理论的耦合参数范围。

Abstract: Black holes, with their strong gravitational fields, provide an important testing ground for theories of gravity beyond General Relativity. Among the many proposed alternatives, considerable recent work has focused on scalar-tensor theories in which a scalar field couples to higher-curvature terms. The black hole solutions that arise in such theories can differ significantly from the Schwarzschild and Kerr solutions of General Relativity. Characteristic properties of these black holes include instabilities, shadows, and gravitational wave spectra, which can be used to constrain the couplings of the underlying theories.

</details>


### [182] [An off-shell conformally invariant Galilean Weyl tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04053)
*Quentin Vigneron,Philip K. Schwartz,James Read*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出伽利略几何中Weyl张量的显式共形不变离壳定义，并给出其电部分与磁部分的定义。作者主张磁部分为零需要具有特定运动学性质的观测者存在，而这一条件应作为伽利略不变理论被称为"牛顿式"的必要条件（延续Trautman精神）。附带结果：仅由伽利略结构与Coriolis场即可唯一构造伽利略boost不变联络，无需额外质量规范场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在牛顿-卡特（Newton-Cartan）引力及其推广中，Weyl张量及其电/磁分解的离壳（off-shell）定义尚不明确，尤其是保持共形不变性的表述缺失。此外，判断一个伽利略不变理论何时真正具有"牛顿式"动力学特征（如观测者感知到的磁场消失）缺乏严格判据。

Method: 从伽利略几何结构出发，构造显式共形不变且离壳有效的Weyl张量定义；随后通过运动学观测者分解（electric/magnetic decomposition）定义其电磁两部分。分析磁部分为零所需的观测者运动学条件，并与牛顿-卡特方程及Trautman"牛顿式"条件进行对比。文中还通过几何构造证明了一个唯一性定理：伽利略结构与Coriolis场足以唯一确定boost不变联络，且不要求时钟形式（clock form）闭。

Result: 成功给出了满足共形不变性与离壳可定义性的Galilean Weyl张量，及其电磁分解。证明磁部分为零需要特殊运动学性质的观测者，该性质在牛顿-卡特理论中自动满足，但并非所有伽利略不变理论都如此。同时证明了伽利略boost不变联络的唯一存在性结论，无需引入质量规范场（mass gauge field）这一额外结构。

Conclusion: 作者提出将"存在使离壳磁部分为零的观测者"作为伽利略不变理论被称为"牛顿式"的必要条件，作为对Trautman原始牛顿式条件的推广与补充。附带结果则表明经典构造boost不变联络时引入的质量规范场并非本质必需，从而简化了伽利略几何的结构预设。

Abstract: We propose a manifestly conformally invariant off-shell definition of the Weyl tensor in Galilean geometry. We also propose definitions for the electric and magnetic parts thereof. For the latter to vanish, observers with specific kinematical properties need to exist. While this is guaranteed by the Newton--Cartan equation, it might not be true for other Galilean invariant theories. Therefore, we propose that imposing the existence of observers for which the off-shell magnetic part is zero should be a necessary condition for a Galilean invariant theory to be called `Newtonian', in the spirit of the `Newtonian' condition, introduced by Trautman, in standard Newton--Cartan gravity.
  As a side result, we also show that there exists a unique Galilean boost-invariant connection that can be built from a Galilean structure and a choice of Coriolis field, even when the clock form is not closed. No extra structure is needed, contrasting with the standard approach used to construct boost-invariant connections which introduces a mass gauge field.

</details>


### [183] [Dyonic rotating cosmological black hole surrounded by quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04111)
*M. D. de Oliveira,Alexandre G. M. Schmidt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个带电荷的旋转宇宙学黑洞度量，该黑洞存在于具有五重态暗能量的背景下，通过Newman-Janis算法引入旋转，并分析了其视界、曲率标量、能动张量及能量条件等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在构建一个旋转带电黑洞的广义相对论度规解，该解同时包含宇宙学常数和五重态暗能量的贡献，以更真实地描述宇宙背景下黑洞的物理性质，并消除以往工作中在引入旋转时可能出现的歧义。

Method: 从Schwarzschild-type度量出发，考虑f(r)函数中所有能量贡献（包括电/磁电荷、宇宙学常数和五重态参数），通过Newman-Janis算法并采用唯一且通用的复化规则引入旋转。随后假设度量满足含物质的Einstein场方程，计算了事件视界条件、能动张量Tμν、Kretschmann标量和Ricci标量。

Result: 论文分析了宇宙学常数Λ和五重态参数α对事件视界和能层的影响。发现当α→0时，标量曲率不等于真空结果（R≠-4Λ），而奇点区域保持不变。此外还考察了测试粒子的角速度和转动速度以及能量密度和压力方面的能量条件。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地刻画了在五重态暗能量背景下旋转带电（电磁双荷）黑洞的几何与物理性质，并讨论了其存在所需满足的能量条件，为理解暗能量环境下黑洞解提供了新的度量框架。

Abstract: In this work, we constructed a new metric describing a rotating cosmological black hole with electric and magnetic charges in the presence of quintessence dark energy, where the cosmological constant and quintessence are associated with external matter. Starting from a Schwarzschild-type metric and considering all energy contributions in the function $f(r)$, we introduced rotation through the Newman--Janis algorithm using a unique and general complexification rule, avoiding possible ambiguities. Assuming that the metric satisfies the Einstein field equations with matter, we calculate the event horizon condition, the total stress--energy tensor $T_{μν}$, and the Kretschmann and Ricci scalars. We also analyze the influence of the cosmological constant $Λ$ and quintessence parameter $α$ on the event horizon and ergosphere. In the limit $α\rightarrow 0$, the scalar curvature differs from the vacuum result, $R \neq -4Λ$, while the singularity region remains unchanged. Finally, we investigate the angular and rotational velocities of a test particle, as well as the energy conditions, through the energy density and pressures required for the existence of this black hole solution.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [A Galois connection between subalgebras and tensor subcategories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04073)
*Kenichi Shimizu,Harshit Yadav*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究辫子有限张量范畴中的伽罗瓦对偶。设 B 为辫子有限张量范畴，A 为其上的简单交换代数，构造了 A 的子代数与 B_A 的张量子范畴之间的反序伽罗瓦连接，并给出闭包算子与格的反对同构的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在张量范畴理论中，子代数与子范畴之间的对偶关系是理解量子对称性、模块范畴结构以及量子群论中伽罗瓦对应的重要工具。本文旨在建立辫子有限张量范畴中简单交换代数的子代数与其表示范畴张量子范畴之间的一般性伽罗瓦连接框架。

Method: 构造了从 A 的子代数到 B_A 的张量子范畴的映射及反向映射，利用 Müger 中心 B' 和局部张量子范畴 B_A^{loc} 定义闭包算子，证明二者构成反序伽罗瓦连接，进而得到固定点区间的格反对同构。随后将框架推广到有限张量范畴的中心代数（Drinfeld 中心中的典型代数），并讨论非退化情形下 Frobenius 扩张与单模张量子范畴的对应，以及对 Hopf 代数的特化。

Result: 建立了反序伽罗瓦连接，证明闭子代数恰为包含 A' 的，闭张量子范畴恰为包含 B_A^{loc} 的；区间 [A',A] 与 [B_A^{loc}, B_A] 作为格反对同构。对有限张量范畴 C 的中心典型代数给出了量子对称化版本；非退化 B 情形下 Frobenius 扩张对应单模张量子范畴；Hopf 代数情形给出 Hopf 理想与正规左余理想的序保持双射。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了此前关于伽罗瓦对应、子范畴分类与量子群对偶的若干结果，为辫子范畴与有限张量范畴的结构分析提供了系统的框架。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{B}$ be a braided finite tensor category and let $A$ be a simple commutative algebra in $\mathcal{B}$. We construct an order-reversing Galois connection between subalgebras of $A$ and tensor subcategories of $\mathcal{B}_A$. Let $\mathcal{B}'$ denote the Müger center and set $A':=A\cap\mathcal{B}'$. The closure operators are $B\mapsto\langle B,A'\rangle_{\mathrm{alg}}$ and $\mathcal{E}\mapsto\langle\mathcal{E},\mathcal{B}_A^{\mathrm{loc}}\rangle_{\otimes}$. Thus the closed subalgebras are those containing $A'$, while the closed tensor subcategories are those containing $\mathcal{B}_A^{\mathrm{loc}}$; equivalently, the fixed-point intervals $[A',A]_{\mathrm{alg}}$ and $[\mathcal{B}_A^{\mathrm{loc}},\mathcal{B}_A]_{\otimes}$ are anti-isomorphic as lattices.
  For a finite tensor category $\mathcal{C}$, the canonical algebra in $\mathcal{Z}(\mathcal{C})$ yields an anti-isomorphism between its subalgebras and tensor subcategories of $\mathcal{C}$. When $\mathcal{B}$ is nondegenerate, Frobenius extensions correspond to unimodular tensor subcategories. For a Hopf algebra in $\mathcal{B}$, the correspondence specializes to an order-preserving bijection between Hopf ideals and normal left coideal subalgebras.

</details>
